### [16] BlitzGS: City-Scale Gaussian Splatting at Lightning Speed  
- **⏳发布**：2026-05-13  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Zhongtao Wang, Huishan Au, Yilong Li et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13794v1)    
- **📝摘要**：We present BlitzGS, a distributed 3DGS framework that reduces active Gaussian workload for fast city-scale reconstruction. BlitzGS manages this workload at three coupled levels. At the system level, the framework shards Gaussians across GPUs by index parity rather than spatial blocks. This approach mitigates the cross-block visibility redundancy inherent in spatial partitioning. Furthermore, it distributes each rendering step through a single cross-GPU exchange that routes projected Gaussians to their tile owners. At the model level, scheduled importance-scoring passes shrink the global Gaussian population. During these passes, the framework generates a per-Gaussian visibility weight to bias density-control updates toward contributing primitives and a per-view importance mask for the view-level renderer. At the view level, BlitzGS trims each camera's active set with a distance-based LOD gate to exclude excessively fine primitives for the current frustum and the importance-based culling mask to skip Gaussians with negligible cross-view contribution.   On large-scale benchmarks, BlitzGS matches the rendering quality of recent large-scale baselines while delivering an order-of-magnitude speedup, training city-scale scenes in tens of minutes. Our code is available at https: //github.com/AkierRaee/BlitzGS.  
- **📝翻译**: 我们介绍BlitzGS，一个分布式3DGS框架，能够降低主动高斯工作负载，实现快速城市级重建。BlitzGS在三个耦合层面管理这些工作负载。在系统层面，该框架通过索引奇偶校验而非空间块，将高斯分布分片到各个GPU上。这种方法缓解了空间划分中固有的跨块可见性冗余问题。此外，它通过单一跨GPU交换将每个渲染步骤分配到其图块所有者。在模型层面，计划的重要性评分通过会减少全球高斯分布。在这些处理过程中，框架生成一个按高斯可视性权重，将密度控制更新偏向贡献的原语，并为视野级渲染器提供每视图重要性掩码。在视角层面，BlitzGS通过基于距离的LOD门裁剪每个摄像头的活动集，以排除当前视锥体中过于细微的图元，并使用基于重要性的剔除掩码跳过高斯图，且其交叉视角贡献可忽略不计。  在大规模基准测试中，BlitzGS的渲染质量可媲美近期大型基线，同时实现数量级的加速，数十分钟内完成城市级场景的训练。我们的代码可在 https： //github.com/AkierRaee/BlitzGS 获取。  

### [17] Sparse Code Uplifting for Efficient 3D Language Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2026-05-13  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Lovre Antonio Budimir, Yushi Guan, Steve Ryhner, Sven Lončarić, Nandita Vijaykumar  
- **📝说明**：18 pages (9 pages main paper), 10 figures, preprint  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13600v1)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Language Gaussian Splatting (3DLGS) augments 3D Gaussian Splatting with language-aligned visual features for open-vocabulary 3D scene understanding. A core challenge is efficiently associating high-dimensional vision-language embeddings with millions of 3D Gaussians while preserving efficient feature rendering for text-based querying. Existing methods either store dense features directly on Gaussians, causing high storage costs and slow rendering, or learn compact representations through expensive per-scene optimization with repeated feature rasterization. No existing method simultaneously achieves fast 3D semantic reconstruction, efficient storage, and fast rendering. We propose SCOUP (Sparse COde UPlifting), which addresses all three by decoupling language representation learning from 3D Gaussian optimization. Rather than working directly in 3D, we learn sparse codebook-based representations entirely using features associated with 2D image regions, associating each region with a sparse set of codebook coefficients. We then uplift these coefficients to 3D Gaussians with our weighted sparse aggregation using Gaussian-to-pixel associations, where each Gaussian accumulates coefficients over codebook atoms across views. Top-$K$ filtering then extracts the most dominant multi-view coefficients per Gaussian, enabling efficient storage and fast rendering. Our method achieves up to $400\times$ training speedup while being $3\times$ more memory efficient during training compared to the state-of-the-art in rendering speed. Across multiple benchmarks, SCOUP matches or outperforms existing methods in open-vocabulary querying accuracy.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D语言高斯喷溅（3DLGS）通过语言对齐的视觉特征增强3D高斯喷溅，实现开放词汇3D场景理解。核心挑战之一是如何在高效地将高维视觉语言嵌入与数百万个3D高斯分布关联，同时保持文本查询的高效特征渲染。现有方法要么直接将密集特征存储在高斯分布上，导致高存储成本和渲染缓慢，要么通过重复特征栅格化的昂贵每场景优化学习紧凑的表示。目前没有任何方法能同时实现快速的三维语义重建、高效的存储和快速渲染。我们提出了SCOUP（稀疏COde提升），通过将语言表示学习与三维高斯优化解耦来解决这三者。我们不直接在三维中工作，而是完全利用与二维图像区域相关的特征学习基于稀疏码本的表示，并将每个区域与一套稀疏的码本系数相关联。然后，我们利用加权稀疏聚合，利用高斯到像素的关联，将这些系数提升为三维高斯，每个高斯在多个视图中累积码本原子的系数。顶$K美元过滤随后提取每个高斯图中最主导的多视图系数，实现高效的存储和快速渲染。我们的方法在训练过程中提升了高达400美元/倍倍的增长，同时在训练期间的内存效率比最先进的渲染速度高出3倍/倍。在多个基准测试中，SCOUP在开放词汇查询准确性方面与现有方法相当甚至优于。  

### [18] Z-Order Transformer for Feed-Forward Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2026-05-13  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Can Wang, Lei Liu, Wei Jiang, Dong Xu  
- **📝说明**：Accept by CVPR 2026, Oral  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13465v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Recent advances in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have enabled significant progress in photorealistic novel view synthesis. However, traditional 3DGS relies on a slow, iterative optimization process, which limits its use in scenarios demanding real-time results. To overcome this bottleneck, recent feed-forward methods aim to predict Gaussian attributes directly from images, but they often struggle with the redundancy of Gaussian primitives and rendering quality. In this work, we introduce a transformer-based architecture specifically designed for feed-forward Gaussian Splatting. Our key insight is that spatial and semantic relationships among Gaussians can be effectively captured through a sparse attention mechanism, enabled by a Z-order strategy that organizes the unstructured Gaussian set into a spatially coherent sequence. Furthermore, we incorporate this Z-order strategy to adaptively suppress redundancy while preserving critical structural details. This allows the transformer to efficiently model context, compress Gaussian primitives, and predict Gaussian attributes in a single forward pass. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves fast and high-quality novel view synthesis with fewer Gaussian primitives.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）的最新进展推动了照片级真实新颖视图合成的显著进展。然而，传统的3DGS依赖于缓慢且迭代的优化过程，限制了其在需要实时结果的场景中的应用。为克服这一瓶颈，近期的前馈方法旨在直接从图像预测高斯属性，但它们常常面临高斯原语冗余和渲染质量的困难。在本研究中，我们介绍了一种专门为前馈高斯喷溅设计的基于变换器的架构。我们的关键见解是，高斯分布组之间的空间和语义关系可以通过稀疏的注意力机制有效捕捉，这一机制通过Z序策略将无结构的高斯集合组织成空间相干的序列。此外，我们采用了这种Z序策略，在保持关键结构细节的同时，自适应地抑制冗余。这使得变换器能够高效地建模上下文、压缩高斯原语，并在一次前向传递中预测高斯属性。全面的实验表明，我们的方法能够以更少的高斯原语实现快速且高质量的新颖视图合成。  

### [19] HarmoGS: Robust 3D Gaussian Splatting in the Wild via Conflict-Aware Gradient Harmonization  
- **⏳发布**：2026-05-13（更新：2026-05-15）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Yulei Kang, Tianze Zhu, Jian-Fang Hu, Jianhuang Lai, Wei-Shi Zheng  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13073v2)    
- **📝摘要**：In-the-wild 3D Gaussian Splatting remains challenging due to transient distractors and illumination-induced cross-view appearance inconsistencies. Existing methods mainly rely on image-level masking to suppress unreliable supervision, but masking alone cannot fully eliminate residual occlusions or resolve illumination-induced inconsistencies, both of which can introduce conflicting cross-view gradients. These unresolved conflicts may destabilize Gaussian optimization and lead to visible reconstruction artifacts. We propose a conflict-aware 3DGS framework that addresses this problem from both image-space supervision and gradient-level optimization. Semantic Consistency-Guided Masking learns pixel-wise consistency scores to adaptively refine prior masks and suppress unreliable supervision before gradient formation. A dual-view Conflict-Aware Gradient Harmonization strategy further reconciles view-specific gradients by mutually rotating them into an orthogonal configuration, reducing negative directional interference across views. We also introduce conflict-aware densification and pruning to stabilize Gaussian growth and remove persistently conflicting primitives. Extensive experiments on standard in-the-wild benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art rendering quality under complex transient distractors and cross-view inconsistencies.  
- **📝翻译**: 由于瞬态干扰和照明引起的交叉视图不一致，实际3D高斯喷溅依然具有挑战性。现有方法主要依赖图像级遮蔽来抑制不可靠的监督，但仅靠遮蔽无法完全消除残留遮挡或解决照明引起的不一致性，这两者都可能引入冲突的交叉视角梯度。这些未解决的冲突可能使高斯优化不稳定，并导致可见的重建伪影。我们提出了一个冲突感知的3DGS框架，从图像空间监督和梯度级优化角度解决该问题。语义一致性引导掩蔽学习像素一致性评分，以适应性地优化先前的掩码，并在梯度形成前抑制不可靠的监督。双视角冲突感知梯度协调策略通过相互旋转视角特有梯度形成正交配置，进一步协调视角特有的梯度，减少视角间的负向干扰。我们还引入了冲突感知的密集化和修剪，以稳定高斯增长并消除持续冲突的原节点。在标准的野外基准测试上进行了大量实验，表明我们的方法在复杂的瞬态干扰和交叉视角不一致下，能够实现最先进的渲染质量。  

### [20] 3DGS$^3$: Joint Super Sampling and Frame Interpolation for Real-Time Large-Scale 3DGS Rendering  
- **⏳发布**：2026-05-12  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Yibo Zhao, Fan Gao, Youcheng Cai, Ligang Liu  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11489v1)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables high-quality real-time 3D rendering but faces challenges in efficiently scaling to ultra-dense scenes and high-resolution due to computational bottlenecks that limit its use in latency-sensitive applications. Instead of optimizing the splatting pipeline itself, we propose \textbf{3DGS$^3$}, a unified post-rendering framework that jointly performs super sampling and frame interpolation through differentiable processing of low-resolution outputs to achieve both high-resolution and high-frame-rate rendering. Our \textbf{Gradient\- \-Aware Super Sampling (GASS)} module leverages the continuous differentiability of 3DGS to extract image gradients that guide a GRU-based refinement network to enable high-fidelity super sampling. Furthermore, a \textbf{Lightweight Temporal Frame Interpolation (LTFI)} module based on a compact U-Net-like backbone fuses temporal and differentiable spatial cues from consecutive frames to synthesize temporally coherent intermediate frames. Experiments on public datasets demonstrate that 3DGS$^3$ achieves superior rendering efficiency and visual quality when compared with state-of-the-art methods and remains compatible with existing 3DGS acceleration techniques. The code will be publicly released upon acceptance.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）支持高质量的实时3D渲染，但由于计算瓶颈限制其在延迟敏感应用中的应用，在高效扩展到超密集场景和高分辨率方面面临挑战。我们不优化喷涂流水线本身，而是提出 \textbf{3DGS$^3$}，一个统一的后期渲染框架，通过对低分辨率输出进行可微分处理，联合执行超采样和帧插值，实现高分辨率和高帧率渲染。我们的\textbf{梯度\- \-感知超级采样（GASS）}模块利用3DGS的连续微分性提取图像梯度，指导基于GRU的精细网络，实现高精度超采样。此外，基于紧凑型U-Net类骨干的\textbf{轻量级时间帧插值（LTFI）}模块融合了连续帧的时间和可微空间线索，合成时间上相干的中间帧。在公共数据集上的实验表明，与最先进的方法相比，3DGS$^3$在渲染效率和视觉质量上更优，并且与现有的3DGS加速技术保持兼容。代码一旦被接受，将公开发布。  

### [21] PD-4DGS:Progressive Decomposition of 4D Gaussian Splatting for Bandwidth-Adaptive Dynamic Scene Streaming  
- **⏳发布**：2026-05-12  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Jiachen Li, Guangzhi Han, Jin Wan et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11427v1)    
- **📝摘要**：4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS) enables high-quality dynamic novel view synthesis, yet current models remain monolithic bitstreams that clients must download in full before any frame can be rendered, causing black-screen waits of tens to hundreds of seconds on mobile bandwidth and leaving 4DGS incompatible with modern adaptive-bitrate delivery. Progressive 3DGS compression alleviates this for static scenes, but it acts only on spatial anchors and cannot partition the temporal deformation networks that dominate dynamic-scene size. We present PD-4DGS, the first framework for progressive compression and on-demand transmission of 4DGS. Hierarchical Deformation Decomposition (HDD) externalises the coarse-to-fine motion hierarchy already latent in 4DGS into three independently transmittable layers -- a static scaffold, a global deformation, and a local refinement -- so that any prefix of the bitstream is already renderable, turning a single training run into a scalable, DASH/HLS-compatible bitstream. A Gaussian-entropy attribute rate-distortion loss together with a temporal mask consistency regulariser shrink the base layer while suppressing low-bitrate flicker; a capacity-weighted rollout schedule, gated online by a learnt activation rate rho, then prevents deformation-network under-training without any per-scene hyperparameter. On the Dycheck iPhone benchmark, PD-4DGS cuts the streamed bitstream by >60% at matched rendering fidelity and reduces first-frame latency from 73--930 s to ~1.7 s on a 2 Mbps link, uniquely enabling true on-demand progressive streaming for 4DGS.  
- **📝翻译**: 4D高斯拼图（4DGS）实现了高质量的动态新颖视图合成，但当前模型仍是单体比特流，客户端必须完整下载才能渲染任何帧，导致移动带宽下黑屏等待数十到数百秒，使4DGS与现代自适应码率传输不兼容。渐进式3DGS压缩缓解了静态场景的问题，但它仅作用于空间锚点，无法分割主导动态场景规模的时间变形网络。我们介绍PD-4DGS，这是首个用于渐进压缩和按需传输的4DGS框架。分层变形分解（HDD）将4DGS中已潜伏的粗细运动层级外部化为三个独立可传输的层级——静态支架、全局变形和局部细化——使得任何比特流前缀都已可渲染，将单次训练运行转化为可扩展、兼容DASH/HLS的比特流。高斯熵属性失真损失加上时间遮罩一致性正则化器可缩小基层，同时抑制低码率闪烁;一个以能力加权的推广计划为门槛，通过学习的激活率RHO进行在线限制，从而防止在没有每场景超参数的情况下出现变形网络训练不足的情况。在Dycheck iPhone基准测试中，PD-4DGS在匹配渲染保真度下将流式比特流减少>60%，并将首帧延迟从73-930秒降至2 Mbps链路上的~1.7秒，独一无二地实现了4DGS的真正点播渐进式流媒体。  

### [22] MAGS-SLAM: Monocular Multi-Agent Gaussian Splatting SLAM for Geometrically and Photometrically Consistent Reconstruction  
- **⏳发布**：2026-05-11  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Zhihao Cao, Qi Shao, Shuhao Zhai et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10760v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Collaborative photorealistic 3D reconstruction from multiple agents enables rapid large-scale scene capture for virtual production and cooperative multi-robot exploration. While recent 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) SLAM algorithms can generate high-fidelity real-time mapping, most of the existing multi-agent Gaussian SLAM methods still rely on RGB-D sensors to obtain metric depth and simplify cross-agent alignment, which limits the deployment on lightweight, low-cost, or power-constrained robotic platforms. To address this challenge, we propose MAGS-SLAM, the first RGB-only multi-agent 3DGS SLAM framework for collaborative scene reconstruction. Each agent independently builds local monocular Gaussian submaps and transmits compact submap summaries rather than raw observations or dense maps. To facilitate robust collaboration in the presence of monocular scale ambiguity, our framework integrates compact submap communication, geometry- and appearance-aware loop verification, and occupancy-aware Gaussian fusion, enabling coherent global reconstruction without active depth sensors. We further introduce ReplicaMultiagent Plus benchmark for evaluating collaborative Gaussian SLAM. Intensive experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets show that MAGS-SLAM achieves competitive tracking accuracy and comparable or superior rendering quality to state-of-the-art RGB-D collaborative Gaussian SLAM methods while relying only RGB images.  
- **📝翻译**: 多智能体协作逼真的3D重建实现了大规模场景快速捕捉，支持虚拟制作和多机器人协作探索。虽然最新的3D高斯喷涂（3DGS）SLAM算法能够生成高精度的实时映射，但大多数现有多代理高斯SLAM方法仍依赖RGB-D传感器来获得度量深度并简化跨代理对齐，这限制了在轻量级、低成本或功耗限制型机器人平台上的部署。为应对这一挑战，我们提出了MAGS-SLAM，这是首个仅支持RGB的多智能体3DGS SLAM协作场景重建框架。每个代理独立构建局部单眼高斯子映射，传输的是紧凑的子映射摘要，而非原始观测值或密集映射。为了在单眼尺度模糊性存在下促进稳健协作，我们的框架集成了紧凑的子地图通信、几何和外观感知的环验证以及占用感知的高斯融合，实现了无主动深度传感器的连贯全局重建。我们还进一步介绍了ReplicaMultiagent Plus基准测试，用于评估协作高斯SLAM。对合成和现实世界数据集的密集实验表明，MAGS-SLAM在仅依赖RGB图像的情况下，能够实现竞争性的跟踪精度和与最先进的RGB-D协作高斯SLAM方法相当甚至更优的渲染质量。  

### [23] CAGS: Color-Adaptive Volumetric Video Streaming with Dynamic 3D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2026-05-10  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Daheng Yin, Yili Jin, Jianxin Shi et al.  
- **📝说明**：SIGGRAPH 2026 Conference Paper. Code is available at https://github.com/yindaheng98/ColorAdaptiveGaussianSplatting  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09279v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Volumetric video (VV) streaming enables real-time, immersive access to remote 3D environments, powering telepresence, ecological monitoring, and robotic teleoperation. These applications turn VV streaming into a real-time interface to remote physical environments, imposing new system-level demands for photorealistic scene representation, low-latency interaction, and robust performance under heterogeneous networks. 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has been widely used for real-time photorealistic rendering, offering superior visual quality and rendering performance, but it faces challenges due to bandwidth consumption. Furthermore, as the foundation of adaptive VV streaming, existing Levels of Detail (LoD) methods based on density are not well-suited to Gaussian representations, leading to visible gaps and severe quality degradation. Recent studies have also explored attribute compression techniques to reduce bandwidth consumption. Our preliminary studies reveal that aggressive attribute compression primarily causes color distortion, which can be effectively corrected in the rendered image using a reference image. Motivated by these findings, we propose a novel Color-Adaptive scheme for adaptive VV streaming that uses vector quantization (VQ) to establish LoDs and correct color distortions with low-resolution reference images. We further present CAGS, an adaptive VV streaming system compatible with diverse Gaussian representations, which integrates the Color-Adaptive scheme by rendering reference images on the streaming server and performing color restoration on the client. Extensive experiments on our prototype system demonstrate that CAGS outperforms the existing adaptive streaming systems in PSNR by 5$\sim$20 dB under fluctuating bandwidth, operates significantly faster than existing scalable Gaussian compression methods, and generalizes across different Gaussian representations.  
- **📝翻译**: 体积视频（VV）流媒体实现了对远程3D环境的实时沉浸式访问，支持远程临场、生态监测和机器人远程操作。这些应用将VV流媒体转变为远程物理环境的实时接口，对逼真的场景表现、低延迟交互以及在异构网络下的强大性能提出了新的系统级需求。3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）已被广泛用于实时写实渲染，提供更优越的视觉质量和渲染性能，但由于带宽消耗，面临挑战。此外，作为自适应VV流的基础，基于密度的现有细节层级（LoD）方法并不适合高斯表示，导致可见的缺口和严重的质量下降。近期研究还探讨了属性压缩技术以减少带宽消耗。我们的初步研究表明，激进属性压缩主要会导致颜色变形，而在渲染图像中，使用参考图像可以有效校正颜色。基于这些发现，我们提出了一种新的自适应VV流色彩自适应方案，利用矢量量化（VQ）建立光度度（LoD）并校正低分辨率参考图像的色彩失真。我们还进一步介绍了CAGS，这是一种兼容多种高斯表示的自适应VV流系统，通过在流媒体服务器渲染参考图像并在客户端进行色彩恢复，实现了色彩自适应方案。我们原型系统的大量实验表明，CAGS在PSNR中以5$\sim$20 dB在波动带宽下表现优于现有自适应流系统，运行速度远快于现有可扩展的高斯压缩方法，并且可推广到不同的高斯表示中。  

### [24] Generative 3D Gaussians with Learned Density Control  
- **⏳发布**：2026-05-08  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Runjie Yan, Yan-Pei Cao, Peng Wang, Ding Liang, Yuan-Chen Guo  
- **📝说明**：19 pages, 16 figures, SIGGRAPH Conference Papers '26  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16355v1)    
- **📝摘要**：We present Density-Sampled Gaussians (DeG), a novel 3D representation designed to bridge the gap between adaptive rendering primitives and scalable generative modeling. Unlike existing approaches that constrain 3D Gaussians to fixed voxel grids or arrays, DeG models Gaussian centers as samples from a learnable probability density function defined over an octree. This formulation provides a rigorous mathematical framework for adaptive density control: by jointly optimizing the spatial density and Gaussian attributes under rendering supervision, our model naturally concentrates primitives in regions of high geometric complexity. We achieve this via a new render loss contribution gradient that serves as a fully differentiable analogue to the discrete densification and pruning heuristics used in standard Gaussian Splatting. The resulting representation is highly flexible, supporting variable-resolution decoding from a single latent code by simply adjusting the sampling budget. To enable generative synthesis, we train a latent diffusion model on DeG. We identify a critical challenge in applying diffusion to unordered set-structured latents, which can significantly slow convergence, and propose VecSeq, a canonical re-indexing mechanism that anchors latent tokens to a deterministic 3D Sobol sequence. This transforms the ambiguous set-generation problem into a robust sequence modeling task. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our pipeline achieves state-of-the-art quality in single-image-to-3D generation, combining the structural adaptivity of unstructured primitives with the training stability of grid-based methods.  
- **📝翻译**: 我们介绍了密度采样高斯分布（DeG），一种新型三维表示法，旨在弥合自适应渲染原语与可扩展生成建模之间的鸿沟。与现有将三维高斯分布限制在固定体素网格或数组的方法不同，DeG将高斯中心建模为定义在八叉树上的可学习概率密度函数中的样本。该表述为自适应密度控制提供了严谨的数学框架：通过在渲染监督下联合优化空间密度和高斯属性，我们的模型自然地将基元集中在几何复杂度高的区域。我们通过一种新的渲染损耗贡献梯度实现这一点，该梯度作为标准高斯喷溅中离散密度化和修剪启发式的完全可微类比。由此产生的表示非常灵活，支持通过调整采样预算即可从单个潜在码进行可变分辨率解码。为了实现生成综合，我们在DeG上训练了一个潜在扩散模型。我们指出在将扩散应用于无序集合结构潜伏时面临的关键挑战，这会显著减缓收敛速度，并提出了VecSeq，这是一种典范的重新索引机制，将潜在标记锚定在确定性的三维Sobol序列上。这将模糊的集合生成问题转变为一个稳健的序列建模任务。大量实验表明，我们的流水线在单图到三维生成方面实现了最先进的质量，结合了非结构化图元的结构适应性与基于网格方法的训练稳定性。  

### [25] Disambiguating 2D-3D Correspondences in Gaussian Splatting-based Feature Fields for Visual Localization  
- **⏳发布**：2026-05-08  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Miso Lee, Sangeek Hyun, Yerim Jeon, Jae-Pil Heo  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07351v1)    
- **📝摘要**：While Gaussian Splatting-based Feature Fields (GSFFs) have shown promise for visual localization, this paper highlights that photometrically optimized GSFFs are inherently ill-suited for 2D-3D matching. The volumetric extent of each Gaussian induces many-to-one pixel-to-point mappings that destabilize PnP-based pose estimation, while photometric optimization gives rise to superfluous Gaussians devoid of multi-view consistency. To address these issues, we propose SplitGS-Loc, a localization-specialized GSFFs construction framework that disambiguates 2D-3D correspondences by exploiting Gaussian attributes. Our key design, Mixture-of-Gaussians-based splitting, decomposes each Gaussian into smaller Gaussians, replacing ambiguous many-to-one with precise one-to-one correspondences. In parallel, we exploit composition weights from GS rasterization to select Gaussians that significantly and consistently contribute across multiple views and aggregate discriminative features through strong pixel-Gaussian associations, enforcing multi-view consistency. The resulting compact yet discriminative feature fields enable stable PnP convergence, achieving state-of-the-art performance on localization benchmarks. Extensive experiments validate that SplitGS-Loc extends the utility of photometric GSFFs to accurate and efficient localization by exploiting Gaussian attributes, without per-scene training or iterative pose refinement.  
- **📝翻译**: 虽然基于高斯的喷溅特征场（GSFF）在视觉定位方面展现出潜力，本文强调光度优化的GSFF本质上不适合二维-三维匹配。每个高斯的体积范围会导致多对一像素到点的映射，从而破坏基于PnP的姿态估计，而光度优化则产生缺乏多视角一致性的高斯分布。为解决这些问题，我们提出了SplitGS-Loc，一种专注于本地化的GSFFs构造框架，通过利用高斯属性消除二维与三维对应的歧义。我们的关键设计是基于高斯分布的混合分解，将每个高斯分解为更小的高斯分布，将模糊的多对一替换为精确的一一对应关系。同时，我们利用GS光栅化的合成权重，选择在多视图中显著且持续贡献的高斯分布，并通过强像素-高斯关联汇总判别特征，强化多视图一致性。由此产生的紧凑而判别能力强的特征场实现了稳定的PnP收敛，在本地化基准测试中实现了最先进的性能。大量实验验证了SplitGS-Loc通过利用高斯属性，将光度GSFF的实用性扩展到准确高效的定位，无需每场景训练或迭代姿态细化。  

### [26] Large-Scale High-Quality 3D Gaussian Head Reconstruction from Multi-View Captures  
- **⏳发布**：2026-05-05（更新：2026-07-01）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Evangelos Ntavelis, Sean Wu, Mohamad Shahbazi et al.  
- **📝说明**：Accepted to ECCV 2026. Project website: https://apple.github.io/ml-headsup/  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04035v3)    
- **📝摘要**：We propose HeadsUp, a scalable feed-forward method for reconstructing high-quality 3D Gaussian heads from large-scale multi-camera setups. Our method employs an efficient encoder-decoder architecture that compresses input views into a compact latent representation. This latent representation is then decoded into a set of UV-parameterized 3D Gaussians anchored to a neutral head template. This UV representation decouples the number of 3D Gaussians from the number and resolution of input images, enabling training with many high-resolution input views. We train and evaluate our model on an internal dataset with more than 10,000 subjects, which is an order of magnitude larger than existing multi-view human head datasets. HeadsUp achieves state-of-the-art reconstruction quality and generalizes to novel identities without test-time optimization. We extensively analyze the scaling behavior of our model across identities, views, and model capacity, revealing practical insights for quality-compute trade-offs. Finally, we highlight the strength of our latent space by showcasing two downstream applications: generating novel 3D identities and animating the 3D heads with expression blendshapes.  
- **📝翻译**: 我们提出了HeadsUp，这是一种可扩展的前馈方法，用于从大规模多机位设备重建高质量三维高斯云台。我们的方法采用高效的编码-解码架构，将输入视图压缩成紧凑的潜在表示。该潜在表示随后被解码为一组UV参数化的三维高斯分布，锚定在中性头模板上。这种UV表示法将三维高斯数与输入图像的数量和分辨率解耦，使得使用多种高分辨率输入视图进行训练成为可能。我们在一个包含超过1万名受试者的内部数据集上训练和评估模型，这比现有多视角人头数据集大一个数量级。HeadsUp 实现了最先进的重建质量，并在不进行测试时间优化的情况下推广到新颖的身份。我们广泛分析模型在身份、视图和模型容量下的缩放行为，揭示了质量与计算权衡的实用见解。最后，我们通过展示两个下游应用来突出潜在空间的优势：生成新颖的3D身份和用表情混合形状动画3D头部。  

### [27] GETA-3DGS: Automatic Joint Structured Pruning and Quantization for 3D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2026-05-03  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Baobing Zhang, Wanxin Sui  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02086v1)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) is a state-of-the-art representation for real-time photorealistic novel-view synthesis, yet a single high-fidelity scene typically occupies hundreds of megabytes to several gigabytes, exceeding the budgets of mobile, immersive, and volumetric video platforms. Existing 3DGS compression methods (e.g., HAC++, FlexGaussian, LP-3DGS) treat pruning, quantization, and entropy coding as separate stages and rely on hand-tuned heuristics (opacity thresholds, fixed bit-widths, SH truncation), limiting cross-scene generalization and preventing users from specifying a target rate or quality budget. We propose GETA-3DGS, to our knowledge the first end-to-end automatic joint structured pruning and quantization framework for 3DGS. Building on GETA for joint pruning-quantization of deep networks, we contribute: (i) a 3DGS-aware quantization-aware dependency graph (QADG) treating each Gaussian primitive as a group with five attribute sub-nodes and degree-aware SH sub-nodes; (ii) a render-aware saliency fusing transmittance-weighted contribution, screen-space gradient, and pixel coverage into a Gaussian-level importance score; and (iii) a heterogeneous per-attribute mixed-precision scheme co-optimized with structural sparsity under a projected partial saliency-guided (PPSG) descent guarantee. On Mip-NeRF 360, Tanks and Temples, and Deep Blending, GETA-3DGS operates directly on raw Gaussian primitives rather than a post-hoc anchor representation, delivering ~5x storage reduction over Vanilla 3DGS with no per-scene thresholds. Bit-width policy is the dominant rate-distortion lever: a uniform 6-bit cap costs up to -6.74 dB on view-dependent scenes versus our heterogeneous allocation, matching an information-theoretic reverse-water-filling analysis we develop. GETA-3DGS is complementary to existing codecs: entropy coding (HAC++, CompGS) is downstream, so the two can be composed.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）是一种最先进的实时写实小说视角合成技术，但单个高保真场景通常占用数百兆字节到数GB的内存，超出移动、沉浸式和体积视频平台的预算。现有的3DGS压缩方法（如HAC++、FlexGaussian、LP-3DGS）将剪枝、量化和熵编码视为独立阶段，依赖手工调优的启发式方法（不透明度阈值、固定位宽、SH截断），限制了跨场景泛化，并防止用户指定目标速率或质量预算。我们提出了GETA-3DGS，据我们所知，这是3DGS首个端到端自动关节结构剪枝和量化框架。基于GETA的深度网络联合剪枝-量子化，我们贡献了：（i） 一个3DGS感知的量化感知依赖图（QADG），将每个高斯原语视为一个群，包含五个属性子节点和度感知的SH子节点;（ii）将透射加权贡献、屏幕空间梯度和像素覆盖融合为高斯级别重要性评分的渲染感知显著性;以及（iii）在预测部分显著性引导（PPSG）下降保证下，与结构稀疏性协同优化的异质每属性混合精度方案。在 Mip-NeRF 360、Tanks and Temples 以及 Deep Blending 中，GETA-3DGS 直接运行在原始高斯原语上，而非事后锚点表示，相比原版 3DGS 实现了约 5 倍的存储空间，且无每场景阈值。位宽策略是主导的速率失真杠杆：在视角相关场景中，统一的6位上限成本可降低至-6.74 dB，与我们异构分配相匹配，符合我们开发的信息理论反向填充分析。GETA-3DGS 是现有编解码器的补充：熵编码（HAC++、CompGS）位于下游，因此两者可以组合。  

### [28] 2D-SuGaR: Surface-Aware Gaussian Splatting for Geometrically Accurate Mesh Reconstruction  
- **⏳发布**：2026-05-01  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Prajwal Gupta C. R., Divyam Sheth, Jinjoo Ha, Mirela Ostrek, Justus Thies  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00569v1)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as a powerful technique for generating photorealistic renderings of a scene in real-time. However, the volumetric nature of 3DGS limits its ability to accurately capture surface geometry. To address this, 2D Gaussian Splatting (2DGS) was proposed to enable view-consistent and geometrically accurate surface reconstruction from multi-view images. However, 2DGS can be sensitive to the initialization of the Gaussian primitives. Reliance on Structure-from-Motion (SfM) initializations, which can produce poor estimates on challenging image sets, may lead to subpar results. In this work, we enhance 2DGS by incorporating monocular depth and normal priors to improve both geometric accuracy and robustness. We propose a depth-guided initialization strategy for Gaussians and introduce a clustering-based technique for pruning degenerate Gaussians. We evaluate our method on the DTU dataset, where it achieves state-of-the-art results in mesh reconstruction while preserving high-quality novel view synthesis.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）已成为一种强大的实时生成场景照片级真实渲染的技术。然而，3DGS的体积特性限制了其准确捕捉表面几何形态的能力。为此，提出了二维高斯喷溅（2DGS）技术，以实现从多视图图像中实现视图一致且几何精确的表面重建。然而，2DGS对高斯原语的初始化可能敏感。依赖运动结构（SfM）初始化，可能导致对复杂图像集的估计不佳，可能导致效果不佳。在本研究中，我们通过引入单眼深度和法向先验来增强二重重眼镜，以提升几何精度和鲁棒性。我们提出了一种深度引导的高斯初始化策略，并引入基于聚类的技术用于剪枝退化高斯量。我们在DTU数据集上评估了我们的方法，在保持高质量新颖视图合成的同时，实现了网格重建的尖端成果。  


## April 2026

### [1] MesonGS++: Post-training Compression of 3D Gaussian Splatting with Hyperparameter Searching  
- **⏳发布**：2026-04-29（更新：2026-05-07）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Shuzhao Xie, Junchen Ge, Weixiang Zhang et al.  
- **📝说明**：https://github.com/mmlab-sigs/mesongs_plus  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26799v2)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) achieves high-quality novel view synthesis with real-time rendering, but its storage cost remains prohibitive for practical deployment. Existing post-training compression methods still rely on many coupled hyperparameters across pruning, transformation, quantization, and entropy coding, making it difficult to control the final compressed size and fully exploit the rate-distortion trade-off. We propose MesonGS++, a size-aware post-training codec for 3D Gaussian compression. On the codec side, MesonGS++ combines joint importance-based pruning, octree geometry coding, attribute transformation, selective vector quantization for higher-degree spherical harmonics, and group-wise mixed-precision quantization with entropy coding. On the configuration side, it treats the reserve ratio and bit-width allocation as the dominant rate-distortion knobs and jointly optimizes them under a target storage budget via discrete sampling and 0--1 integer linear programming. We further propose a linear size estimator and a CUDA parallel quantization operator to accelerate the hyperparameter searching process. Extensive experiments show that MesonGS++ achieves over 34$\times$ compression while preserving rendering fidelity, outperforming state-of-the-art post-training methods and accurately meeting target size budgets. Remarkably, without any training, MesonGS++ can even surpass the PSNR of vanilla 3DGS at a 20$\times$ compression rate on the Stump scene. Our code is available at https://github.com/mmlab-sigs/mesongs_plus  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷射（3DGS）实现了高质量的新颖视图合成和实时渲染，但其存储成本仍然高昂，难以实际部署。现有的训练后压缩方法仍依赖于剪枝、变换、量化和熵编码等多种耦合超参数，这使得控制最终压缩尺寸和充分利用速率-失真权衡变得困难。我们提出了 MesonGS++，一种尺寸感知的训练后编解码器，用于三维高斯压缩。在编解码器方面，MesonGS++结合了基于重要性的联合剪枝、八叉树几何编码、属性变换、高阶球谐波的选择性向量量化，以及带熵编码的群混合精度量化。在配置端，它将储备比和位宽分配视为主导的速率失真旋钮，并通过离散采样和0-1整数线性规划在目标存储预算下联合优化。我们还提出了线性尺寸估计器和CUDA并行量化算子，以加速超参数搜索过程。大量实验表明，MesonGS++在保持渲染保真度的同时，实现了超过34美元\倍数的压缩，超越了最先进的后期训练方法，并准确满足目标规模预算。令人惊讶的是，毫无培训的情况下，MesonGS++甚至能在Stump场景中以20$\times$的压缩率超越原版3DGS的PSNR。我们的代码可在 https://github.com/mmlab-sigs/mesongs_plus 获取  

### [2] Generalizable 3D Gaussian Splatting enabled Semantic Coding for Real-Time Immersive Video Communications  
- **⏳发布**：2026-04-28  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Dingxi Yang, Wenqi Guo, Yue Liu, Jungong Han, Zhijin Qin  
- **📝说明**：Under review  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25330v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Real-time immersive video communications, particularly high-fidelity 3D telepresence, necessitates a synergistic balance between instantaneous dynamic scene reconstruction and high-efficiency data transmission. While recent advancements in feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have enabled real-time rendering, performing multi-view video coding and 3D reconstruction in a decoupled manner leads to suboptimal compression efficiency and high computational complexity. To address this, we propose GS-SCNet, the first unified end-to-end framework that seamlessly integrates generalizable 3DGS reconstruction with a dedicated deep Semantic Coding pipeline. Our architecture is underpinned by two core technical contributions: (i) we introduce a Disparity-Guided Parallel Semantic Codec that exploits epipolar geometric priors to facilitate cross-view contextual interaction via disparity compensation and semantic fusion, thereby enabling real-time parallel processing of stereo streams while significantly enhancing rate-distortion performance, and (ii) we develop a Lightweight Gaussian Parameter Predictor which directly projects decoded semantic latents into 3DGS attributes, obviating the need for intermediate pixel-domain reconstruction. By coupling the codec with the task-specific predictor, our framework extracts geometric correlations only once, effectively eliminating the redundant computational bottleneck inherent in conventional decoupled paradigms. Extensive evaluations on both synthetic and real-world human datasets demonstrate that GS-SCNet achieves a superior trade-off across compression efficiency, rendering quality, and real-time performance. Notably, our framework exhibits strong cross-domain generalization and robustness against compression artifacts when applied to out-of-domain real-world data, significantly outperforming conventional decoupled transmission paradigms.  
- **📝翻译**: 实时沉浸式视频通信，尤其是高保真3D远程呈现，需要即时动态场景重建与高效数据传输之间的协同平衡。虽然前馈三维高斯拼接（3DGS）的最新进展使实时渲染成为可能，但以解耦方式进行多视图视频编码和三维重建会导致压缩效率不佳且计算复杂度较高。为此，我们提出了GS-SCNet，这是首个统一的端到端框架，能够无缝集成可推广的3DGS重建与专用的深度语义编码流水线。我们的架构基于两项核心技术贡献：（i）我们引入了一种差导引并行语义编码器，利用极性几何先验通过差补偿和语义融合实现交叉视图上下文交互，从而实现立体流的实时并行处理，同时显著提升速率失真性能;（ii）我们开发了一种轻量级高斯参数预测器，直接投射已解码的语义潜伏转换为3DGS属性，从而避免了中间像素域重建的需求。通过将编解码器与任务特定预测器耦合，我们的框架仅提取一次几何相关性，有效消除了传统解耦范式中固有的冗余计算瓶颈。对合成和真实人类数据集的广泛评估表明，GS-SCNet在压缩效率、渲染质量和实时性能方面实现了更优的权衡。值得注意的是，我们的框架在应用于域外真实数据时表现出强的跨域泛化性和对压缩伪影的鲁棒性，显著优于传统的解耦传输范式。  

### [3] Gaussians on a Diet: High-Quality Memory-Bounded 3D Gaussian Splatting Training  
- **⏳发布**：2026-04-21（更新：2026-04-23）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Yangming Zhang, Jian Xu, Chaojian Li et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20046v2)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has revolutionized novel view synthesis with high-quality rendering through continuous aggregations of millions of 3D Gaussian primitives. However, it suffers from a substantial memory footprint, particularly during training due to uncontrolled densification, posing a critical bottleneck for deployment on memory-constrained edge devices. While existing methods prune redundant Gaussians post-training, they fail to address the peak memory spikes caused by the abrupt growth of Gaussians early in the training process. To solve the training memory consumption problem, we propose a systematic memory-bounded training framework that dynamically optimizes Gaussians through iterative growth and pruning. In other words, the proposed framework alternates between incremental pruning of low-impact Gaussians and strategic growing of new primitives with an adaptive Gaussian compensation, maintaining a near-constant low memory usage while progressively refining rendering fidelity. We comprehensively evaluate the proposed training framework on various real-world datasets under strict memory constraints, showing significant improvements over existing state-of-the-art methods. Particularly, our proposed method practically enables memory-efficient 3DGS training on NVIDIA Jetson AGX Xavier, achieving similar visual quality with up to 80% lower peak training memory consumption than the original 3DGS.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）通过连续聚合数百万个3D高斯原语，实现了高质量渲染，彻底革新了新颖视图合成。然而，由于不受控制的密度化，尤其是在训练期间，它存在较大的内存占用，这成为部署在内存受限边缘设备上的关键瓶颈。现有方法在训练后修剪冗余高斯分布，但未能解决高斯分布在训练初期突然增长引发的记忆峰值。为解决训练内存消耗问题，我们提出了一个系统化的记忆受限训练框架，通过迭代增长和修剪动态优化高斯分布。换言之，所提框架在低影响高斯的增量修剪与采用自适应高斯补偿的新图元的战略性增长之间交替进行，保持近乎恒定的低内存使用率，同时逐步优化渲染的保真度。我们在严格内存约束下，在各种真实世界数据集上全面评估了所提出的训练框架，显示出相较于现有最先进方法的显著改进。特别是，我们提出的方法实际上实现了NVIDIA Jetson AGX Xavier上的内存高效3DGS训练，实现了类似的视觉质量，同时峰值训练内存消耗比原始3DGS低了多达80%。  

### [4] OT-UVGS: Revisiting UV Mapping for Gaussian Splatting as a Capacity Allocation Problem  
- **⏳发布**：2026-04-21  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Byunghyun Kim  
- **📝说明**：Accepted to Eurographics 2026 Short Papers  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19127v1)    
- **📝摘要**：UV-parameterized Gaussian Splatting (UVGS) maps an unstructured set of 3D Gaussians to a regular UV tensor, enabling compact storage and explicit control of representation capacity. Existing UVGS, however, uses a deterministic spherical pro- jection to assign Gaussians to UV locations. Because this mapping ignores the global Gaussian distribution, it often leaves many UV slots empty while causing frequent collisions in dense regions. We reinterpret UV mapping as a capacity-allocation problem under a fixed UV budget and propose OT-UVGS, a lightweight, separable one-dimensional optimal-transport-inspired mapping that globally couples assignments while preserving the original UVGS representation. The method is implemented with rank-based sorting, has O(N log N) complexity for N Gaussians, and can be used as a drop-in replacement for spherical UVGS. Across 184 object-centric scenes and the Mip-NeRF dataset, OT-UVGS consistently improves peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR), structural similarity (SSIM), and Learned Perceptual Image Patch Similarity (LPIPS) under the same UV resolution and per-slot capacity (K=1). These gains are accompanied by substantially better UV utilization, including higher non-empty slot ratios, fewer collisions, and higher Gaussian retention. Our results show that revisiting the mapping alone can unlock a significant fraction of the latent capacity of UVGS.  
- **📝翻译**: UV参数化高斯喷射（UVGS）将一组非结构化的三维高斯分布映射到正规的UV张量，实现紧凑的存储和显式控制表示容量。然而，现有的UVGS使用确定性球面射来将高斯分布分配到紫外位置。由于该映射忽略了全局高斯分布，常常导致许多紫外狭缝空缺，同时在密集区域中频繁发生碰撞。我们将UV映射重新解释为固定UV预算下的容量分配问题，并提出了OT-UVGS，这是一种轻量级、可分离的一维最优传输启发映射，能够全局耦合分配，同时保持原始UVGS表示。该方法采用基于秩排序实现，N个高斯分布的复杂度为O（N log N），可作为球面UVGS的直接替代。在184个以物体为中心的场景和Mip-NeRF数据集中，OT-UVGS在相同的紫外分辨率和每槽容量（K=1）下，持续提升峰值信噪比（PSNR）、结构相似度（SSIM）和习得性感知图像贴片相似度（LPIPS）。这些提升伴随着显著提升的紫外利用率，包括更高的非空槽比、更少的碰撞以及更高的高斯保留率。我们的结果表明，仅重新审视该映射就能释放UVGS潜在容量的相当一部分。  

### [5] Incoherent Deformation, Not Capacity: Diagnosing and Mitigating Overfitting in Dynamic Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2026-04-17（更新：2026-04-29）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Ahmad Droby  
- **📝说明**：10 pages, 6 figures, 2 tables  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16747v2)    
- **📝摘要**：Dynamic 3D Gaussian Splatting methods achieve strong training-view PSNR on monocular video but generalize poorly: on the D-NeRF benchmark we measure an average train-test PSNR gap of 6.18 dB, rising to 11 dB on individual scenes. We report two findings that together account for most of that gap.   Finding 1 (the role of splitting). A systematic ablation of the Adaptive Density Control pipeline (split, clone, prune, frequency, threshold, schedule) shows that splitting is responsible for over 80% of the gap: disabling split collapses the cloud from 44K to 3K Gaussians and the gap from 6.18 dB to 1.15 dB. Across all threshold-varying ablations, gap is log-linear in count (r = 0.995, bootstrap 95% CI [0.99, 1.00]), which suggests a capacity-based explanation.   Finding 2 (the role of deformation coherence). We show that the capacity explanation is incomplete. A local-smoothness penalty on the per-Gaussian deformation field -- Elastic Energy Regularization (EER) -- reduces the gap by 40.8% while growing the cloud by 85%. Measuring per-Gaussian strain directly on trained checkpoints, EER reduces mean strain by 99.72% (median 99.80%) across all 8 scenes; on 8/8 scenes the median Gaussian under EER is less strained than the 1st-percentile (best-behaved) Gaussian under baseline. Alongside EER, we evaluate two further regularizers: GAD, a loss-rate-aware densification threshold, and PTDrop, a jitter-weighted Gaussian dropout. GAD+EER reduces the gap by 48%; adding PTDrop and a soft growth cap reaches 57%. We confirm that coherence generalizes to (a) a different deformation architecture (Deformable-3DGS, +40.6% gap reduction at re-tuned lambda), and (b) real monocular video (4 HyperNeRF scenes, reducing the mean PSNR gap by 14.9% at the same lambda as D-NeRF, with near-zero quality cost). The overfitting in dynamic 3DGS is driven by incoherent deformation, not parameter count.  
- **📝翻译**: 动态三维高斯散射方法在单目视频上实现强的训练视图PSNR，但泛化效果不佳：在D-NeRF基准测试中，我们测量的平均训练测试PSNR差为6.18 dB，单个场景上升至11 dB。我们报告了两个共同解释了大部分差距的发现。  寻找1（分裂的角色）。对自适应密度控制流水线（分裂、克隆、修剪、频率、阈值、调度）进行系统消融显示，分裂导致了80%以上的间隙：禁用分裂会使云从44K高斯降至3K，间隙从6.18 dB降至1.15 dB。在所有阈值变化消融中，缺口计数为对数线性（r = 0.995，自助95%置信区间[0.99， 1.00]），这表明了基于容量的解释。  寻找2（变形相干性的作用）。我们证明容量解释不完整。对全高斯变形场施加局部光滑性惩罚——弹性能量正则化（EER）——可将间隙减少40.8%，同时使云增长85%。通过直接在训练好的检查点上测量全高斯应变，EER在所有8个场景中平均应变降低了99.72%（中位数99.80%）;在8/8场景中，EER下的中位高斯分布比基线下第一百分位（行为最佳）高斯分布更小。除了EER外，我们还评估了另外两种正则化因子：GAD（一种对损失率感知的致密阈值）和PTDrop（抖动加权高斯脱落量）。GAD+EER将差距缩小了48%;加上PTDrop和软增长上限，达到57%。我们确认相干性可推广为（a）不同的变形架构（可变形3DGS，在重新调谐λ下减少+40.6%的间隙），以及（b）真实单目视频（4个HyperNeRF场景，在与D-NeRF相同λ下平均PSNR间隙减少14.9%，且质量成本几乎为零）。动态3DGS中的过拟合是由非相干变形驱动的，而非参数数量。  

### [6] Neural Gabor Splatting: Enhanced Gaussian Splatting with Neural Gabor for High-frequency Surface Reconstruction  
- **⏳发布**：2026-04-17  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Haato Watanabe, Nobuyuki Umetani  
- **📝说明**：Accepted to CVPR 2026  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15941v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Recent years have witnessed the rapid emergence of 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) as a powerful approach for 3D reconstruction and novel view synthesis. Its explicit representation with Gaussian primitives enables fast training, real-time rendering, and convenient post-processing such as editing and surface reconstruction. However, 3DGS suffers from a critical drawback: the number of primitives grows drastically for scenes with high-frequency appearance details, since each primitive can represent only a single color, requiring multiple primitives for every sharp color transition. To overcome this limitation, we propose neural Gabor splatting, which augments each Gaussian primitive with a lightweight multi-layer perceptron that models a wide range of color variations within a single primitive. To further control primitive numbers, we introduce a frequency-aware densification strategy that selects mismatch primitives for pruning and cloning based on frequency energy. Our method achieves accurate reconstruction of challenging high-frequency surfaces. We demonstrate its effectiveness through extensive experiments on both standard benchmarks, such as Mip-NeRF360 and High-Frequency datasets (e.g., checkered patterns), supported by comprehensive ablation studies.  
- **📝翻译**: 近年来，三维高斯喷溅（3DGS）迅速崛起，成为三维重建和新颖视图合成的强大方法。其用高斯原语的显式表示支持快速训练、实时渲染以及方便的后期处理，如编辑和曲面重建。然而，3DGS存在一个关键缺点：对于具有高频外观细节的场景，图元数量会急剧增加，因为每个图元只能表示单一颜色，每个锐利的色彩转换都需要多个图元。为克服这一限制，我们提出了神经Gabor溅射技术，它在每个高斯原意基础上添加轻量级多层感知器，模拟单个原体内广泛的颜色变化。为了进一步控制原始数，我们引入了一种频率感知密度化策略，根据频率能量选择不匹配原语进行剪枝和克隆。我们的方法能够准确重建具有挑战性的高频表面。我们通过对标准基准测试（如Mip-NeRF360和高频数据集，如棋盘纹图案）进行大量实验，并辅以全面的消融研究，验证了其有效性。  

### [7] Splats in Splats++: Robust and Generalizable 3D Gaussian Splatting Steganography  
- **⏳发布**：2026-04-17  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Yijia Guo, Wenkai Huang, Tong Hu et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15862v1)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has recently redefined the paradigm of 3D reconstruction, striking an unprecedented balance between visual fidelity and computational efficiency. As its adoption proliferates, safeguarding the copyright of explicit 3DGS assets has become paramount. However, existing invisible message embedding frameworks struggle to reconcile secure and high-capacity data embedding with intrinsic asset utility, often disrupting the native rendering pipeline or exhibiting vulnerability to structural perturbations. In this work, we present \textbf{\textit{Splats in Splats++}}, a unified and pipeline-agnostic steganography framework that seamlessly embeds high-capacity 3D/4D content directly within the native 3DGS representation. Grounded in a principled analysis of the frequency distribution of Spherical Harmonics (SH), we propose an importance-graded SH coefficient encryption scheme that achieves imperceptible embedding without compromising the original expressive power. To fundamentally resolve the geometric ambiguities that lead to message leakage, we introduce a \textbf{Hash-Grid Guided Opacity Mapping} mechanism. Coupled with a novel \textbf{Gradient-Gated Opacity Consistency Loss}, our formulation enforces a stringent spatial-attribute coupling between the original and hidden scenes, effectively projecting the discrete attribute mapping into a continuous, attack-resilient latent manifold. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method substantially outperforms existing approaches, achieving up to \textbf{6.28 db} higher message fidelity, \textbf{3$\times$} faster rendering, and exceptional robustness against aggressive 3D-targeted structural attacks (e.g., GSPure). Furthermore, our framework exhibits remarkable versatility, generalizing seamlessly to 2D image embedding, 4D dynamic scene steganography, and diverse downstream tasks.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯拼拍（3DGS）最近重新定义了3D重建的范式，在视觉真实度与计算效率之间取得了前所未有的平衡。随着其普及，保护3DGS特定资产的版权变得至关重要。然而，现有的隐形消息嵌入框架难以调和安全且高容量的数据嵌入与内在资产的实用性，常常扰乱原生渲染流水线或对结构性扰动表现出脆弱性。在本研究中，我们提出了 \textbf{\textit{Splats in Splats++}}，一个统一且无关流水线的隐写术框架，能够无缝嵌入高容量的3D/4D内容，直接嵌入原生3DGS表示中。基于对球谐（SH）频率分布的原则性分析，我们提出了一种按重要度分级的SH系数加密方案，实现了不可察觉的嵌入，同时不牺牲原始的表达力。为了从根本上解决导致消息泄露的几何歧义，我们引入了 \textbf{哈希网格引导不透明度映射}机制。结合一种新颖的 \textbf{渐变门控不透明度一致性损失}，我们的表述强制原始场景与隐藏场景之间严格的空间属性耦合，有效地将离散属性映射投影为连续且具抗攻击性的潜在流形。大量实验表明，我们的方法远超现有方法，实现高达 \textbf{6.28 db} 更高的消息忠实度，\textbf{3$\times$} 更快的渲染速度，并且对针对激进的3D结构攻击（如GSPure）表现出卓越的鲁棒性。此外，我们的框架展现出卓越的多功能性，能够无缝推广到二维图像嵌入、四维动态场景隐写术以及多种下游任务。  

### [8] GaussianFlow SLAM: Monocular Gaussian Splatting SLAM Guided by GaussianFlow  
- **⏳发布**：2026-04-17  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Dong-Uk Seo, Jinwoo Jeon, Eungchang Mason Lee, Hyun Myung  
- **📝说明**：8 pages, 5 figures, 7 tables, accepted to IEEE RA-L  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15612v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Gaussian splatting has recently gained traction as a compelling map representation for SLAM systems, enabling dense and photo-realistic scene modeling. However, its application to monocular SLAM remains challenging due to the lack of reliable geometric cues from monocular input. Without geometric supervision, mapping or tracking could fall in local-minima, resulting in structural degeneracies and inaccuracies. To address this challenge, we propose GaussianFlow SLAM, a monocular 3DGS-SLAM that leverages optical flow as a geometry-aware cue to guide the optimization of both the scene structure and camera poses. By encouraging the projected motion of Gaussians, termed GaussianFlow, to align with the optical flow, our method introduces consistent structural cues to regularize both map reconstruction and pose estimation. Furthermore, we introduce normalized error-based densification and pruning modules to refine inactive and unstable Gaussians, thereby contributing to improved map quality and pose accuracy. Experiments conducted on public datasets demonstrate that our method achieves superior rendering quality and tracking accuracy compared with state-of-the-art algorithms. The source code is available at: https://github.com/url-kaist/gaussianflow-slam.  
- **📝翻译**: 高斯喷溅最近作为一种引人注目的SLAM系统地图表示方式，使得密集且逼真的场景建模成为可能。然而，由于缺乏可靠的几何线索，单眼SLAM的应用仍然具有挑战性。如果没有几何监督，映射或跟踪可能处于局部极小值，导致结构简并和不准确。为应对这一挑战，我们提出了GaussianFlow SLAM，这是一种单眼3DGS-SLAM，利用光流作为几何感知线索，指导场景结构和摄像机姿态的优化。通过鼓励高斯流（称为高斯流）的投影运动与光流对齐，我们的方法引入了一致的结构线索，以规范映射重建和位姿估计。此外，我们引入了归一化的基于误差的致密和剪枝模块，以优化非活跃和不稳定的高斯分布，从而提升了地图质量和姿态精度。在公开数据集上的实验表明，我们的方法相比最先进的算法，在渲染质量和跟踪精度上更优。源代码可在以下网站获取：https://github.com/url-kaist/gaussianflow-slam。  

### [9] GlobalSplat: Efficient Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting via Global Scene Tokens  
- **⏳发布**：2026-04-16（更新：2026-04-17）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Roni Itkin, Noam Issachar, Yehonatan Keypur et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15284v2)   · [Project](https://r-itk.github.io/globalsplat/)  
- **📝摘要**：The efficient spatial allocation of primitives serves as the foundation of 3D Gaussian Splatting, as it directly dictates the synergy between representation compactness, reconstruction speed, and rendering fidelity. Previous solutions, whether based on iterative optimization or feed-forward inference, suffer from significant trade-offs between these goals, mainly due to the reliance on local, heuristic-driven allocation strategies that lack global scene awareness. Specifically, current feed-forward methods are largely pixel-aligned or voxel-aligned. By unprojecting pixels into dense, view-aligned primitives, they bake redundancy into the 3D asset. As more input views are added, the representation size increases and global consistency becomes fragile. To this end, we introduce GlobalSplat, a framework built on the principle of align first, decode later. Our approach learns a compact, global, latent scene representation that encodes multi-view input and resolves cross-view correspondences before decoding any explicit 3D geometry. Crucially, this formulation enables compact, globally consistent reconstructions without relying on pretrained pixel-prediction backbones or reusing latent features from dense baselines. Utilizing a coarse-to-fine training curriculum that gradually increases decoded capacity, GlobalSplat natively prevents representation bloat. On RealEstate10K and ACID, our model achieves competitive novel-view synthesis performance while utilizing as few as 16K Gaussians, significantly less than required by dense pipelines, obtaining a light 4MB footprint. Further, GlobalSplat enables significantly faster inference than the baselines, operating under 78 milliseconds in a single forward pass. Project page is available at https://r-itk.github.io/globalsplat/  
- **📝翻译**: 图元的高效空间分配是三维高斯拼写的基础，因为它直接决定了表示的紧凑性、重建速度和渲染真实度之间的协同效应。以往的解决方案，无论是基于迭代优化还是前馈推断，都存在这些目标之间的显著权衡，主要由于依赖于缺乏全局场景感知的本地启发式分配策略。具体来说，当前前馈方法主要是像素对齐或体素对齐的。通过将像素非投影为密集且视角对齐的图元，他们在3D资产中内置冗余。随着输入视图的增加，表示大小增加，全局一致性变得脆弱。为此，我们引入了GlobalSplat，这是一个基于先对齐、后解码原则构建的框架。我们的方法学习一种紧凑、全局、潜在场景表示，编码多视角输入并解析交叉视角对应关系，然后再解码任何显式三维几何。关键是，这种表述能够实现紧凑、全局一致的重建，无需依赖预训练的像素预测骨干，也无需重复利用密集基线的潜在特征。GlobalSplat 采用从粗到细的培训课程，逐步提升解码容量，原生防止代表性膨胀。在RealEstate10K和ACID上，我们的模型在使用低至16K高斯的同时实现了具有竞争力的新视角综合性能，远低于密集流水线的需求，实现了轻量的4MB占用。此外，GlobalSplat 的推理速度远快于基线，单次前向传递运行时间低于 78 毫秒。项目页面可在 https://r-itk.github.io/globalsplat/  

### [10] MSGS: Multispectral 3D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2026-04-14  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Iris Zheng, Guojun Tang, Alexander Doronin, Paul Teal, Fang-Lue Zhang  
- **📝说明**：Published in IEEE ISMAR 2025 Adjunct  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13340v1)    
- **📝摘要**：We present a multispectral extension to 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) for wavelength-aware view synthesis. Each Gaussian is augmented with spectral radiance, represented via per-band spherical harmonics, and optimized under a dual-loss supervision scheme combining RGB and multispectral signals. To improve rendering fidelity, we perform spectral-to-RGB conversion at the pixel level, allowing richer spectral cues to be retained during optimization. Our method is evaluated on both public and self-captured real-world datasets, demonstrating consistent improvements over the RGB-only 3DGS baseline in terms of image quality and spectral consistency. Notably, it excels in challenging scenes involving translucent materials and anisotropic reflections. The proposed approach maintains the compactness and real-time efficiency of 3DGS while laying the foundation for future integration with physically based shading models.  
- **📝翻译**: 我们提出了对三维高斯喷溅（3DGS）的多光谱扩展，用于波长感知视角合成。每个高斯量都被增强了光谱辐射度，通过每带球谐波表示，并在结合RGB和多光谱信号的双损耗监督方案下进行优化。为了提高渲染精度，我们在像素层面进行光谱转RGB转换，从而在优化过程中保留更丰富的光谱线索。我们的方法在公开和自采集的真实世界数据集上进行了评估，显示出在图像质量和光谱一致性方面相较仅限RGB的3DGS基线有持续的提升。值得注意的是，它在涉及半透明材料和各向异性反射的复杂场景中表现出色。该方法既保持了3DGS的紧凑性和实时效率，又为未来与基于物理的着色模型集成奠定了基础。  

### [11] Unfolding 3D Gaussian Splatting via Iterative Gaussian Synopsis  
- **⏳发布**：2026-04-13  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Yuqin Lu, Yang Zhou, Yihua Dai, Guiqing Li, Shengfeng He  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11685v1)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has become a state-of-the-art framework for real-time, high-fidelity novel view synthesis. However, its substantial storage requirements and inherently unstructured representation pose challenges for deployment in streaming and resource-constrained environments. Existing Level-of-Detail (LOD) strategies, particularly those based on bottom-up construction, often introduce redundancy or lead to fidelity degradation. To overcome these limitations, we propose Iterative Gaussian Synopsis, a novel framework for compact and progressive rendering through a top-down "unfolding" scheme. Our approach begins with a full-resolution 3DGS model and iteratively derives coarser LODs using an adaptive, learnable mask-based pruning mechanism. This process constructs a multi-level hierarchy that preserves visual quality while improving efficiency. We integrate hierarchical spatial grids, which capture the global scene structure, with a shared Anchor Codebook that models localized details. This combination produces a compact yet expressive feature representation, designed to minimize redundancy and support efficient, level-specific adaptation. The unfolding mechanism promotes inter-layer reusability and requires only minimal data overhead for progressive refinement. Experiments show that our method maintains high rendering quality across all LODs while achieving substantial storage reduction. These results demonstrate the practicality and scalability of our approach for real-time 3DGS rendering in bandwidth- and memory-constrained scenarios.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）已成为实时、高保真小说视图合成的先进框架。然而，其庞大的存储需求和本质上的非结构化表示，在流式流和资源受限环境中的部署面临挑战。现有的细节层级（LOD）策略，尤其是基于自下而上的构建，常常会引入冗余或导致保真度下降。为克服这些限制，我们提出了迭代高斯概要，这是一种通过自上而下“展开”方案实现紧凑且渐进渲染的新框架。我们的方法从一个全分辨率的3DGS模型出发，并通过自适应、可学习的遮罩剪枝机制迭代推导出更粗的LOD。这一过程构建了一个多层级层级结构，既保持视觉质量，又提高了效率。我们将捕捉全局场景结构的分层空间网格与用于局部细节建模的共享锚代码手册集成。这种组合产生了紧凑而富有表现力的特征表示，旨在最小化冗余并支持高效的层级特定适应。展开机制促进了层间可重用性，并且只需极低的数据开销即可逐步优化。实验显示，我们的方法在所有LOD上保持了高渲染质量，同时实现了显著的存储减少。这些结果展示了我们在带宽和内存受限场景下实时3DGS渲染方法的实用性和可扩展性。  

### [12] GS4City: Hierarchical Semantic Gaussian Splatting via City-Model Priors  
- **⏳发布**：2026-04-13  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Qilin Zhang, Jinyu Zhu, Olaf Wysocki, Benjamin Busam, Boris Jutzi  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11401v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Recent semantic 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) methods primarily rely on 2D foundation models, often yielding ambiguous boundaries and limited support for structured urban semantics. While city models such as CityGML encode hierarchically organized semantics together with building geometry, these labels cannot be directly mapped to Gaussian primitives. We present GS4City, a hierarchical semantic Gaussian Splatting method that incorporates city-model priors for urban scene understanding. GS4City derives reliable image-aligned masks from Level of Detail (LoD) 3 CityGML models via two-pass raycasting, explicitly using parent-child relations to validate and recover fine-grained facade elements. It then fuses these geometry-grounded masks with foundation-model predictions to establish scene-consistent instance correspondences, and learns a compact identity encoding for each Gaussian under joint 2D identity supervision and 3D spatial regularization. Experiments on the TUM2TWIN and Gold Coast datasets show that GS4City effectively incorporates structured building semantics into Gaussian scene representations, outperforming existing 2D-driven semantic 3DGS baselines, including LangSplat and Gaga, by up to 15.8 IoU points in coarse building segmentation and 14.2 mIoU points in fine-grained semantic segmentation. By bridging structured city models and photorealistic Gaussian scene representations, GS4City enables semantically queryable and structure-aware urban reconstruction. Code is available at https://github.com/Jinyzzz/GS4City.  
- **📝翻译**: 最新的语义三维高斯喷溅（3DGS）方法主要依赖二维基础模型，常导致边界模糊且对结构化城市语义的支持有限。虽然像CityGML这样的城市模型将层级组织语义和建筑几何一起编码，但这些标签不能直接映射到高斯原语。我们介绍GS4City，一种层级语义高斯喷涂方法，结合城市模型先验以理解城市场景。GS4City 通过双遍光线投射，从 Level of Detail（LoD）3 CityGML 模型中推导出可靠的图像对齐遮罩，明确使用父子关系来验证和恢复细粒度的门面元素。然后，它将这些几何基础掩模与基础模型预测融合，建立场景一致的实例对应关系，并在联合二维恒等监督和三维空间正则化下，为每个高斯量学习紧致恒等编码。在TUM2TWIN和黄金海岸数据集上的实验显示，GS4City在高斯场景表示中有效将结构化建筑语义融入其中，在粗建筑分割中比现有的2D驱动语义3DGS基线（包括LangSplat和Gaga）多达15.8 IoU点，在细粒度语义分割中高出14.2 mIoU点。通过桥接结构化城市模型和照片级真实高斯场景表示，GS4City 实现了语义可查询且结构感知型的城市重建。代码可在 https://github.com/Jinyzzz/GS4City 获取。  

### [13] Naka-GS: A Bionics-inspired Dual-Branch Naka Correction and Progressive Point Pruning for Low-Light 3DGS  
- **⏳发布**：2026-04-13  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Runyu Zhu, SiXun Dong, Zhiqiang Zhang, Qingxia Ye, Zhihua Xu  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11142v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Low-light conditions severely hinder 3D restoration and reconstruction by degrading image visibility, introducing color distortions, and contaminating geometric priors for downstream optimization. We present NAKA-GS, a bionics-inspired framework for low-light 3D Gaussian Splatting that jointly improves photometric restoration and geometric initialization. Our method starts with a Naka-guided chroma-correction network, which combines physics-prior low-light enhancement, dual-branch input modeling, frequency-decoupled correction, and mask-guided optimization to suppress bright-region chromatic artifacts and edge-structure errors. The enhanced images are then fed into a feed-forward multi-view reconstruction model to produce dense scene priors. To further improve Gaussian initialization, we introduce a lightweight Point Preprocessing Module (PPM) that performs coordinate alignment, voxel pooling, and distance-adaptive progressive pruning to remove noisy and redundant points while preserving representative structures. Without introducing heavy inference overhead, NAKA-GS improves restoration quality, training stability, and optimization efficiency for low-light 3D reconstruction. The proposed method was presented in the NTIRE 3D Restoration and Reconstruction (3DRR) Challenge, and outperformed the baseline methods by a large margin. The code is available at https://github.com/RunyuZhu/Naka-GS  
- **📝翻译**: 低光环境严重阻碍三维恢复和重建，因为图像可见度下降，引入色彩畸变，并污染几何先验以促进后续优化。我们介绍了NAKA-GS，一个仿生技术启发的低光3D高斯喷溅框架，能够共同提升光度恢复和几何初始化。我们的方法始于Naka引导色度校正网络，结合了物理先验的低光增强、双分支输入建模、频率解耦校正和掩膜引导优化，以抑制明亮区域的色伪影和边缘结构误差。增强后的图像随后被输入前馈多视角重建模型，生成密集的场景先验。为进一步提升高斯初始化，我们引入了轻量级点预处理模块（PPM），该模块执行坐标对齐、体素池化和距离自适应渐进剪枝，以去除噪点和冗余点，同时保留代表性结构。NAKA-GS在不引入大量推断开销的情况下，提升了低光3D重建的恢复质量、训练稳定性和优化效率。该方法在NTIRE三维修复与重建（3DRR）挑战赛中展示，并远远优于基线方法。代码可在 https://github.com/RunyuZhu/Naka-GS 获取  

### [14] A 129FPS Full HD Real-Time Accelerator for 3D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2026-04-11  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Fang-Chi Chang, Tian-Sheuan Chang  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10223v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Rendering large-scale, unbounded scenes on AR/VR-class devices is constrained by the computation, bandwidth, and storage cost of 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS). We propose a low-power, low-cost 3DGS hardware accelerator that renders full-HD images in real time, together with a hardware-friendly compression pipeline that combines iterative Gaussian pruning and fine-tuning, progressive spherical harmonics (SH) degree reduction, and vector quantization of all SH coefficients and colors. The scheme achieves a $51.6\times$ model-size reduction with a 0.743 dB PSNR loss. The accelerator uses a frame-level pipeline that integrates point-based culling and projection with tile-based sorting and rasterization, skips zero-Jacobian matrix multiplications (reducing processing elements by 63\% and computation by 53\%), and adopts comparison-free tile-based sorting with deterministic latency. Implemented in a TSMC 28-nm process at 800 MHz, the design occupies $0.66~\text{mm}^2$ with 1.1438 M gates and 120 kB SRAM, consumes 0.219 W, and delivers 1219 Mpixels/J at 267.5 Mpixels/s, enabling 1080p at 129 FPS. Overall, it is $5.98\times$ smaller in area, $5.94\times$ higher throughput, and delivers $7.5\times$ higher energy efficiency than prior 3DGS accelerators.  
- **📝翻译**: 在AR/VR类设备上渲染大规模、无界场景受限于3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）的计算、带宽和存储成本。我们提出了一款低功耗、低成本的3DGS硬件加速器，能够实时渲染全高清图像，并结合了硬件友好型压缩流水线，结合了迭代高斯剪枝与微调、渐进球面谐波（SH）度数降低，以及所有SH系数和颜色的矢量量化。该方案实现了51.6美元×倍的模型体积缩小，PSNR损耗为0.743 dB。该加速器采用帧级流水线，将基于点的剔除和投影与基于瓦片的排序和栅格化整合，跳过零雅可比矩阵乘法（减少处理元素63%和计算减少53%），并采用无比较的基于瓦片的排序，具有确定性延迟。该设计采用台积电28纳米工艺，主频800 MHz，耗资0.66美元~\text{mm}^2$，门长1.1438 M，SRAM 120 kB 显存，功耗0.219瓦，输出1219 Mpixels/J，速度为267.5 Mpixels/s，支持1080p，帧率129 FPS。总体来看，面积比之前的3DGS加速器小5.98美元，吞吐量高5.94美元，能效提升7.5美元。  

### [15] PointSplat: Efficient Geometry-Driven Pruning and Transformer Refinement for 3D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2026-04-10  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Anh Thuan Tran, Jana Kosecka  
- **📝说明**：Accepted to CVPRW 2026 (3DMV)  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09903v1)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has recently unlocked real-time, high-fidelity novel view synthesis by representing scenes using explicit 3D primitives. However, traditional methods often require millions of Gaussians to capture complex scenes, leading to significant memory and storage demands. Recent approaches have addressed this issue through pruning and per-scene fine-tuning of Gaussian parameters, thereby reducing the model size while maintaining visual quality. These strategies typically rely on 2D images to compute important scores followed by scene-specific optimization. In this work, we introduce PointSplat, 3D geometry-driven prune-and-refine framework that bridges previously disjoint directions of gaussian pruning and transformer refinement. Our method includes two key components: (1) an efficient geometry-driven strategy that ranks Gaussians based solely on their 3D attributes, removing reliance on 2D images during pruning stage, and (2) a dual-branch encoder that separates, re-weights geometric and appearance to avoid feature imbalance. Extensive experiments on ScanNet++ and Replica across varying sparsity levels demonstrate that PointSplat consistently achieves competitive rendering quality and superior efficiency without additional per-scene optimization.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）最近通过使用显式3D原语来表示场景，解锁了实时、高保真的新颖视图合成功能。然而，传统方法通常需要数百万个高斯图来捕捉复杂场景，导致内存和存储需求较大。近期方法通过修剪和每场景微调高斯参数来解决这一问题，从而在保持视觉质量的同时减少模型规模。这些策略通常依赖二维图像计算重要分数，随后进行场景特定优化。在本研究中，我们介绍了PointSplat，一种三维几何驱动的修剪与细化框架，连接了之前不相交的高斯剪枝和变换器细化方向。我们的方法包含两个关键组成部分：（1）高效的几何驱动策略，仅根据高斯三维属性进行排名，去除修剪阶段对二维图像的依赖;（2）双分支编码器，分离并重新权重几何和外观，以避免特征不平衡。在 ScanNet++ 和 Replica 上，针对不同稀疏度水平进行了大量实验，表明 PointSplat 在无需额外逐场景优化的情况下，始终能够实现具有竞争力的渲染质量和卓越的效率。  

### [16] DOC-GS: Dual-Domain Observation and Calibration for Reliable Sparse-View Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2026-04-08  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Hantang Li, Qiang Zhu, Xiandong Meng, Debin Zhao, Xiaopeng Fan  
- **📝说明**：10 pages, 5 figures  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06739v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Sparse-view reconstruction with 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) is fundamentally ill-posed due to insufficient geometric supervision, often leading to severe overfitting and the emergence of structural distortions and translucent haze-like artifacts. While existing approaches attempt to alleviate this issue via dropout-based regularization, they are largely heuristic and lack a unified understanding of artifact formation. In this paper, we revisit sparse-view 3DGS reconstruction from a new perspective and identify the core challenge as the unobservability of Gaussian primitive reliability. Unreliable Gaussians are insufficiently constrained during optimization and accumulate as haze-like degradations in rendered images. Motivated by this observation, we propose a unified Dual-domain Observation and Calibration (DOC-GS) framework that models and corrects Gaussian reliability through the synergy of optimization-domain inductive bias and observation-domain evidence. Specifically, in the optimization domain, we characterize Gaussian reliability by the degree to which each primitive is constrained during training, and instantiate this signal via a Continuous Depth-Guided Dropout (CDGD) strategy, where the dropout probability serves as an explicit proxy for primitive reliability. This imposes a smooth depth-aware inductive bias to suppress weakly constrained Gaussians and improve optimization stability. In the observation domain, we establish a connection between floater artifacts and atmospheric scattering, and leverage the Dark Channel Prior (DCP) as a structural consistency cue to identify and accumulate anomalous regions. Based on cross-view aggregated evidence, we further design a reliability-driven geometric pruning strategy to remove low-confidence Gaussians.  
- **📝翻译**: 采用3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）的稀疏视图重建本质上是错态的，原因是几何监督不足，常导致严重的过拟合以及结构畸变和半透明的雾状伪影出现。虽然现有方法试图通过基于退出的正则化来缓解这一问题，但它们大多是启发式的，缺乏对人工物形成的统一理解。本文从新视角重新审视稀疏视3DGS重建，并指出核心挑战是高斯原始可靠性的不可观测性。不可靠的高斯分布在优化过程中约束不足，并在渲染图像中积累为类似雾霾的退化。基于这一观察，我们提出了一个统一的双域观察与校准（DOC-GS）框架，通过优化域归纳偏置与观察域证据的协同作用，建模并修正高斯信度。具体来说，在优化领域，我们通过训练过程中每个原语的约束程度来表征高斯信度，并通过连续深度引导丢弃（CDGD）策略实现该信号，其中丢弃概率作为原始可靠性的显式代理。这施加了平滑的深度感知归纳偏置，以抑制弱约束的高斯分布并提升优化稳定性。在观测领域，我们建立了漂浮伪影与大气散射之间的联系，并利用暗通道先验（DCP）作为结构一致性线索，识别和积累异常区域。基于交叉视图汇总证据，我们进一步设计了一种基于可靠性的几何剪枝策略，以去除低置信度高斯分布。  

### [17] GaussFly: Contrastive Reinforcement Learning for Visuomotor Policies in 3D Gaussian Fields  
- **⏳发布**：2026-04-06  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Yuhang Zhang, Mingsheng Li, Yujing Shang et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05062v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Learning visuomotor policies for Autonomous Aerial Vehicles (AAVs) relying solely on monocular vision is an attractive yet highly challenging paradigm. Existing end-to-end learning approaches directly map high-dimensional RGB observations to action commands, which frequently suffer from low sample efficiency and severe sim-to-real gaps due to the visual discrepancy between simulation and physical domains. To address these long-standing challenges, we propose GaussFly, a novel framework that explicitly decouples representation learning from policy optimization through a cohesive real-to-sim-to-real paradigm. First, to achieve a high-fidelity real-to-sim transition, we reconstruct training scenes using 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) augmented with explicit geometric constraints. Second, to ensure robust sim-to-real transfer, we leverage these photorealistic simulated environments and employ contrastive representation learning to extract compact, noise-resilient latent features from the rendered RGB images. By utilizing this pre-trained encoder to provide low-dimensional feature inputs, the computational burden on the visuomotor policy is significantly reduced while its resistance against visual noise is inherently enhanced. Extensive experiments in simulated and real-world environments demonstrate that GaussFly achieves superior sample efficiency and asymptotic performance compared to baselines. Crucially, it enables robust and zero-shot policy transfer to unseen real-world environments with complex textures, effectively bridging the sim-to-real gap.  
- **📝翻译**: 学习仅依赖单眼视觉的自主飞行器（AAV）视觉运动政策是一种既有吸引力又极具挑战性的范式。现有的端到端学习方法将高维RGB观测直接映射到动作命令，而动作指令常因模拟与物理域的视觉差异而存在低采样效率和严重的模拟与真实差距。为解决这些长期存在的挑战，我们提出了GaussFly，一种新颖框架，通过一个连贯的真实到模拟到真实的范式，明确将表征学习与策略优化解耦。首先，为了实现高保真度的真实到模拟转换，我们利用3D高斯溅射（3DGS）并辅以显式几何约束重建训练场景。其次，为确保稳健的模拟到实物传输，我们利用这些写实模拟环境，并采用对比表示学习，从渲染后的RGB图像中提取紧凑、抗噪的潜在特征。通过利用该预训练编码器提供低维特征输入，显著降低了视觉运动策略的计算负担，同时其对视觉噪声的抵抗力也得以固有增强。在模拟和现实环境中的大量实验表明，高斯飞在样本效率和渐近性能方面优于基线。关键是，它实现了稳健且零机会的政策传输到复杂纹理的现实世界中看不见的环境，有效弥合了模拟与现实之间的差距。  

### [18] Flash-Mono: Feed-Forward Accelerated Gaussian Splatting Monocular SLAM  
- **⏳发布**：2026-04-03  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Zicheng Zhang, Ke Wu, Xiangting Meng et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03092v1)   · [Project](https://victkk.github.io/flash-mono.)  
- **📝摘要**：Monocular 3D Gaussian Splatting SLAM suffers from critical limitations in time efficiency, geometric accuracy, and multi-view consistency. These issues stem from the time-consuming $\textit{Train-from-Scratch}$ optimization and the lack of inter-frame scale consistency from single-frame geometry priors. We contend that a feed-forward paradigm, leveraging multi-frame context to predict Gaussian attributes directly, is crucial for addressing these challenges. We present Flash-Mono, a system composed of three core modules: a feed-forward prediction frontend, a 2D Gaussian Splatting mapping backend, and an efficient hidden-state-based loop closure module. We trained a recurrent feed-forward frontend model that progressively aggregates multi-frame visual features into a hidden state via cross attention and jointly predicts camera poses and per-pixel Gaussian properties. By directly predicting Gaussian attributes, our method bypasses the burdensome per-frame optimization required in optimization-based GS-SLAM, achieving a $\textbf{10x}$ speedup while ensuring high-quality rendering. The power of our recurrent architecture extends beyond efficient prediction. The hidden states act as compact submap descriptors, facilitating efficient loop closure and global $\mathrm{Sim}(3)$ optimization to mitigate the long-standing challenge of drift. For enhanced geometric fidelity, we replace conventional 3D Gaussian ellipsoids with 2D Gaussian surfels. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Flash-Mono achieves state-of-the-art performance in both tracking and mapping quality, highlighting its potential for embodied perception and real-time reconstruction applications. Project page: https://victkk.github.io/flash-mono.  
- **📝翻译**: 单目三维高斯喷射SLAM在时间效率、几何精度和多视角一致性方面存在严重限制。这些问题源于耗时的$\textit{Train-from-Scratch}优化以及单帧几何事先验导致的帧间尺度一致性不足。我们认为，利用多帧上下文直接预测高斯属性的前馈范式对于应对这些挑战至关重要。我们介绍了Flash-Mono，这是一个由三个核心模块组成的系统：前馈预测前端、二维高斯Splatting映射后端，以及高效的基于隐藏态的环闭包模块。我们训练了一个循环前馈前端模型，通过交叉注意力逐步聚合多帧视觉特征到隐藏状态，并联合预测相机姿态和每像素高斯属性。通过直接预测高斯属性，我们的方法绕过了基于优化的GS-SLAM中繁重的每帧优化，实现了$\textbf{10x}$的加速，同时保证了高质量的渲染。我们循环架构的力量不仅限于高效预测。隐藏态作为紧致子映射描述符，促进高效的环闭和全局 $\mathrm{Sim}（3）$ 优化，以缓解漂移这一长期存在的挑战。为了增强几何精度，我们用二维高斯波面取代了传统的三维高斯椭球体。大量实验表明，Flash-Mono在跟踪和映射质量方面都达到了最先进的性能，凸显了其在具象感知和实时重建应用中的潜力。项目页面：https://victkk.github.io/flash-mono。  

### [19] SparseSplat: Towards Applicable Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting with Pixel-Unaligned Prediction  
- **⏳发布**：2026-04-03  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Zicheng Zhang, Xiangting Meng, Ke Wu, Wenchao Ding  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03069v1)   · [Project](https://victkk.github.io/SparseSplat-page/.)  
- **📝摘要**：Recent progress in feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has notably improved rendering quality. However, the spatially uniform and highly redundant 3DGS map generated by previous feed-forward 3DGS methods limits their integration into downstream reconstruction tasks. We propose SparseSplat, the first feed-forward 3DGS model that adaptively adjusts Gaussian density according to scene structure and information richness of local regions, yielding highly compact 3DGS maps. To achieve this, we propose entropy-based probabilistic sampling, generating large, sparse Gaussians in textureless areas and assigning small, dense Gaussians to regions with rich information. Additionally, we designed a specialized point cloud network that efficiently encodes local context and decodes it into 3DGS attributes, addressing the receptive field mismatch between the general 3DGS optimization pipeline and feed-forward models. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that SparseSplat can achieve state-of-the-art rendering quality with only 22% of the Gaussians and maintain reasonable rendering quality with only 1.5% of the Gaussians. Project page: https://victkk.github.io/SparseSplat-page/.  
- **📝翻译**: 前馈3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）的最新进展显著提升了渲染质量。然而，先前前馈3DGS方法生成的空间均匀且高度冗余的3DGS映射限制了它们与后续重建任务的整合。我们提出了SparseSplat，这是首个前馈3DGS模型，能够根据场景结构和局部区域信息丰富度自适应调整高斯密度，生成高度紧凑的3DGS映射。为此，我们提出了基于熵的概率采样，在无纹理区域生成大型稀疏高斯分布，并将小而密集的高斯分布分配到信息丰富的区域。此外，我们设计了一个专用点云网络，高效编码局部上下文并将其解码为3DGS属性，解决了3DGS优化流水线与前馈模型之间的感受野不匹配问题。大量实验结果表明，SparseSplat 仅用 22% 的高斯算数就能达到最先进的渲染质量，仅用 1.5% 的高斯算数也能保持合理的渲染质量。项目页面：https://victkk.github.io/SparseSplat-page/。  

### [20] GS^2: Graph-based Spatial Distribution Optimization for Compact 3D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2026-04-02  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Xianben Yang, Tao Wang, Yuxuan Li, Yi Jin, Haibin Ling  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01884v1)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has demonstrated breakthrough performance in novel view synthesis and real-time rendering. Nevertheless, its practicality is constrained by the high memory cost due to a huge number of Gaussian points. Many pruning-based 3DGS variants have been proposed for memory saving, but often compromise spatial consistency and may lead to rendering artifacts. To address this issue, we propose graph-based spatial distribution optimization for compact 3D Gaussian Splatting (GS\textasciicircum2), which enhances reconstruction quality by optimizing the spatial distribution of Gaussian points. Specifically, we introduce an evidence lower bound (ELBO)-based adaptive densification strategy that automatically controls the densification process. In addition, an opacity-aware progressive pruning strategy is proposed to further reduce memory consumption by dynamically removing low-opacity Gaussian points. Furthermore, we propose a graph-based feature encoding module to adjust the spatial distribution via feature-guided point shifting. Extensive experiments validate that GS\textasciicircum2 achieves a compact Gaussian representation while delivering superior rendering quality. Compared with 3DGS, it achieves higher PSNR with only about 12.5\% Gaussian points. Furthermore, it outperforms all compared baselines in both rendering quality and memory efficiency.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）在新颖视图合成和实时渲染方面展现了突破性的性能。然而，由于大量高斯点，其实用性受到高内存消耗的限制。许多基于剪枝的3DGS变体被提出用于节省内存，但通常会破坏空间一致性，并可能导致渲染伪影。为解决这一问题，我们提出了基于图的空间分布优化方法，用于紧凑三维高斯散布（GS\textasciicircum2），通过优化高斯点的空间分布来提升重建质量。具体来说，我们引入了一种基于证据下界（ELBO）的自适应致密策略，能够自动控制致密过程。此外，还提出了一种不透明度感知的渐进剪枝策略，通过动态移除低不透明度的高斯点，进一步减少内存消耗。此外，我们提出了一个基于图的特征编码模块，通过特征引导点位移调整空间分布。大量实验验证了GS\textasciicircum2在实现紧凑高斯表示的同时，呈现出更优越的渲染质量。与3DGS相比，它以约12.5%的高斯点数获得更高的PSNR。此外，它在渲染质量和内存效率方面都优于所有对比基线。  

### [21] FaCT-GS: Fast and Scalable CT Reconstruction with Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2026-04-02  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Pawel Tomasz Pieta, Rasmus Juul Pedersen, Sina Borgi et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01844v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Gaussian Splatting (GS) has emerged as a dominating technique for image rendering and has quickly been adapted for the X-ray Computed Tomography (CT) reconstruction task. However, despite being on par or better than many of its predecessors, the benefits of GS are typically not substantial enough to motivate a transition from well-established reconstruction algorithms. This paper addresses the most significant remaining limitations of the GS-based approach by introducing FaCT-GS, a framework for fast and flexible CT reconstruction. Enabled by an in-depth optimization of the voxelization and rasterization pipelines, our new method is significantly faster than its predecessors and scales well with projection and output volume size. Furthermore, the improved voxelization enables rapid fitting of Gaussians to pre-existing volumes, which can serve as a prior for warm-starting the reconstruction, or simply as an alternative, compressed representation. FaCT-GS is over 4X faster than the State of the Art GS CT reconstruction on standard 512x512 projections, and over 13X faster on 2k projections. Implementation available at: https://github.com/PaPieta/fact-gs.  
- **📝翻译**: 高斯喷射（GS）已成为图像渲染的主流技术，并迅速被应用于X射线计算机断层扫描（CT）重建任务。然而，尽管GS与许多前辈持平甚至更好，其优势通常不足以促使人们从成熟的重建算法转变。本文通过介绍FaCT-GS框架，解决基于GS方法的最显著剩余局限性，这一快速且灵活的CT重建框架。通过对体素化和光栅化流水线的深入优化，我们的新方法比前辈更快，并且随着投影和输出体积大小的扩展性较强。此外，改进的体素化使高斯量能够快速拟合到已有体积，既可作为预热启动重建的先验，也可作为替代的压缩表示。FaCT-GS在标准512x512投影上比最先进的GS CT重建快4倍以上，在2k投影上快13倍以上。实现可于：https://github.com/PaPieta/fact-gs。  

### [22] Autoregressive Appearance Prediction for 3D Gaussian Avatars  
- **⏳发布**：2026-04-01  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Michael Steiner, Zhang Chen, Alexander Richard et al.  
- **📝说明**：Project Page: https://steimich96.github.io/AAP-3DGA/  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00928v1)    
- **📝摘要**：A photorealistic and immersive human avatar experience demands capturing fine, person-specific details such as cloth and hair dynamics, subtle facial expressions, and characteristic motion patterns. Achieving this requires large, high-quality datasets, which often introduce ambiguities and spurious correlations when very similar poses correspond to different appearances. Models that fit these details during training can overfit and produce unstable, abrupt appearance changes for novel poses. We propose a 3D Gaussian Splatting avatar model with a spatial MLP backbone that is conditioned on both pose and an appearance latent. The latent is learned during training by an encoder, yielding a compact representation that improves reconstruction quality and helps disambiguate pose-driven renderings. At driving time, our predictor autoregressively infers the latent, producing temporally smooth appearance evolution and improved stability. Overall, our method delivers a robust and practical path to high-fidelity, stable avatar driving.  
- **📝翻译**: 一种写实且沉浸式的人形虚拟形象体验需要捕捉细致且个性化的细节，如衣物和头发动态、细微的面部表情以及典型的运动模式。实现这一点需要大量高质量的数据集，当非常相似的姿势对应不同的外貌时，常常会引入歧义和虚假的相关性。在训练中符合这些细节的模型可能会过拟合，导致不稳定、突兀的外观变化，适合新颖的姿势。我们提出了一个3D高斯喷溅头像模型，其空间MLP骨干基于姿势和潜在的外观。潜在值在编码器训练过程中被学习，产生紧凑的表示，提升重建质量并帮助消除姿态驱动渲染的歧义。在驾驶时，我们的预测器会自回归推断潜在因素，从而实现时间上的平滑外观演变和更好的稳定性。总体而言，我们的方法提供了一条稳健且实用的路径，实现高保真、稳定的虚拟形象驾驶。  

### [23] Compact Keyframe-Optimized Multi-Agent Gaussian Splatting SLAM  
- **⏳发布**：2026-04-01  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Monica M. Q. Li, Pierre-Yves Lajoie, Jialiang Liu, Giovanni Beltrame  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00804v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Efficient multi-agent 3D mapping is essential for robotic teams operating in unknown environments, but dense representations hinder real-time exchange over constrained communication links. In multi-agent Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM), systems typically rely on a centralized server to merge and optimize the local maps produced by individual agents. However, sharing these large map representations, particularly those generated by recent methods such as Gaussian Splatting, becomes a bottleneck in real-world scenarios with limited bandwidth. We present an improved multi-agent RGB-D Gaussian Splatting SLAM framework that reduces communication load while preserving map fidelity. First, we incorporate a compaction step into our SLAM system to remove redundant 3D Gaussians, without degrading the rendering quality. Second, our approach performs centralized loop closure computation without initial guess, operating in two modes: a pure rendered-depth mode that requires no data beyond the 3D Gaussians, and a camera-depth mode that includes lightweight depth images for improved registration accuracy and additional Gaussian pruning. Evaluation on both synthetic and real-world datasets shows up to 85-95\% reduction in transmitted data compared to state-of-the-art approaches in both modes, bringing 3D Gaussian multi-agent SLAM closer to practical deployment in real-world scenarios. Code: https://github.com/lemonci/coko-slam
