- **📝摘要**：The field of novel-view synthesis has recently witnessed the emergence of 3D Gaussian Splatting, which represents scenes in a point-based manner and renders through rasterization. This methodology, in contrast to Radiance Fields that rely on ray tracing, demonstrates superior rendering quality and speed. However, the explicit and unstructured nature of 3D Gaussians poses a significant storage challenge, impeding its broader application. To address this challenge, we introduce the Gaussian-Forest modeling framework, which hierarchically represents a scene as a forest of hybrid 3D Gaussians. Each hybrid Gaussian retains its unique explicit attributes while sharing implicit ones with its sibling Gaussians, thus optimizing parameterization with significantly fewer variables. Moreover, adaptive growth and pruning strategies are designed, ensuring detailed representation in complex regions and a notable reduction in the number of required Gaussians. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Gaussian-Forest not only maintains comparable speed and quality but also achieves a compression rate surpassing 10 times, marking a significant advancement in efficient scene modeling. Codes will be available at https://github.com/Xian-Bei/GaussianForest.  
- **📝翻译**: 新颖视角综合领域最近出现了三维高斯喷涂技术，它以基于点的方式表示场景，并通过光栅化渲染。与依赖光线追踪的辐射场相比，这种方法展现了更优越的渲染质量和速度。然而，3D高斯的显式且非结构化特性带来了重大存储挑战，阻碍了其更广泛的应用。为应对这一挑战，我们引入了高斯-森林建模框架，该框架以层级方式表示场景为一片混合三维高斯森林。每个混合高斯分布表表都保留了其独特的显式属性，同时与其兄弟高斯分布共享隐式属性，从而以显著减少的变量优化参数化。此外，设计了适应性生长和修剪策略，确保复杂区域的详细表示，显著减少所需高斯量。大量实验表明，高斯-森林不仅保持了相当的速度和质量，压缩率还超过10倍，标志着高效场景建模的重大进展。兑换码将于 https://github.com/Xian-Bei/GaussianForest 年提供。  

### [11] Generative Gaussian Splatting for Unbounded 3D City Generation  
- **⏳发布**：2024-06-10（更新：2025-02-27）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Haozhe Xie, Zhaoxi Chen, Fangzhou Hong, Ziwei Liu  
- **📝说明**：CVPR 2025. Project Page: https://haozhexie.com/project/gaussian-city  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.06526v3)    
- **📝摘要**：3D city generation with NeRF-based methods shows promising generation results but is computationally inefficient. Recently 3D Gaussian Splatting (3D-GS) has emerged as a highly efficient alternative for object-level 3D generation. However, adapting 3D-GS from finite-scale 3D objects and humans to infinite-scale 3D cities is non-trivial. Unbounded 3D city generation entails significant storage overhead (out-of-memory issues), arising from the need to expand points to billions, often demanding hundreds of Gigabytes of VRAM for a city scene spanning 10km^2. In this paper, we propose GaussianCity, a generative Gaussian Splatting framework dedicated to efficiently synthesizing unbounded 3D cities with a single feed-forward pass. Our key insights are two-fold: 1) Compact 3D Scene Representation: We introduce BEV-Point as a highly compact intermediate representation, ensuring that the growth in VRAM usage for unbounded scenes remains constant, thus enabling unbounded city generation. 2) Spatial-aware Gaussian Attribute Decoder: We present spatial-aware BEV-Point decoder to produce 3D Gaussian attributes, which leverages Point Serializer to integrate the structural and contextual characteristics of BEV points. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GaussianCity achieves state-of-the-art results in both drone-view and street-view 3D city generation. Notably, compared to CityDreamer, GaussianCity exhibits superior performance with a speedup of 60 times (10.72 FPS v.s. 0.18 FPS).  
- **📝翻译**: 基于NeRF的方法进行三维城市生成显示出有前景的生成效果，但计算效率较低。近年来，3D高斯喷溅（3D-GS）作为对象级三维生成的高效替代方案出现。然而，将3D-GS从有限尺度的3D物体和人类应用到无限尺度的3D城市并非易事。无界3D城市生成带来了显著的存储开销（内存不足问题），这源于需要将点扩展到数十亿个点，通常需要数百GB的显存来覆盖10km^2的城市场景。本文提出了高斯城（GaussianCity），一种生成式高斯喷溅框架，致力于通过单次前馈传递高效合成无界三维城市。我们的核心见解有两个方面：1）紧凑的3D场景表示：我们将BEV-Point介绍为高度紧凑的中间表示，确保无界场景的显存使用增长保持稳定，从而实现无界城市生成。2）空间感知高斯属性解码器：我们提供空间感知BEV点解码器，生成3D高斯属性，利用点串行器整合BEV点的结构和上下文特性。大量实验表明，GaussianCity 在无人机视角和街景 3D 城市生成方面都取得了最先进的性能。值得注意的是，与《CityDreamer》相比，GaussianCity的性能更优，速度提升了60倍（10.72帧对0.18帧）。  

### [12] Improving Gaussian Splatting with Localized Points Management  
- **⏳发布**：2024-06-06（更新：2025-04-19）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Haosen Yang, Chenhao Zhang, Wenqing Wang et al.  
- **📝说明**：CVPR 2025 (Highlight). Github: https://happy-hsy.github.io/projects/LPM/  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.04251v3)    
- **📝摘要**：Point management is critical for optimizing 3D Gaussian Splatting models, as point initiation (e.g., via structure from motion) is often distributionally inappropriate. Typically, Adaptive Density Control (ADC) algorithm is adopted, leveraging view-averaged gradient magnitude thresholding for point densification, opacity thresholding for pruning, and regular all-points opacity reset. We reveal that this strategy is limited in tackling intricate/special image regions (e.g., transparent) due to inability of identifying all 3D zones requiring point densification, and lacking an appropriate mechanism to handle ill-conditioned points with negative impacts (e.g., occlusion due to false high opacity). To address these limitations, we propose a Localized Point Management (LPM) strategy, capable of identifying those error-contributing zones in greatest need for both point addition and geometry calibration. Zone identification is achieved by leveraging the underlying multiview geometry constraints, subject to image rendering errors. We apply point densification in the identified zones and then reset the opacity of the points in front of these regions, creating a new opportunity to correct poorly conditioned points. Serving as a versatile plugin, LPM can be seamlessly integrated into existing static 3D and dynamic 4D Gaussian Splatting models with minimal additional cost. Experimental evaluations validate the efficacy of our LPM in boosting a variety of existing 3D/4D models both quantitatively and qualitatively. Notably, LPM improves both static 3DGS and dynamic SpaceTimeGS to achieve state-of-the-art rendering quality while retaining real-time speeds, excelling on challenging datasets such as Tanks & Temples and the Neural 3D Video dataset.  
- **📝翻译**: 点管理对于优化三维高斯散落模型至关重要，因为点起始（例如通过运动结构）通常分布上不合适。通常采用自适应密度控制（ADC）算法，利用视均梯度幅度阈值进行点密度化，不透明度阈值进行剪枝，以及常规的全点不透明度重置。我们发现，由于无法识别所有需要点密度化的三维区域，且缺乏处理条件不良点（如因虚高不透明度导致遮挡）的机制，该策略在处理复杂/特殊图像区域（如透明）方面存在限制。为解决这些局限性，我们提出了一种局部点管理（LPM）策略，能够识别那些最需要点加法和几何校准的误差来源区域。区域识别通过利用底层的多视图几何约束实现，但存在图像渲染错误。我们在已识别的区域进行点密度化，然后重置这些区域前方点的不透明度，创造了修正条件不佳点的新机会。作为多功能插件，LPM可以无缝集成到现有的静态3D和动态4D高斯喷涂模型中，额外成本极低。实验评估验证了我们LPM在定量和定性上提升多种现有3D/4D模型的有效性。值得注意的是，LPM改进了静态3DGS和动态时空GS，实现了最先进的渲染质量，同时保持实时速度，在Tanks & Temples和神经3D视频数据集等具有挑战性的数据集上表现出色。  

### [13] Dynamic 3D Gaussian Fields for Urban Areas  
- **⏳发布**：2024-06-05（更新：2024-11-23）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Tobias Fischer, Jonas Kulhanek, Samuel Rota Bulò et al.  
- **📝说明**：NeurIPS'24 spotlight. Project page is available at https://tobiasfshr.github.io/pub/4dgf/  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.03175v2)    
- **📝摘要**：We present an efficient neural 3D scene representation for novel-view synthesis (NVS) in large-scale, dynamic urban areas. Existing works are not well suited for applications like mixed-reality or closed-loop simulation due to their limited visual quality and non-interactive rendering speeds. Recently, rasterization-based approaches have achieved high-quality NVS at impressive speeds. However, these methods are limited to small-scale, homogeneous data, i.e. they cannot handle severe appearance and geometry variations due to weather, season, and lighting and do not scale to larger, dynamic areas with thousands of images. We propose 4DGF, a neural scene representation that scales to large-scale dynamic urban areas, handles heterogeneous input data, and substantially improves rendering speeds. We use 3D Gaussians as an efficient geometry scaffold while relying on neural fields as a compact and flexible appearance model. We integrate scene dynamics via a scene graph at global scale while modeling articulated motions on a local level via deformations. This decomposed approach enables flexible scene composition suitable for real-world applications. In experiments, we surpass the state-of-the-art by over 3 dB in PSNR and more than 200 times in rendering speed.  
- **📝翻译**: 我们提出了一种高效的神经三维场景表示，用于大规模动态城市区域的新视角综合（NVS）。现有作品由于视觉质量有限且渲染速度非交互，不适合混合现实或闭环模拟等应用。近年来，基于光栅化的方法以令人印象深刻的速度实现了高质量的NVS。然而，这些方法仅限于小尺度、均匀的数据，即无法处理因天气、季节和光照引起的严重外观和几何变化，也无法扩展到拥有数千张图像的更大动态区域。我们提出了4DGF，一种神经场景表示，能够扩展到大规模动态城市区域，处理异构输入数据，并大幅提升渲染速度。我们使用三维高斯算作为高效的几何支架，同时依赖神经场作为紧凑且灵活的外观模型。我们通过全局尺度的场景图整合场景动力学，同时通过变形在局部层面建模关节运动。这种分解方法使场景构图变得灵活，适用于现实应用。在实验中，我们在PSNR上超越了最先进的3 dB以上，渲染速度提升了200多倍。  


## May 2024

### [1] ContextGS: Compact 3D Gaussian Splatting with Anchor Level Context Model  
- **⏳发布**：2024-05-31  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Yufei Wang, Zhihao Li, Lanqing Guo et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.20721v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Recently, 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has become a promising framework for novel view synthesis, offering fast rendering speeds and high fidelity. However, the large number of Gaussians and their associated attributes require effective compression techniques. Existing methods primarily compress neural Gaussians individually and independently, i.e., coding all the neural Gaussians at the same time, with little design for their interactions and spatial dependence. Inspired by the effectiveness of the context model in image compression, we propose the first autoregressive model at the anchor level for 3DGS compression in this work. We divide anchors into different levels and the anchors that are not coded yet can be predicted based on the already coded ones in all the coarser levels, leading to more accurate modeling and higher coding efficiency. To further improve the efficiency of entropy coding, e.g., to code the coarsest level with no already coded anchors, we propose to introduce a low-dimensional quantized feature as the hyperprior for each anchor, which can be effectively compressed. Our work pioneers the context model in the anchor level for 3DGS representation, yielding an impressive size reduction of over 100 times compared to vanilla 3DGS and 15 times compared to the most recent state-of-the-art work Scaffold-GS, while achieving comparable or even higher rendering quality.  
- **📝翻译**: 近年来，3D高斯喷涂（3DGS）已成为新颖视图合成的有前景框架，提供快速渲染速度和高保真度。然而，大量高斯及其相关属性需要有效的压缩技术。现有方法主要单独且独立地压缩神经高斯，即同时编码所有神经高斯，几乎没有设计它们的相互作用和空间依赖性。受上下文模型在图像压缩中的有效性启发，我们提出了3DGS压缩锚点层面的首个自回归模型。我们将锚点划分为不同层级，尚未编码的锚点可以根据所有粗层中已编码的锚点进行预测，从而实现更准确的建模和更高的编码效率。为了进一步提高熵编码的效率，例如编码最粗的层级且没有已编码锚点，我们提出引入一个低维量子化特征作为每个锚点的超先验，该特征可以有效压缩。我们的工作开创了3DGS表示的锚点层上下文模型，与原版3DGS相比，尺寸减少了100多倍，与最新最先进的Scaffold-GS相比减少了15倍，同时实现了相当甚至更高的渲染质量。  

### [2] GaussianRoom: Improving 3D Gaussian Splatting with SDF Guidance and Monocular Cues for Indoor Scene Reconstruction  
- **⏳发布**：2024-05-30（更新：2025-04-01）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Haodong Xiang, Xinghui Li, Kai Cheng et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.19671v2)    
- **📝摘要**：Embodied intelligence requires precise reconstruction and rendering to simulate large-scale real-world data. Although 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has recently demonstrated high-quality results with real-time performance, it still faces challenges in indoor scenes with large, textureless regions, resulting in incomplete and noisy reconstructions due to poor point cloud initialization and underconstrained optimization. Inspired by the continuity of signed distance field (SDF), which naturally has advantages in modeling surfaces, we propose a unified optimization framework that integrates neural signed distance fields (SDFs) with 3DGS for accurate geometry reconstruction and real-time rendering. This framework incorporates a neural SDF field to guide the densification and pruning of Gaussians, enabling Gaussians to model scenes accurately even with poor initialized point clouds. Simultaneously, the geometry represented by Gaussians improves the efficiency of the SDF field by piloting its point sampling. Additionally, we introduce two regularization terms based on normal and edge priors to resolve geometric ambiguities in textureless areas and enhance detail accuracy. Extensive experiments in ScanNet and ScanNet++ show that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in both surface reconstruction and novel view synthesis.  
- **📝翻译**: 具身智能需要精确的重建和渲染，以模拟大规模的真实世界数据。尽管3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）最近展示了高质量的实时性能，但在室内场景中具有大面积且无纹理区域时仍面临挑战，导致由于点云初始化不佳和优化受限不足，重建过程不完整且噪声较大。受带符号距离场（SDF）连续性（SDF）在曲面建模中自然具有优势的启发，我们提出了一个统一的优化框架，将神经带符号距离场（SDF）与3DGS集成，实现精确的几何重建和实时渲染。该框架包含神经SDF场，指导高斯分布的密度化和修剪，使高斯分布即使在初始化点云较差的情况下也能准确建模场景。同时，高斯分布表示的几何通过试点采样提高了SDF场的效率。此外，我们引入了基于法线和边缘先验的两个正则化项，以解决无纹理区域的几何模糊性并提升细节精度。ScanNet和ScanNet++中的大量实验表明，我们的方法在曲面重建和新颖视图综合方面都达到了最先进的性能。  

### [3] LP-3DGS: Learning to Prune 3D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2024-05-29  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Zhaoliang Zhang, Tianchen Song, Yongjae Lee et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.18784v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Recently, 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has become one of the mainstream methodologies for novel view synthesis (NVS) due to its high quality and fast rendering speed. However, as a point-based scene representation, 3DGS potentially generates a large number of Gaussians to fit the scene, leading to high memory usage. Improvements that have been proposed require either an empirical and preset pruning ratio or importance score threshold to prune the point cloud. Such hyperparamter requires multiple rounds of training to optimize and achieve the maximum pruning ratio, while maintaining the rendering quality for each scene. In this work, we propose learning-to-prune 3DGS (LP-3DGS), where a trainable binary mask is applied to the importance score that can find optimal pruning ratio automatically. Instead of using the traditional straight-through estimator (STE) method to approximate the binary mask gradient, we redesign the masking function to leverage the Gumbel-Sigmoid method, making it differentiable and compatible with the existing training process of 3DGS. Extensive experiments have shown that LP-3DGS consistently produces a good balance that is both efficient and high quality.  
- **📝翻译**: 近年来，3D高斯拼拍（3DGS）因其高质量和快速渲染速度，已成为新颖视图合成（NVS）的主流方法之一。然而，作为基于点的场景表示，3DGS可能会生成大量高斯以适应场景，导致内存使用较高。已提出的改进方案需要一个经验且预设的剪枝比率或重要性评分阈值来修剪点云。这种超参数需要多次训练，以优化并实现最大剪枝比，同时保持每个场景的渲染质量。在本研究中，我们提出了学习修剪3DGS（LP-3DGS）方法，即对重要性评分应用可训练的二元遮罩，从而自动找到最优修剪比率。我们不再使用传统的直通估计器（STE）方法来近似二元遮罩梯度，而是重新设计了遮罩函数，利用Gumbel-Sigmoid方法，使其可微且兼容3DGS现有的训练过程。大量实验表明，LP-3DGS始终能够实现高效且高质量的良好平衡。  

### [4] A Refined 3D Gaussian Representation for High-Quality Dynamic Scene Reconstruction  
- **⏳发布**：2024-05-28  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Bin Zhang, Bi Zeng, Zexin Peng  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.17891v1)    
- **📝摘要**：In recent years, Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) has revolutionized three-dimensional (3D) reconstruction with its implicit representation. Building upon NeRF, 3D Gaussian Splatting (3D-GS) has departed from the implicit representation of neural networks and instead directly represents scenes as point clouds with Gaussian-shaped distributions. While this shift has notably elevated the rendering quality and speed of radiance fields but inevitably led to a significant increase in memory usage. Additionally, effectively rendering dynamic scenes in 3D-GS has emerged as a pressing challenge. To address these concerns, this paper purposes a refined 3D Gaussian representation for high-quality dynamic scene reconstruction. Firstly, we use a deformable multi-layer perceptron (MLP) network to capture the dynamic offset of Gaussian points and express the color features of points through hash encoding and a tiny MLP to reduce storage requirements. Subsequently, we introduce a learnable denoising mask coupled with denoising loss to eliminate noise points from the scene, thereby further compressing 3D Gaussian model. Finally, motion noise of points is mitigated through static constraints and motion consistency constraints. Experimental results demonstrate that our method surpasses existing approaches in rendering quality and speed, while significantly reducing the memory usage associated with 3D-GS, making it highly suitable for various tasks such as novel view synthesis, and dynamic mapping.  
- **📝翻译**: 近年来，神经辐射场（NeRF）以其隐含的表示方式彻底革新了三维（3D）重建。基于NeRF，3D高斯喷溅（3D-GS）已摒弃了神经网络的隐式表示，直接将场景表示为带有高斯分布的点云。虽然这一变化显著提升了辐射场的渲染质量和速度，但不可避免地导致内存使用显著增加。此外，在3D-GS中有效渲染动态场景也成为了一个紧迫的挑战。为解决这些问题，本文采用了精细的三维高斯表示法，以实现高质量的动态场景重建。首先，我们使用可变形多层感知器（MLP）网络捕捉高斯点的动态偏移，并通过哈希编码和极小的MLP表达点的颜色特征，以减少存储需求。随后，我们引入可学习的去噪掩模与去噪损耗结合，以消除场景中的噪点，从而进一步压缩三维高斯模型。最后，点的运动噪声通过静态约束和运动一致性约束得到缓解。实验结果表明，我们的方法在渲染质量和速度方面超越了现有方法，同时显著降低了3D-GS相关的内存使用，使其非常适合新颖视图综合和动态映射等多种任务。  

### [5] SafeguardGS: 3D Gaussian Primitive Pruning While Avoiding Catastrophic Scene Destruction  
- **⏳发布**：2024-05-28（更新：2024-11-22）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Yongjae Lee, Zhaoliang Zhang, Deliang Fan  
- **📝说明**：19 pages, 20 figures, 7 tables  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.17793v2)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has made significant strides in novel view synthesis. However, its suboptimal densification process results in the excessively large number of Gaussian primitives, which impacts frame-per-second and increases memory usage, making it unsuitable for low-end devices. To address this issue, many follow-up studies have proposed various pruning techniques with score functions designed to identify and remove less important primitives. Nonetheless, a comprehensive discussion of their effectiveness and implications across all techniques is missing. In this paper, we are the first to categorize 3DGS pruning techniques into two types: Scene-level pruning and Pixel-level pruning, distinguished by their scope for ranking primitives. Our subsequent experiments reveal that, while scene-level pruning leads to disastrous quality drops under extreme decimation of Gaussian primitives, pixel-level pruning not only sustains relatively high rendering quality with minuscule performance degradation but also provides an inherent boundary of pruning, i.e., a safeguard of Gaussian pruning. Building on this observation, we further propose multiple variations of score functions based on the factors of rendering equations and discover that assessing based on color similarity with blending weight is the most effective method for discriminating insignificant primitives. In our experiments, our SafeguardGS with the optimal score function shows the highest PSNR-per-primitive performance under an extreme pruning setting, retaining only about 10% of the primitives from the original 3DGS scene (i.e., 10x compression ratio). We believe our research provides valuable insights for optimizing 3DGS for future works.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）在小说视图合成方面取得了显著进展。然而，其密度化过程不理想，导致高斯原语数量过多，影响帧率并增加内存使用，使其不适合低端设备。为解决这一问题，许多后续研究提出了多种剪枝技术，并结合评分函数，旨在识别和去除次要的原语。然而，缺乏对其在所有技术中有效性和影响的全面讨论。本文首次将3DGS剪枝技术分为两类：场景级剪枝和像素级剪枝，区别于其对原语排序的范围。我们后续的实验显示，场景层级剪枝在高斯原语极端毁灭下会导致严重的质量下降，而像素级剪枝不仅能以极小的性能下降维持相对较高的渲染质量，还为剪枝提供了固有的边界，即高斯剪枝的保障。基于这一观察，我们进一步提出了基于渲染方程因子的多种评分函数变体，并发现基于颜色相似度与混合权重进行评估是区分无显著基元最有效的方法。在我们的实验中，带有最优评分函数的SafeguardGS在极端修剪设置下显示出最高的PSNR每个基元性能，仅保留了原始3DGS场景约10%的原件（即10倍压缩比）。我们相信我们的研究为优化3DGS未来工作提供了宝贵见解。  

### [6] GOI: Find 3D Gaussians of Interest with an Optimizable Open-vocabulary Semantic-space Hyperplane  
- **⏳发布**：2024-05-27（更新：2024-07-27）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Yansong Qu, Shaohui Dai, Xinyang Li et al.  
- **📝说明**：Our project page is available at https://quyans.github.io/GOI-Hyperplane/  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.17596v2)   · [Project](https://quyans.github.io/GOI-Hyperplane/)  
- **📝摘要**：3D open-vocabulary scene understanding, crucial for advancing augmented reality and robotic applications, involves interpreting and locating specific regions within a 3D space as directed by natural language instructions. To this end, we introduce GOI, a framework that integrates semantic features from 2D vision-language foundation models into 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) and identifies 3D Gaussians of Interest using an Optimizable Semantic-space Hyperplane. Our approach includes an efficient compression method that utilizes scene priors to condense noisy high-dimensional semantic features into compact low-dimensional vectors, which are subsequently embedded in 3DGS. During the open-vocabulary querying process, we adopt a distinct approach compared to existing methods, which depend on a manually set fixed empirical threshold to select regions based on their semantic feature distance to the query text embedding. This traditional approach often lacks universal accuracy, leading to challenges in precisely identifying specific target areas. Instead, our method treats the feature selection process as a hyperplane division within the feature space, retaining only those features that are highly relevant to the query. We leverage off-the-shelf 2D Referring Expression Segmentation (RES) models to fine-tune the semantic-space hyperplane, enabling a more precise distinction between target regions and others. This fine-tuning substantially improves the accuracy of open-vocabulary queries, ensuring the precise localization of pertinent 3D Gaussians. Extensive experiments demonstrate GOI's superiority over previous state-of-the-art methods. Our project page is available at https://quyans.github.io/GOI-Hyperplane/ .  
- **📝翻译**: 三维开放词汇场景理解对于推进增强现实和机器人应用至关重要，涉及根据自然语言指令解释和定位三维空间中的特定区域。为此，我们引入了GOI，这是一个将二维视觉语言基础模型的语义特征整合到三维高斯喷射（3DGS）中的框架，并通过可优化语义空间超平面识别感兴趣的三维高斯图。我们的方法包括一种高效的压缩方法，利用场景先验将噪声高维语义特征浓缩为紧凑的低维向量，这些向量随后嵌入到3DGS中。在开放词汇查询过程中，我们采用了与现有方法不同的方法，现有方法依赖手动设定固定的经验阈值，根据其语义特征距离与查询文本嵌入选择区域。这种传统方法常缺乏普遍准确性，导致精准识别特定目标区域存在挑战。相反，我们的方法将特征选择过程视为特征空间中的超平面划分，只保留与查询高度相关的特征。我们利用现成的二维引用表达切割（RES）模型微调语义空间超平面，从而实现目标区域与其他区域的更精确区分。这种微调大大提高了开放词汇查询的准确性，确保相关三维高斯的精确定位。大量实验证明了印度政府优于以往最先进方法的优势。我们的项目页面可在 https://quyans.github.io/GOI-Hyperplane/ 访问。  

### [7] F-3DGS: Factorized Coordinates and Representations for 3D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2024-05-27（更新：2024-05-28）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Xiangyu Sun, Joo Chan Lee, Daniel Rho et al.  
- **📝说明**：Our project page including code is available at https://xiangyu1sun.github.io/Factorize-3DGS/  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.17083v2)    
- **📝摘要**：The neural radiance field (NeRF) has made significant strides in representing 3D scenes and synthesizing novel views. Despite its advancements, the high computational costs of NeRF have posed challenges for its deployment in resource-constrained environments and real-time applications. As an alternative to NeRF-like neural rendering methods, 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) offers rapid rendering speeds while maintaining excellent image quality. However, as it represents objects and scenes using a myriad of Gaussians, it requires substantial storage to achieve high-quality representation. To mitigate the storage overhead, we propose Factorized 3D Gaussian Splatting (F-3DGS), a novel approach that drastically reduces storage requirements while preserving image quality. Inspired by classical matrix and tensor factorization techniques, our method represents and approximates dense clusters of Gaussians with significantly fewer Gaussians through efficient factorization. We aim to efficiently represent dense 3D Gaussians by approximating them with a limited amount of information for each axis and their combinations. This method allows us to encode a substantially large number of Gaussians along with their essential attributes -- such as color, scale, and rotation -- necessary for rendering using a relatively small number of elements. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that F-3DGS achieves a significant reduction in storage costs while maintaining comparable quality in rendered images.  
- **📝翻译**: 神经辐射场（NeRF）在三维场景的表现和新颖视角的综合方面取得了显著进展。尽管有进步，NeRF的高计算成本在资源受限环境和实时应用中仍面临挑战。作为类似NeRF神经渲染方法的替代方案，3D高斯喷射（3DGS）提供了快速渲染速度，同时保持优异的图像质量。然而，由于它使用大量高斯量表示对象和场景，因此需要大量存储才能实现高质量的表示。为减轻存储开销，我们提出了分解三维高斯喷涂（F-3DGS）新颖方法，大幅降低存储需求同时保持图像质量。受经典矩阵和张量分解技术的启发，我们的方法通过高效分解表示并近似高斯密集簇，高斯分布显著减少。我们旨在通过对每个轴及其组合提供有限信息，高效地表示稠密的三维高斯分布。这种方法使我们能够编码大量高斯分布及其基本属性——如颜色、比例和旋转——这些属性对于使用相对较少的元素进行渲染至关重要。大量实验结果表明，F-3DGS在保持渲染图像质量相当的同时，显著降低了存储成本。  

### [8] PyGS: Large-scale Scene Representation with Pyramidal 3D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2024-05-27（更新：2024-05-29）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Zipeng Wang, Dan Xu  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.16829v3)    
- **📝摘要**：Neural Radiance Fields (NeRFs) have demonstrated remarkable proficiency in synthesizing photorealistic images of large-scale scenes. However, they are often plagued by a loss of fine details and long rendering durations. 3D Gaussian Splatting has recently been introduced as a potent alternative, achieving both high-fidelity visual results and accelerated rendering performance. Nonetheless, scaling 3D Gaussian Splatting is fraught with challenges. Specifically, large-scale scenes grapples with the integration of objects across multiple scales and disparate viewpoints, which often leads to compromised efficacy as the Gaussians need to balance between detail levels. Furthermore, the generation of initialization points via COLMAP from large-scale dataset is both computationally demanding and prone to incomplete reconstructions. To address these challenges, we present Pyramidal 3D Gaussian Splatting (PyGS) with NeRF Initialization. Our approach represent the scene with a hierarchical assembly of Gaussians arranged in a pyramidal fashion. The top level of the pyramid is composed of a few large Gaussians, while each subsequent layer accommodates a denser collection of smaller Gaussians. We effectively initialize these pyramidal Gaussians through sampling a rapidly trained grid-based NeRF at various frequencies. We group these pyramidal Gaussians into clusters and use a compact weighting network to dynamically determine the influence of each pyramid level of each cluster considering camera viewpoint during rendering. Our method achieves a significant performance leap across multiple large-scale datasets and attains a rendering time that is over 400 times faster than current state-of-the-art approaches.  
- **📝翻译**: 神经辐射场（NeRFs）在合成大比例场景的写实图像方面表现出了卓越的熟练度。然而，它们常常因细节丢失和渲染时间过长而困扰。3D高斯喷射技术最近被引入，作为一种强有力的替代方案，既实现了高保真视觉效果，又提升了渲染性能。尽管如此，3D高斯喷溅的缩放依然充满挑战。具体来说，大尺度场景需要在多个尺度和不同视角中整合物体，这常常导致效果受损，因为高斯分布器需要在细节层面之间取得平衡。此外，通过COLMAP从大规模数据集生成初始化点既计算量大，且重建不完整。为应对这些挑战，我们介绍了带有NeRF初始化的金字塔三维高斯喷溅（PyGS）。我们的方法通过高斯分布层叠加的结构，呈金字塔形排列。金字塔的顶层由几个大型高斯分布组成，而每一层则容纳更密集的较小高斯分布。我们通过在不同频率下快速训练的基于网格的NeRF采样，有效地初始化这些金字塔型高斯分布。我们将这些金字塔型高斯分布子分组为簇，并使用紧凑的加权网络动态确定每个簇金字塔层级在渲染过程中从摄像机视角计算的影响。我们的方法在多个大规模数据集中实现了显著的性能飞跃，渲染时间比当前最先进方法快了400多倍。  

### [9] Splat-SLAM: Globally Optimized RGB-only SLAM with 3D Gaussians  
- **⏳发布**：2024-05-26  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Erik Sandström, Keisuke Tateno, Michael Oechsle et al.  
- **📝说明**：21 pages  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.16544v1)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting has emerged as a powerful representation of geometry and appearance for RGB-only dense Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM), as it provides a compact dense map representation while enabling efficient and high-quality map rendering. However, existing methods show significantly worse reconstruction quality than competing methods using other 3D representations, e.g. neural points clouds, since they either do not employ global map and pose optimization or make use of monocular depth. In response, we propose the first RGB-only SLAM system with a dense 3D Gaussian map representation that utilizes all benefits of globally optimized tracking by adapting dynamically to keyframe pose and depth updates by actively deforming the 3D Gaussian map. Moreover, we find that refining the depth updates in inaccurate areas with a monocular depth estimator further improves the accuracy of the 3D reconstruction. Our experiments on the Replica, TUM-RGBD, and ScanNet datasets indicate the effectiveness of globally optimized 3D Gaussians, as the approach achieves superior or on par performance with existing RGB-only SLAM methods methods in tracking, mapping and rendering accuracy while yielding small map sizes and fast runtimes. The source code is available at https://github.com/eriksandstroem/Splat-SLAM.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷涂已成为仅限RGB密集的同时定位与映射（SLAM）中几何和外观的强大表示方式，因为它提供了紧凑且密集的地图表示，同时实现高效且高质量的地图渲染。然而，现有方法的重建质量明显逊于使用其他三维表示的方法，例如神经点云，因为它们要么不采用全局映射和姿态优化，要么使用单眼深度。为此，我们提出了首个仅支持RGB的SLAM系统，采用密集的3D高斯映射表示，充分利用全局优化跟踪的所有优势，通过动态适应关键帧姿态和深度更新，主动变形3D高斯映射。此外，我们发现用单眼深度估计器细化不准确区域的深度更新，进一步提升了三维重建的准确性。我们在Replica、TUM-RGBD和ScanNet数据集上的实验显示，全球优化的3D高斯方法具有显著效果，该方法在跟踪、映射和渲染精度方面表现优于或与现有仅限RGB的SLAM方法相当，同时实现了小地图尺寸和快速运行时间。源代码可在 https://github.com/eriksandstroem/Splat-SLAM 获取。  

### [10] GS-ROR$^2$: Bidirectional-guided 3DGS and SDF for Reflective Object Relighting and Reconstruction  
- **⏳发布**：2024-05-22（更新：2026-06-02）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Zuo-Liang Zhu, Beibei Wang, Jian Yang  
- **📝说明**：Accepted by ACM TOG  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.18544v4)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has shown a powerful capability for novel view synthesis due to its detailed expressive ability and highly efficient rendering speed. Unfortunately, creating relightable 3D assets and reconstructing faithful geometry with 3DGS is still problematic, particularly for reflective objects, as its discontinuous representation raises difficulties in constraining geometries. Volumetric signed distance field (SDF) methods provide robust geometry reconstruction, while the expensive ray marching hinders its real-time application and slows the training. Besides, these methods struggle to capture sharp geometric details. To this end, we propose to guide 3DGS and SDF bidirectionally in a complementary manner, including an SDF-aided Gaussian splatting for efficient optimization of the relighting model and a GS-guided SDF enhancement for high-quality geometry reconstruction. At the core of our SDF-aided Gaussian splatting is the mutual supervision of the depth and normal between blended Gaussians and SDF, which avoids the expensive volume rendering of SDF. Thanks to this mutual supervision, the learned blended Gaussians are well-constrained with a minimal time cost. As the Gaussians are rendered in a deferred shading mode, the alpha-blended Gaussians are smooth, while individual Gaussians may still be outliers, yielding floater artifacts. Therefore, we introduce an SDF-aware pruning strategy to remove Gaussian outliers located distant from the surface defined by SDF, avoiding floater issue. This way, our GS framework provides reasonable normal and achieves realistic relighting, while the mesh from depth is still problematic. Therefore, we design a GS-guided SDF refinement, which utilizes the blended normal from Gaussians to finetune SDF. With this enhancement, our method can further provide high-quality meshes for reflective objects at the cost of 17% extra training time.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷涂（3DGS）因其细致的表现力和高效的渲染速度，展现出强大的新颖视图合成能力。遗憾的是，创建可重照的3D素材和用3DGS重建忠实几何体仍然存在问题，尤其是反光物体，因为其不连续的表现方式增加了约束几何体的困难。体积有符号距离场（SDF）方法提供了稳健的几何重建，而昂贵的光线行进阻碍了其实时应用并减缓训练速度。此外，这些方法难以捕捉清晰的几何细节。为此，我们提议以互补方式双向引导3DGS和SDF，包括SDF辅助的高斯喷溅以高效优化重照模型，以及GS引导SDF增强以实现高质量几何重建。我们SDF辅助高斯喷涂的核心是对混合高斯与SDF之间深度和法向的相互监督，避免了SDF昂贵的体积渲染。得益于这种相互监督，学习过的混合高斯分布器在时间成本下受到良好约束。由于高斯分布以延迟着色模式渲染，α混合的高斯分布是光滑的，而单个高斯分布仍可能为异常值，导致浮动伪影。因此，我们引入了一种基于SDF的剪枝策略，去除距离SDF定义的表面较远的高斯离群值，避免浮游现象。这样，我们的GS框架能提供合理的法线并实现真实的重新光照，而深度网格仍然存在问题。因此，我们设计了一个GS引导的SDF细化，利用高斯分布的混合法线对SDF进行微调。通过这一改进，我们的方法还能以额外17%的训练时间代价，为反射物体提供高质量网格。  

### [11] CoR-GS: Sparse-View 3D Gaussian Splatting via Co-Regularization  
- **⏳发布**：2024-05-20（更新：2024-07-11）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Jiawei Zhang, Jiahe Li, Xiaohan Yu et al.  
- **📝说明**：Accepted at ECCV 2024. Project page: https://jiaw-z.github.io/CoR-GS/  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.12110v2)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) creates a radiance field consisting of 3D Gaussians to represent a scene. With sparse training views, 3DGS easily suffers from overfitting, negatively impacting rendering. This paper introduces a new co-regularization perspective for improving sparse-view 3DGS. When training two 3D Gaussian radiance fields, we observe that the two radiance fields exhibit point disagreement and rendering disagreement that can unsupervisedly predict reconstruction quality, stemming from the randomness of densification implementation. We further quantify the two disagreements and demonstrate the negative correlation between them and accurate reconstruction, which allows us to identify inaccurate reconstruction without accessing ground-truth information. Based on the study, we propose CoR-GS, which identifies and suppresses inaccurate reconstruction based on the two disagreements: (1) Co-pruning considers Gaussians that exhibit high point disagreement in inaccurate positions and prunes them. (2) Pseudo-view co-regularization considers pixels that exhibit high rendering disagreement are inaccurate and suppress the disagreement. Results on LLFF, Mip-NeRF360, DTU, and Blender demonstrate that CoR-GS effectively regularizes the scene geometry, reconstructs the compact representations, and achieves state-of-the-art novel view synthesis quality under sparse training views.  
- **📝翻译**: 三维高斯喷溅（3DGS）生成由三维高斯分布组成的辐射场以表示场景。由于训练视野稀疏，3DGS容易出现过拟合问题，从而对渲染产生负面影响。本文引入了一种新的协正则化视角，用于改进稀疏视图3DGS。在训练两个三维高斯辐射场时，我们观察到两个辐射场存在点不一致和生成不一致，这些现象可以无监督地预测重建质量，这源于密度化实现的随机性。我们进一步量化了这两种分歧，并展示了它们与准确重建之间的负相关性，从而在不获取真实信息的情况下识别不准确的重建。基于研究，我们提出了CoR-GS方法，基于两种分歧识别并抑制不准确的重建：（1）共剪处理考虑在不准确位置中表现出高点不一致的高斯分布，并对其进行修剪。（2）伪视角共正则化认为表现出高度渲染不一致的像素不准确，并抑制这种不一致。LLFF、Mip-NeRF360、DTU和Blender的结果表明，CoR-GS能够有效正则化场景几何，重建紧凑的表示，并在稀疏训练视图下实现最先进的新颖视图综合质量。  

### [12] MotionGS : Compact Gaussian Splatting SLAM by Motion Filter  
- **⏳发布**：2024-05-18（更新：2024-05-31）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Xinli Guo, Weidong Zhang, Ruonan Liu, Peng Han, Hongtian Chen  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.11129v2)    
- **📝摘要**：With their high-fidelity scene representation capability, the attention of SLAM field is deeply attracted by the Neural Radiation Field (NeRF) and 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS). Recently, there has been a surge in NeRF-based SLAM, while 3DGS-based SLAM is sparse. A novel 3DGS-based SLAM approach with a fusion of deep visual feature, dual keyframe selection and 3DGS is presented in this paper. Compared with the existing methods, the proposed tracking is achieved by feature extraction and motion filter on each frame. The joint optimization of poses and 3D Gaussians runs through the entire mapping process. Additionally, the coarse-to-fine pose estimation and compact Gaussian scene representation are implemented by dual keyframe selection and novel loss functions. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed algorithm not only outperforms the existing methods in tracking and mapping, but also has less memory usage.  
- **📝翻译**: 凭借其高保真场景表现能力，SLAM场被神经辐射场（NeRF）和三维高斯喷射（3DGS）深深吸引。最近，基于NeRF的SLAM激增，而基于3DGS的SLAM则较为稀疏。本文提出了一种基于3DGS的新颖SLAM方法，融合了深度视觉特征、双关键帧选择和3DGS。与现有方法相比，所提跟踪是通过特征提取和每帧的运动滤波实现的。姿态和三维高斯的联合优化贯穿整个映射过程。此外，粗到细的姿态估计和紧凑的高斯场景表示通过对偶关键帧选择和新颖的损失函数实现。实验结果表明，所提出的算法不仅在跟踪和映射方面优于现有方法，而且内存占用更少。  

### [13] From NeRFs to Gaussian Splats, and Back  
- **⏳发布**：2024-05-15（更新：2024-08-13）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Siming He, Zach Osman, Pratik Chaudhari  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.09717v3)    
- **📝摘要**：For robotics applications where there is a limited number of (typically ego-centric) views, parametric representations such as neural radiance fields (NeRFs) generalize better than non-parametric ones such as Gaussian splatting (GS) to views that are very different from those in the training data; GS however can render much faster than NeRFs. We develop a procedure to convert back and forth between the two. Our approach achieves the best of both NeRFs (superior PSNR, SSIM, and LPIPS on dissimilar views, and a compact representation) and GS (real-time rendering and ability for easily modifying the representation); the computational cost of these conversions is minor compared to training the two from scratch.  
- **📝翻译**: 对于机器人应用中，视角数量有限（通常是以自我为中心的）方式，参数化表示如神经辐射场（NeRFs）比非参数表示（如高斯溅射（GS））更能推广到与训练数据截然不同的视图;不过GS渲染速度远快于NeRF。我们开发了一套在两者之间转换的程序。我们的方法既实现了NeRFs（在不同视图上更优越的PSNR、SSIM和LPIPS，且表示紧凑）和GS（实时渲染及易于修改表示的能力）的最佳优势;这些转换的计算成本相比从零开始训练两者要小得多。  

### [14] I3DGS: Improve 3D Gaussian Splatting from Multiple Dimensions  
- **⏳发布**：2024-05-10  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Jinwei Lin  
- **📝说明**：16 pages  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.06408v1)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting is a novel method for 3D view synthesis, which can gain an implicit neural learning rendering result than the traditional neural rendering technology but keep the more high-definition fast rendering speed. But it is still difficult to achieve a fast enough efficiency on 3D Gaussian Splatting for the practical applications. To Address this issue, we propose the I3DS, a synthetic model performance improvement evaluation solution and experiments test. From multiple and important levels or dimensions of the original 3D Gaussian Splatting, we made more than two thousand various kinds of experiments to test how the selected different items and components can make an impact on the training efficiency of the 3D Gaussian Splatting model. In this paper, we will share abundant and meaningful experiences and methods about how to improve the training, performance and the impacts caused by different items of the model. A special but normal Integer compression in base 95 and a floating-point compression in base 94 with ASCII encoding and decoding mechanism is presented. Many real and effective experiments and test results or phenomena will be recorded. After a series of reasonable fine-tuning, I3DS can gain excellent performance improvements than the previous one. The project code is available as open source.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅是一种新颖的3D视图合成方法，它可以获得比传统神经渲染技术隐式的神经学习渲染结果，同时保持更高分辨率的快速渲染速度。但在实际应用中，要实现三维高斯喷溅的足够快速效率仍然困难。为解决这一问题，我们提出了I3DS，一种合成模型性能改进评估解决方案和实验测试。从原始3D高斯喷溅的多个重要层次或维度出发，我们进行了两千多个不同类型的实验，以测试所选不同项目和组件如何影响3D高斯喷溅模型的训练效率。本文将分享丰富且有意义的经验和方法，探讨如何改进培训、绩效及模型中不同项目带来的影响。采用了一种特殊但正常的95进制整数压缩和94进制浮点压缩，采用ASCII编码和解码机制。许多真实有效的实验、测试结果或现象将被记录下来。经过一系列合理的微调，I3DS可以获得比前作更显著的性能提升。该项目代码以开源形式提供。  

### [15] GDGS: Gradient Domain Gaussian Splatting for Sparse Representation of Radiance Fields  
- **⏳发布**：2024-05-08  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Yuanhao Gong  
- **📝说明**：arXiv admin note: text overlap with arXiv:2404.09105  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.05446v1)    
- **📝摘要**：The 3D Gaussian splatting methods are getting popular. However, they work directly on the signal, leading to a dense representation of the signal. Even with some techniques such as pruning or distillation, the results are still dense. In this paper, we propose to model the gradient of the original signal. The gradients are much sparser than the original signal. Therefore, the gradients use much less Gaussian splats, leading to the more efficient storage and thus higher computational performance during both training and rendering. Thanks to the sparsity, during the view synthesis, only a small mount of pixels are needed, leading to much higher computational performance ($100\sim 1000\times$ faster). And the 2D image can be recovered from the gradients via solving a Poisson equation with linear computation complexity. Several experiments are performed to confirm the sparseness of the gradients and the computation performance of the proposed method. The method can be applied various applications, such as human body modeling and indoor environment modeling.  
- **📝翻译**: 三维高斯喷溅方法正在流行。然而，它们直接作用于信号，导致信号的密集表示。即使采用修剪或蒸馏等技术，结果依然较为浓密。本文拟建模原始信号的梯度。梯度比原始信号稀疏得多。因此，梯度使用的高斯散块大幅减少，从而实现更高效的存储，从而在训练和渲染过程中获得更高的计算性能。由于稀疏性，在视图合成过程中只需少量像素挂载，从而实现更高的计算性能（100美元\模拟1000美元\更快时间美元）。通过解一个具有线性计算复杂度的泊松方程，可以从梯度中恢复二维图像。进行了多项实验以确认梯度的稀疏性及所提方法的计算性能。该方法可应用于人体建模和室内环境建模等多种应用。  


## April 2024

### [1] RTG-SLAM: Real-time 3D Reconstruction at Scale using Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2024-04-30（更新：2024-05-09）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Zhexi Peng, Tianjia Shao, Yong Liu et al.  
- **📝说明**：To be published in ACM SIGGRAPH 2024  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.19706v3)    
- **📝摘要**：We present Real-time Gaussian SLAM (RTG-SLAM), a real-time 3D reconstruction system with an RGBD camera for large-scale environments using Gaussian splatting. The system features a compact Gaussian representation and a highly efficient on-the-fly Gaussian optimization scheme. We force each Gaussian to be either opaque or nearly transparent, with the opaque ones fitting the surface and dominant colors, and transparent ones fitting residual colors. By rendering depth in a different way from color rendering, we let a single opaque Gaussian well fit a local surface region without the need of multiple overlapping Gaussians, hence largely reducing the memory and computation cost. For on-the-fly Gaussian optimization, we explicitly add Gaussians for three types of pixels per frame: newly observed, with large color errors, and with large depth errors. We also categorize all Gaussians into stable and unstable ones, where the stable Gaussians are expected to well fit previously observed RGBD images and otherwise unstable. We only optimize the unstable Gaussians and only render the pixels occupied by unstable Gaussians. In this way, both the number of Gaussians to be optimized and pixels to be rendered are largely reduced, and the optimization can be done in real time. We show real-time reconstructions of a variety of large scenes. Compared with the state-of-the-art NeRF-based RGBD SLAM, our system achieves comparable high-quality reconstruction but with around twice the speed and half the memory cost, and shows superior performance in the realism of novel view synthesis and camera tracking accuracy.  
- **📝翻译**: 我们介绍实时高斯SLAM（RTG-SLAM），这是一种基于RGBD摄像机的实时3D重建系统，适用于使用高斯喷涂的大型环境。该系统具有紧凑的高斯表示和高效的即时高斯优化方案。我们强制每个高斯图要么是不透明的，要么是近乎透明的，不透明的高斯图适配表面和主色，透明高斯图则是残余色。通过以不同于色彩渲染的方式渲染深度，我们使单个不透明高斯图能够很好地拟合局部表面区域，而无需多个重叠的高斯，从而大大降低了内存和计算成本。对于即时高斯优化，我们为每帧的三种像素类型显式添加高斯分布：新观测像素、大色彩误差和大深度误差。我们还将所有高斯分布量分为稳定高斯和不稳定，其中稳定高斯分布图预期能很好地拟合先前观测到的RGBD图像，否则则是不稳定的高斯分布。我们只优化不稳定的高斯，只渲染被不稳定高斯占据的像素。这样，需要优化的高斯分布量和渲染的像素数量大幅减少，优化可以实时完成。我们展示了各种大型场景的实时重建。与最先进的基于NeRF的RGBD SLAM相比，我们的系统实现了相当的高质量重建，但速度约是其两倍，内存成本仅为一半，并且在新颖的视角合成和摄像机跟踪精度的真实性方面表现出优越性能。  

### [2] SAGS: Structure-Aware 3D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2024-04-29  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Evangelos Ververas, Rolandos Alexandros Potamias, Jifei Song, Jiankang Deng, Stefanos Zafeiriou  
- **📝说明**：15 pages, 8 figures, 3 tables  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.19149v1)   · [Project](https://eververas.github.io/SAGS/.)  
- **📝摘要**：Following the advent of NeRFs, 3D Gaussian Splatting (3D-GS) has paved the way to real-time neural rendering overcoming the computational burden of volumetric methods. Following the pioneering work of 3D-GS, several methods have attempted to achieve compressible and high-fidelity performance alternatives. However, by employing a geometry-agnostic optimization scheme, these methods neglect the inherent 3D structure of the scene, thereby restricting the expressivity and the quality of the representation, resulting in various floating points and artifacts. In this work, we propose a structure-aware Gaussian Splatting method (SAGS) that implicitly encodes the geometry of the scene, which reflects to state-of-the-art rendering performance and reduced storage requirements on benchmark novel-view synthesis datasets. SAGS is founded on a local-global graph representation that facilitates the learning of complex scenes and enforces meaningful point displacements that preserve the scene's geometry. Additionally, we introduce a lightweight version of SAGS, using a simple yet effective mid-point interpolation scheme, which showcases a compact representation of the scene with up to 24$\times$ size reduction without the reliance on any compression strategies. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate the superiority of SAGS compared to state-of-the-art 3D-GS methods under both rendering quality and model size. Besides, we demonstrate that our structure-aware method can effectively mitigate floating artifacts and irregular distortions of previous methods while obtaining precise depth maps. Project page https://eververas.github.io/SAGS/.  
- **📝翻译**: 随着NeRF的出现，三维高斯喷射（3D-GS）为实时神经渲染铺平了道路，克服了体积计算方法的负担。继3D-GS的开创性工作之后，多种方法尝试实现可压缩和高保真的性能替代方案。然而，通过采用几何无关的优化方案，这些方法忽视了场景固有的三维结构，从而限制了表现力和表现质量，导致各种浮点和伪影。本研究提出一种结构感知高斯喷溅法（SAGS），隐式编码场景几何体，反映出最先进的渲染性能和基准新视角综合数据集的存储需求。SAGS基于局部-全局图表示，便于学习复杂场景，并强制执行有意义的点位移，从而保持场景的几何形状。此外，我们还引入了轻量级SAGS版本，采用简单但有效的中点插值方案，展示了场景的紧凑表现，最大可减少24美元\倍数的尺寸，无需依赖任何压缩策略。跨多个基准数据集的广泛实验证明，SAGS在渲染质量和模型尺寸方面优于最先进的3D-GS方法。此外，我们证明了结构感知方法能够有效减少漂浮伪影和先前方法的不规则畸变，同时获得精确的深度图。项目页面 https://eververas.github.io/SAGS/。  

### [3] GSTalker: Real-time Audio-Driven Talking Face Generation via Deformable Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2024-04-29  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Bo Chen, Shoukang Hu, Qi Chen et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.19040v1)    
- **📝摘要**：We present GStalker, a 3D audio-driven talking face generation model with Gaussian Splatting for both fast training (40 minutes) and real-time rendering (125 FPS) with a 3$\sim$5 minute video for training material, in comparison with previous 2D and 3D NeRF-based modeling frameworks which require hours of training and seconds of rendering per frame. Specifically, GSTalker learns an audio-driven Gaussian deformation field to translate and transform 3D Gaussians to synchronize with audio information, in which multi-resolution hashing grid-based tri-plane and temporal smooth module are incorporated to learn accurate deformation for fine-grained facial details. In addition, a pose-conditioned deformation field is designed to model the stabilized torso. To enable efficient optimization of the condition Gaussian deformation field, we initialize 3D Gaussians by learning a coarse static Gaussian representation. Extensive experiments in person-specific videos with audio tracks validate that GSTalker can generate high-fidelity and audio-lips synchronized results with fast training and real-time rendering speed.  
- **📝翻译**: 我们介绍GStalker，一个基于高斯Splatting的高斯语音面部生成模型，支持快速训练（40分钟）和实时渲染（125帧每秒），并配有3美元\模拟币5分钟的训练视频，与之前需要数小时训练和每帧数秒渲染的2D和3D基于NeRF的建模框架形成鲜明对比。具体来说，GSTalker 学习音频驱动的高斯变形场，将三维高斯变形映射与音频信息同步，其中集成了多分辨率哈希基于网格的三平面和时间光滑模块，以学习细粒度面部细节的精确变形。此外，设计了一个姿态条件变形场以模拟稳定躯干。为了高效优化高斯变形场条件，我们通过学习粗静态高斯表示来初始化三维高斯分布。对针对特定人物的音频轨视频的广泛实验验证了GSTalker能够以快速训练和实时渲染速度生成高保真和音频同步的结果。  

### [4] CLIP-GS: CLIP-Informed Gaussian Splatting for View-Consistent 3D Indoor Semantic Understanding  
- **⏳发布**：2024-04-22（更新：2025-06-23）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Guibiao Liao, Jiankun Li, Zhenyu Bao et al.  
- **📝说明**：ACM TOMM 2025  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.14249v2)    
- **📝摘要**：Exploiting 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) with Contrastive Language-Image Pre-Training (CLIP) models for open-vocabulary 3D semantic understanding of indoor scenes has emerged as an attractive research focus. Existing methods typically attach high-dimensional CLIP semantic embeddings to 3D Gaussians and leverage view-inconsistent 2D CLIP semantics as Gaussian supervision, resulting in efficiency bottlenecks and deficient 3D semantic consistency. To address these challenges, we present CLIP-GS, efficiently achieving a coherent semantic understanding of 3D indoor scenes via the proposed Semantic Attribute Compactness (SAC) and 3D Coherent Regularization (3DCR). SAC approach exploits the naturally unified semantics within objects to learn compact, yet effective, semantic Gaussian representations, enabling highly efficient rendering (>100 FPS). 3DCR enforces semantic consistency in 2D and 3D domains: In 2D, 3DCR utilizes refined view-consistent semantic outcomes derived from 3DGS to establish cross-view coherence constraints; in 3D, 3DCR encourages features similar among 3D Gaussian primitives associated with the same object, leading to more precise and coherent segmentation results. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that our method remarkably suppresses existing state-of-the-art approaches, achieving mIoU improvements of 21.20% and 13.05% on ScanNet and Replica datasets, respectively, while maintaining real-time rendering speed. Furthermore, our approach exhibits superior performance even with sparse input data, substantiating its robustness.  
- **📝翻译**: 利用3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）结合对比语言-图像预训练（CLIP）模型，实现室内场景的开放词汇3D语义理解，已成为一个有吸引力的研究方向。现有方法通常将高维CLIP语义嵌入附加到三维高斯图上，并利用视图不一致的二维CLIP语义作为高斯监督，导致效率瓶颈和三维语义一致性不足。为应对这些挑战，我们介绍了CLIP-GS，通过提出的语义属性紧凑性（SAC）和三维相干正则化（3DCR）高效实现三维室内场景的连贯语义理解。SAC方法利用对象内自然统一的语义，学习紧凑但有效的语义高斯表示，实现高效渲染（>100帧每秒）。3DCR在二维和三维领域强制执行语义一致性：在二维领域，3DCR利用从3DGS导出的精炼视图一致语义结果，建立交叉视野一致性约束;在三维中，3DCR鼓励与同一对象相关的三维高斯原语表现出相似特征，从而获得更精确和连贯的分割结果。大量实验结果表明，我们的方法显著抑制了现有最先进方法，在ScanNet和Replica数据集上分别实现了21.20%和13.05%的mIoU提升，同时保持了实时渲染速度。此外，我们的方法即使在输入数据稀少的情况下也表现出优异性能，证明了其鲁棒性。  

### [5] EfficientGS: Streamlining Gaussian Splatting for Large-Scale High-Resolution Scene Representation  
- **⏳发布**：2024-04-19  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Wenkai Liu, Tao Guan, Bin Zhu et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.12777v1)    
- **📝摘要**：In the domain of 3D scene representation, 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as a pivotal technology. However, its application to large-scale, high-resolution scenes (exceeding 4k$\times$4k pixels) is hindered by the excessive computational requirements for managing a large number of Gaussians. Addressing this, we introduce 'EfficientGS', an advanced approach that optimizes 3DGS for high-resolution, large-scale scenes. We analyze the densification process in 3DGS and identify areas of Gaussian over-proliferation. We propose a selective strategy, limiting Gaussian increase to key primitives, thereby enhancing the representational efficiency. Additionally, we develop a pruning mechanism to remove redundant Gaussians, those that are merely auxiliary to adjacent ones. For further enhancement, we integrate a sparse order increment for Spherical Harmonics (SH), designed to alleviate storage constraints and reduce training overhead. Our empirical evaluations, conducted on a range of datasets including extensive 4K+ aerial images, demonstrate that 'EfficientGS' not only expedites training and rendering times but also achieves this with a model size approximately tenfold smaller than conventional 3DGS while maintaining high rendering fidelity.  
- **📝翻译**: 在三维场景表示领域，三维高斯喷溅（3DGS）已成为一项关键技术。然而，其在大尺度高分辨率场景（超过4k$\times$4k像素）中的应用受到管理大量高斯高斯图计算需求的限制。针对此，我们介绍了“EfficientGS”，一种优化3DGS以适应高分辨率、大尺度场景的方法。我们分析了3DGS中的致密过程，并识别出高斯过度扩散的区域。我们提出了一种选择性策略，将高斯增加限制在关键原节点，从而提升表示效率。此外，我们开发了一种修剪机制，用以去除冗余的高斯分布，即仅为相邻高斯的辅助。为了进一步增强，我们集成了球谐（SH）的稀疏阶增量，旨在缓解存储限制并减少训练开销。我们对包括大量4K+航拍图像在内的多种数据集进行的实证评估表明，“EfficientGS”不仅加快了训练和渲染时间，还能以约十倍规模的模型规模实现这一目标，同时保持高渲染精度。  

### [6] Application of 3D Gaussian Splatting for Cinematic Anatomy on Consumer Class Devices  
- **⏳发布**：2024-04-17（更新：2024-06-25）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Simon Niedermayr, Christoph Neuhauser, Kaloian Petkov, Klaus Engel, Rüdiger Westermann  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.11285v2)    
- **📝摘要**：Interactive photorealistic rendering of 3D anatomy is used in medical education to explain the structure of the human body. It is currently restricted to frontal teaching scenarios, where even with a powerful GPU and high-speed access to a large storage device where the data set is hosted, interactive demonstrations can hardly be achieved. We present the use of novel view synthesis via compressed 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) to overcome this restriction, and to even enable students to perform cinematic anatomy on lightweight and mobile devices. Our proposed pipeline first finds a set of camera poses that captures all potentially seen structures in the data. High-quality images are then generated with path tracing and converted into a compact 3DGS representation, consuming < 70 MB even for data sets of multiple GBs. This allows for real-time photorealistic novel view synthesis that recovers structures up to the voxel resolution and is almost indistinguishable from the path-traced images  
- **📝翻译**: 三维解剖的互动式写实渲染被用于医学教育中解释人体结构。目前它仅限于正面教学场景，即使配备了强大的GPU和高速访问存放数据集的大型存储设备，互动演示也很难实现。我们展示了通过压缩三维高斯喷溅（3DGS）进行新颖视角合成，以克服这一限制，甚至使学生能够在轻量级和移动设备上进行电影解剖。我们提出的流水线首先找到一组摄像机姿态，捕捉数据中所有可能可见的结构。随后通过路径追踪生成高质量图像，并转换为紧凑的3DGS表示，即使是多GB数据集，也占用<70 MB。这允许实时写实的新视角合成，恢复结构至体素分辨率，且几乎与路径追踪图像无异  

### [7] 3D Gaussian Splatting as Markov Chain Monte Carlo  
- **⏳发布**：2024-04-15（更新：2025-02-12）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Shakiba Kheradmand, Daniel Rebain, Gopal Sharma et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.09591v3)    
- **📝摘要**：While 3D Gaussian Splatting has recently become popular for neural rendering, current methods rely on carefully engineered cloning and splitting strategies for placing Gaussians, which can lead to poor-quality renderings, and reliance on a good initialization. In this work, we rethink the set of 3D Gaussians as a random sample drawn from an underlying probability distribution describing the physical representation of the scene-in other words, Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) samples. Under this view, we show that the 3D Gaussian updates can be converted as Stochastic Gradient Langevin Dynamics (SGLD) updates by simply introducing noise. We then rewrite the densification and pruning strategies in 3D Gaussian Splatting as simply a deterministic state transition of MCMC samples, removing these heuristics from the framework. To do so, we revise the 'cloning' of Gaussians into a relocalization scheme that approximately preserves sample probability. To encourage efficient use of Gaussians, we introduce a regularizer that promotes the removal of unused Gaussians. On various standard evaluation scenes, we show that our method provides improved rendering quality, easy control over the number of Gaussians, and robustness to initialization.  
- **📝翻译**: 虽然3D高斯喷涂最近在神经渲染中变得流行，但当前方法依赖于精心设计的高斯克隆和分割策略，这可能导致渲染质量较差，且依赖良好的初始化。在本研究中，我们重新思考三维高斯集合作为从描述场景物理表示的底层概率分布中随机抽取的样本——换句话说，就是马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）样本。根据这种观点，我们证明了三维高斯更新可以通过引入噪声转换为随机梯度朗之文动力学（SGLD）更新。随后，我们将三维高斯喷溅中的密度化和剪枝策略重写为MCMC样本的确定性状态转变，去除这些启发式。为此，我们将高斯分布的“克隆”修正为一个近似保持样本概率的重定位方案。为了鼓励高效使用高斯分布，我们引入了一种正则化器，促进去除未使用的高斯分布。在多个标准评估场景中，我们证明了该方法提升了渲染质量，易于控制高斯数量，并且具有初始化的鲁棒性。  

### [8] CompGS: Efficient 3D Scene Representation via Compressed Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2024-04-15  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Xiangrui Liu, Xinju Wu, Pingping Zhang et al.  
- **📝说明**：Submitted to a conference  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.09458v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Gaussian splatting, renowned for its exceptional rendering quality and efficiency, has emerged as a prominent technique in 3D scene representation. However, the substantial data volume of Gaussian splatting impedes its practical utility in real-world applications. Herein, we propose an efficient 3D scene representation, named Compressed Gaussian Splatting (CompGS), which harnesses compact Gaussian primitives for faithful 3D scene modeling with a remarkably reduced data size. To ensure the compactness of Gaussian primitives, we devise a hybrid primitive structure that captures predictive relationships between each other. Then, we exploit a small set of anchor primitives for prediction, allowing the majority of primitives to be encapsulated into highly compact residual forms. Moreover, we develop a rate-constrained optimization scheme to eliminate redundancies within such hybrid primitives, steering our CompGS towards an optimal trade-off between bitrate consumption and representation efficacy. Experimental results show that the proposed CompGS significantly outperforms existing methods, achieving superior compactness in 3D scene representation without compromising model accuracy and rendering quality. Our code will be released on GitHub for further research.  
- **📝翻译**: 高斯喷溅以其卓越的渲染质量和效率著称，已成为3D场景表现中的一种重要技术。然而，高斯喷溅的庞大数据量限制了其在实际应用中的实用性。本文提出一种高效的三维场景表示，称为压缩高斯喷溅（Compressed Gaussian Splatting，简称CompGS），它利用紧致的高斯原语实现忠实的三维场景建模，同时数据量显著减少。为确保高斯原的紧致性，我们设计了一种混合原始结构，捕捉彼此间的预测关系。然后，我们利用一小部分锚点原语进行预测，使大多数原语能够封装为高度紧致的残差形式。此外，我们开发了一种速率约束优化方案，以消除此类混合原语中的冗余，引导我们的CompGS在比特率消耗与表示效能之间实现最佳权衡。实验结果显示，所提出的CompGS显著优于现有方法，在三维场景表示中实现了卓越的紧凑性，同时不影响模型的准确性和渲染质量。我们的代码将发布在GitHub上，供进一步研究。  

### [9] End-to-End Rate-Distortion Optimized 3D Gaussian Representation  
- **⏳发布**：2024-04-09（更新：2024-10-21）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Henan Wang, Hanxin Zhu, Tianyu He et al.  
- **📝说明**：ECCV 2024  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.01597v2)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has become an emerging technique with remarkable potential in 3D representation and image rendering. However, the substantial storage overhead of 3DGS significantly impedes its practical applications. In this work, we formulate the compact 3D Gaussian learning as an end-to-end Rate-Distortion Optimization (RDO) problem and propose RDO-Gaussian that can achieve flexible and continuous rate control. RDO-Gaussian addresses two main issues that exist in current schemes: 1) Different from prior endeavors that minimize the rate under the fixed distortion, we introduce dynamic pruning and entropy-constrained vector quantization (ECVQ) that optimize the rate and distortion at the same time. 2) Previous works treat the colors of each Gaussian equally, while we model the colors of different regions and materials with learnable numbers of parameters. We verify our method on both real and synthetic scenes, showcasing that RDO-Gaussian greatly reduces the size of 3D Gaussian over 40x, and surpasses existing methods in rate-distortion performance.  
- **📝翻译**: 三维高斯喷溅（3DGS）已成为一种新兴技术，在三维表示和图像渲染中具有显著潜力。然而，3DGS庞大的存储开销严重阻碍了其实际应用。在本研究中，我们将紧凑的三维高斯学习表述为端到端速率失真优化（RDO）问题，并提出了能够实现灵活连续速率控制的RDO-高斯方案。RDO-高斯分布解决了当前方案中存在的两个主要问题：1）与以往在固定畸变条件下最小化速率的尝试不同，我们引入了动态剪枝和熵约束向量量化（ECVQ），以同时优化速率和失真。2）以往的工作对每种高斯分布的颜色一视同仁，而我们则用可学习的参数数量来建模不同区域和材料的颜色。我们在真实和合成场景中验证了我们的方法，展示了RDO-高斯在3D高斯尺寸上大幅缩小了40倍以上，并且在速率失真性能上超越了现有方法。  

### [10] Revising Densification in Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2024-04-09  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Samuel Rota Bulò, Lorenzo Porzi, Peter Kontschieder  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.06109v1)    
- **📝摘要**：In this paper, we address the limitations of Adaptive Density Control (ADC) in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), a scene representation method achieving high-quality, photorealistic results for novel view synthesis. ADC has been introduced for automatic 3D point primitive management, controlling densification and pruning, however, with certain limitations in the densification logic. Our main contribution is a more principled, pixel-error driven formulation for density control in 3DGS, leveraging an auxiliary, per-pixel error function as the criterion for densification. We further introduce a mechanism to control the total number of primitives generated per scene and correct a bias in the current opacity handling strategy of ADC during cloning operations. Our approach leads to consistent quality improvements across a variety of benchmark scenes, without sacrificing the method's efficiency.  
- **📝翻译**: 本文探讨了自适应密度控制（ADC）在3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）中存在的局限性，这是一种场景表示方法，能够实现高质量、逼真的新视角合成结果。ADC已被引入用于自动三维点基元管理，控制密度化和修剪，但密度化逻辑存在一定限制。我们的主要贡献是采用更为原则性的像素误差驱动3DGS密度控制公式，利用辅助的每像素误差函数作为密度化的标准。我们还进一步引入了一种机制，用于控制每个场景生成的图元总数，并纠正ADC在克隆操作中当前不透明度处理策略中的偏见。我们的方法在多个基准场景中实现了持续的质量提升，同时不牺牲方法的效率。  


## March 2024

### [1] TOGS: Gaussian Splatting with Temporal Opacity Offset for Real-Time 4D DSA Rendering  
- **⏳发布**：2024-03-28（更新：2024-09-08）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Shuai Zhang, Huangxuan Zhao, Zhenghong Zhou et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.19586v2)    
- **📝摘要**：Four-dimensional Digital Subtraction Angiography (4D DSA) is a medical imaging technique that provides a series of 2D images captured at different stages and angles during the process of contrast agent filling blood vessels. It plays a significant role in the diagnosis of cerebrovascular diseases. Improving the rendering quality and speed under sparse sampling is important for observing the status and location of lesions. The current methods exhibit inadequate rendering quality in sparse views and suffer from slow rendering speed. To overcome these limitations, we propose TOGS, a Gaussian splatting method with opacity offset over time, which can effectively improve the rendering quality and speed of 4D DSA. We introduce an opacity offset table for each Gaussian to model the opacity offsets of the Gaussian, using these opacity-varying Gaussians to model the temporal variations in the radiance of the contrast agent. By interpolating the opacity offset table, the opacity variation of the Gaussian at different time points can be determined. This enables us to render the 2D DSA image at that specific moment. Additionally, we introduced a Smooth loss term in the loss function to mitigate overfitting issues that may arise in the model when dealing with sparse view scenarios. During the training phase, we randomly prune Gaussians, thereby reducing the storage overhead of the model. The experimental results demonstrate that compared to previous methods, this model achieves state-of-the-art render quality under the same number of training views. Additionally, it enables real-time rendering while maintaining low storage overhead. The code is available at https://github.com/hustvl/TOGS.  
- **📝翻译**: 四维数字减去血管造影（4D DSA）是一种医学影像技术，在造影剂充盈血管过程中，提供一系列在不同阶段和角度拍摄的二维图像。它在脑血管疾病的诊断中起着重要作用。在稀疏采样下提高渲染质量和速度对于观察病变的状态和位置非常重要。当前方法在稀疏视图下表现不佳，且渲染速度较慢。为克服这些限制，我们提出了TOGS，一种具有不透明度随时间偏移的高斯喷涂方法，能够有效提升4D DSA的渲染质量和速度。我们为每个高斯量引入不透明度偏移表，用这些不透明度变化的高斯量来模拟对比剂辐射度的时间变化。通过插值不透明度偏移表，可以确定高斯分布在不同时间点的不透明度变化。这使得我们能够在特定时刻渲染二维DSA图像。此外，我们在损失函数中引入了平滑损失项，以缓解模型在处理稀疏视图场景时可能出现的过拟合问题。在训练阶段，我们随机修剪高斯分布，从而降低模型的存储开销。实验结果表明，与以往方法相比，该模型在相同训练视图数下实现了最先进的渲染质量。此外，它还支持实时渲染，同时保持低存储开销。代码可在 https://github.com/hustvl/TOGS 获取。  

### [2] Octree-GS: Towards Consistent Real-time Rendering with LOD-Structured 3D Gaussians  
- **⏳发布**：2024-03-26（更新：2024-10-17）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Kerui Ren, Lihan Jiang, Tao Lu et al.  
- **📝说明**：Project page: https://city-super.github.io/octree-gs/  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.17898v2)    
- **📝摘要**：The recent 3D Gaussian splatting (3D-GS) has shown remarkable rendering fidelity and efficiency compared to NeRF-based neural scene representations. While demonstrating the potential for real-time rendering, 3D-GS encounters rendering bottlenecks in large scenes with complex details due to an excessive number of Gaussian primitives located within the viewing frustum. This limitation is particularly noticeable in zoom-out views and can lead to inconsistent rendering speeds in scenes with varying details. Moreover, it often struggles to capture the corresponding level of details at different scales with its heuristic density control operation. Inspired by the Level-of-Detail (LOD) techniques, we introduce Octree-GS, featuring an LOD-structured 3D Gaussian approach supporting level-of-detail decomposition for scene representation that contributes to the final rendering results. Our model dynamically selects the appropriate level from the set of multi-resolution anchor points, ensuring consistent rendering performance with adaptive LOD adjustments while maintaining high-fidelity rendering results.  
- **📝翻译**: 最近的3D高斯喷涂（3D-GS）相比基于NeRF的神经场景表现展现出了显著的渲染精度和效率。在展示实时渲染潜力的同时，3D-GS在大型场景中因观视锥内存在过多的高斯原语而出现渲染瓶颈，细节复杂。这种限制在缩放视角中尤为明显，可能导致细节变化场景中的渲染速度不稳定。此外，它常常难以通过启发式密度控制操作在不同尺度下捕捉相应的细节水平。受细节层级（LOD）技术启发，我们介绍了Octree-GS，采用LOD结构的3D高斯方法，支持场景的细节层级分解，助力最终渲染结果。我们的模型会从多分辨率锚点中动态选择合适的层级，确保通过自适应LOD调整实现一致的渲染性能，同时保持高保真度的渲染效果。  

### [3] HAC: Hash-grid Assisted Context for 3D Gaussian Splatting Compression  
- **⏳发布**：2024-03-21（更新：2024-07-12）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Yihang Chen, Qianyi Wu, Weiyao Lin, Mehrtash Harandi, Jianfei Cai  
- **📝说明**：Project Page: https://yihangchen-ee.github.io/project_hac/ Code: https://github.com/YihangChen-ee/HAC  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.14530v3)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as a promising framework for novel view synthesis, boasting rapid rendering speed with high fidelity. However, the substantial Gaussians and their associated attributes necessitate effective compression techniques. Nevertheless, the sparse and unorganized nature of the point cloud of Gaussians (or anchors in our paper) presents challenges for compression. To address this, we make use of the relations between the unorganized anchors and the structured hash grid, leveraging their mutual information for context modeling, and propose a Hash-grid Assisted Context (HAC) framework for highly compact 3DGS representation. Our approach introduces a binary hash grid to establish continuous spatial consistencies, allowing us to unveil the inherent spatial relations of anchors through a carefully designed context model. To facilitate entropy coding, we utilize Gaussian distributions to accurately estimate the probability of each quantized attribute, where an adaptive quantization module is proposed to enable high-precision quantization of these attributes for improved fidelity restoration. Additionally, we incorporate an adaptive masking strategy to eliminate invalid Gaussians and anchors. Importantly, our work is the pioneer to explore context-based compression for 3DGS representation, resulting in a remarkable size reduction of over $75\times$ compared to vanilla 3DGS, while simultaneously improving fidelity, and achieving over $11\times$ size reduction over SOTA 3DGS compression approach Scaffold-GS. Our code is available here: https://github.com/YihangChen-ee/HAC  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）已成为一种有前景的新颖视图合成框架，具有高速渲染速度和高保真度。然而，庞大的高斯分布及其相关属性需要有效的压缩技术。然而，高斯分布点云（或我们论文中的锚点）稀疏且无序的特性为压缩带来了挑战。为此，我们利用无组织锚点与结构化哈希网格之间的关系，利用它们的互信息进行上下文建模，并提出了一个哈希网格辅助上下文（HAC）框架，用于高度紧凑的3DGS表示。我们的方法引入了二进制哈希网格，以建立连续的空间一致性，使我们能够通过精心设计的上下文模型揭示锚点的固有空间关系。为促进熵编码，我们利用高斯分布准确估计每个量子化属性的概率，并提出自适应量化模块，以实现这些属性的高精度量化，从而提升保真度恢复。此外，我们还采用了自适应遮蔽策略，以消除无效的高斯和锚点。重要的是，我们的工作是探索基于上下文的3DGS表示方式的先驱，相比原版3DGS实现了超过75倍的显著缩小，同时提升了保真度，并实现了超过11美元倍的尺寸缩小，相比SOTA 3DGS压缩方法Scaffold-GS。我们的代码在这里可以找到：https://github.com/YihangChen-ee/HAC  

### [4] Mini-Splatting: Representing Scenes with a Constrained Number of Gaussians  
- **⏳发布**：2024-03-21（更新：2024-10-16）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Guangchi Fang, Bing Wang  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.14166v3)    
- **📝摘要**：In this study, we explore the challenge of efficiently representing scenes with a constrained number of Gaussians. Our analysis shifts from traditional graphics and 2D computer vision to the perspective of point clouds, highlighting the inefficient spatial distribution of Gaussian representation as a key limitation in model performance. To address this, we introduce strategies for densification including blur split and depth reinitialization, and simplification through intersection preserving and sampling. These techniques reorganize the spatial positions of the Gaussians, resulting in significant improvements across various datasets and benchmarks in terms of rendering quality, resource consumption, and storage compression. Our Mini-Splatting integrates seamlessly with the original rasterization pipeline, providing a strong baseline for future research in Gaussian-Splatting-based works. \href{https://github.com/fatPeter/mini-splatting}{Code is available}.  
- **📝翻译**: 本研究探讨了在高斯数量受限的情况下高效表示场景的挑战。我们的分析从传统的图形和二维计算机视觉转向点云视角，凸显了高斯表示空间分布效率低下，这是模型性能的关键限制。为此，我们引入了包括模糊分割和深度重初始化在内的致密化策略，以及通过保持交集和采样实现的简化。这些技术重组了高斯分布的空间位置，在不同数据集和基准测试中显著提升了渲染质量、资源消耗和存储压缩。我们的Mini-Splatting与原始光栅化流程无缝集成，为未来基于高斯Splatting的作品研究奠定了坚实基础。\href{https://github.com/fatPeter/mini-splatting}{代码可用}。  

### [5] RadSplat: Radiance Field-Informed Gaussian Splatting for Robust Real-Time Rendering with 900+ FPS  
- **⏳发布**：2024-03-20（更新：2025-03-31）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Michael Niemeyer, Fabian Manhardt, Marie-Julie Rakotosaona et al.  
- **📝说明**：Project page at https://m-niemeyer.github.io/radsplat/ and presented at 3DV (Oral)  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.13806v2)    
- **📝摘要**：Recent advances in view synthesis and real-time rendering have achieved photorealistic quality at impressive rendering speeds. While Radiance Field-based methods achieve state-of-the-art quality in challenging scenarios such as in-the-wild captures and large-scale scenes, they often suffer from excessively high compute requirements linked to volumetric rendering. Gaussian Splatting-based methods, on the other hand, rely on rasterization and naturally achieve real-time rendering but suffer from brittle optimization heuristics that underperform on more challenging scenes. In this work, we present RadSplat, a lightweight method for robust real-time rendering of complex scenes. Our main contributions are threefold. First, we use radiance fields as a prior and supervision signal for optimizing point-based scene representations, leading to improved quality and more robust optimization. Next, we develop a novel pruning technique reducing the overall point count while maintaining high quality, leading to smaller and more compact scene representations with faster inference speeds. Finally, we propose a novel test-time filtering approach that further accelerates rendering and allows to scale to larger, house-sized scenes. We find that our method enables state-of-the-art synthesis of complex captures at 900+ FPS.  
- **📝翻译**: 近期视图合成和实时渲染的进步已在令人印象深刻的渲染速度下实现了照片级写实的质量。虽然基于Radiance Field的方法在野外捕获和大范围场景等具有挑战性的场景中实现了最先进的质量，但它们常常面临与体积渲染相关的过高计算需求。而基于高斯喷溅的方法则依赖光栅化，自然实现实时渲染，但由于优化启发式的脆弱，在更具挑战性的场景中表现不佳。在本研究中，我们介绍了RadSplat，一种用于复杂场景实时稳健渲染的轻量级方法。我们的主要贡献有三方面。首先，我们使用辐射场作为先驱和监督信号，优化基于点的场景表示，从而提升质量并实现更稳健的优化。接下来，我们开发了一种新颖的剪枝技术，减少了总点数，同时保持高质量，从而实现更小、更紧凑、推理速度更快的场景表示。最后，我们提出了一种新的测试时间过滤方法，进一步加速渲染速度，并允许扩展到更大、如房屋大小的场景。我们发现我们的方法能够以900+ FPS进行最先进的复杂捕获合成。  

### [6] NEDS-SLAM: A Neural Explicit Dense Semantic SLAM Framework using 3D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2024-03-18（更新：2024-09-01）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Yiming Ji, Yang Liu, Guanghu Xie, Boyu Ma, Zongwu Xie  
- **📝说明**：accepted by RA-L, IEEE Robotics and Automation Letters  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.11679v3)    
- **📝摘要**：We propose NEDS-SLAM, a dense semantic SLAM system based on 3D Gaussian representation, that enables robust 3D semantic mapping, accurate camera tracking, and high-quality rendering in real-time. In the system, we propose a Spatially Consistent Feature Fusion model to reduce the effect of erroneous estimates from pre-trained segmentation head on semantic reconstruction, achieving robust 3D semantic Gaussian mapping. Additionally, we employ a lightweight encoder-decoder to compress the high-dimensional semantic features into a compact 3D Gaussian representation, mitigating the burden of excessive memory consumption. Furthermore, we leverage the advantage of 3D Gaussian splatting, which enables efficient and differentiable novel view rendering, and propose a Virtual Camera View Pruning method to eliminate outlier gaussians, thereby effectively enhancing the quality of scene representations. Our NEDS-SLAM method demonstrates competitive performance over existing dense semantic SLAM methods in terms of mapping and tracking accuracy on Replica and ScanNet datasets, while also showing excellent capabilities in 3D dense semantic mapping.  
- **📝翻译**: 我们提出了NEDS-SLAM，这是一种基于三维高斯表示的密集语义SLAM系统，能够实现稳健的三维语义映射、准确的摄像机跟踪和实时高质量渲染。在该系统中，我们提出了一个空间一致特征融合模型，以减少预训练分割头部错误估计对语义重建的影响，实现稳健的三维语义高斯映射。此外，我们采用轻量级编码-解码器，将高维语义特征压缩为紧凑的三维高斯表示，减轻了过度内存消耗的负担。此外，我们利用三维高斯散画的优势，实现高效且可微分的新视角渲染，并提出了一种虚拟摄像机视角剪枝方法，以消除离群高斯分布，从而有效提升场景表现的质量。我们的NEDS-SLAM方法在复制和ScanNet数据集的映射和跟踪准确性方面，展示了与现有密集语义SLAM方法的竞争性能，同时也展现出三维密集语义映射的优异能力。  

### [7] Compact 3D Gaussian Splatting For Dense Visual SLAM  
- **⏳发布**：2024-03-17（更新：2026-05-13）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Tianchen Deng, Chang Nie, Shuhong Liu et al.  
- **📝说明**：Accepted by IJCV 2026  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.11247v3)    
- **📝摘要**：Recent work has shown that 3D Gaussian-based SLAM enables high-quality reconstruction, accurate pose estimation, and real-time rendering of scenes. However, these approaches are built on a tremendous number of redundant 3D Gaussian ellipsoids, leading to high memory and storage costs, and slow training speed. To address the limitation, we propose a compact 3D Gaussian Splatting SLAM system that reduces the number and the parameter size of Gaussian ellipsoids. A sliding window-based masking strategy is first proposed to reduce the redundant ellipsoids. Then we observe that the covariance matrix (geometry) of most 3D Gaussian ellipsoids are extremely similar, which motivates a novel geometry codebook to compress 3D Gaussian geometric attributes, i.e., the parameters. Robust and accurate pose estimation is achieved by a global bundle adjustment method with reprojection loss. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves faster training and rendering speed while maintaining the state-of-the-art (SOTA) quality of the scene representation.  
- **📝翻译**: 最新研究表明，基于3D高斯的SLAM能够实现高质量的重建、准确的姿态估计和场景的实时渲染。然而，这些方法建立在大量冗余的三维高斯椭球体之上，导致内存和存储成本高，训练速度较慢。为解决这一限制，我们提出了一个紧凑的三维高斯喷射SLAM系统，以减少高斯椭球体的数量和参数大小。最初提出滑动窗口掩蔽策略以减少冗余椭球体。随后我们观察到大多数三维高斯椭球的协方差矩阵（几何）极为相似，这促使一本新颖的几何代码书压缩三维高斯几何属性，即参数。通过带重投影损耗的全局丛调整方法实现稳健且准确的姿态估计。大量实验表明，我们的方法在保持场景表现的最先进（SOTA）质量的同时，实现了更快的训练和渲染速度。  

### [8] GeoGS3D: Single-view 3D Reconstruction via Geometric-aware Diffusion Model and Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2024-03-15（更新：2024-10-31）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Qijun Feng, Zhen Xing, Zuxuan Wu, Yu-Gang Jiang  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.10242v2)    
- **📝摘要**：We introduce GeoGS3D, a novel two-stage framework for reconstructing detailed 3D objects from single-view images. Inspired by the success of pre-trained 2D diffusion models, our method incorporates an orthogonal plane decomposition mechanism to extract 3D geometric features from the 2D input, facilitating the generation of multi-view consistent images. During the following Gaussian Splatting, these images are fused with epipolar attention, fully utilizing the geometric correlations across views. Moreover, we propose a novel metric, Gaussian Divergence Significance (GDS), to prune unnecessary operations during optimization, significantly accelerating the reconstruction process. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GeoGS3D generates images with high consistency across views and reconstructs high-quality 3D objects, both qualitatively and quantitatively.  
- **📝翻译**: 我们介绍了GeoGS3D，一种新颖的两阶段框架，用于从单视角图像重建详细的三维物体。受预训练二维扩散模型成功的启发，我们的方法采用正交平面分解机制，从二维输入中提取三维几何特征，便于生成多视图一致图像。在随后的高斯喷射过程中，这些图像与极性关注融合，充分利用视图间的几何相关性。此外，我们提出了一种新颖的指标——高斯散度显著性（GDS），用于在优化过程中修剪不必要的操作，显著加快重建过程。大量实验表明，GeoGS3D能够在不同视图中生成高度一致的图像，并能在定性和定量上重建高质量的三维对象。  

### [9] GaussianImage: 1000 FPS Image Representation and Compression by 2D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2024-03-13（更新：2024-07-09）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Xinjie Zhang, Xingtong Ge, Tongda Xu et al.  
- **📝说明**：Accepted by ECCV 2024. Project Page:https://xingtongge.github.io/GaussianImage-page/ Code: https://github.com/Xinjie-Q/GaussianImage  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.08551v5)    
- **📝摘要**：Implicit neural representations (INRs) recently achieved great success in image representation and compression, offering high visual quality and fast rendering speeds with 10-1000 FPS, assuming sufficient GPU resources are available. However, this requirement often hinders their use on low-end devices with limited memory. In response, we propose a groundbreaking paradigm of image representation and compression by 2D Gaussian Splatting, named GaussianImage. We first introduce 2D Gaussian to represent the image, where each Gaussian has 8 parameters including position, covariance and color. Subsequently, we unveil a novel rendering algorithm based on accumulated summation. Remarkably, our method with a minimum of 3$\times$ lower GPU memory usage and 5$\times$ faster fitting time not only rivals INRs (e.g., WIRE, I-NGP) in representation performance, but also delivers a faster rendering speed of 1500-2000 FPS regardless of parameter size. Furthermore, we integrate existing vector quantization technique to build an image codec. Experimental results demonstrate that our codec attains rate-distortion performance comparable to compression-based INRs such as COIN and COIN++, while facilitating decoding speeds of approximately 2000 FPS. Additionally, preliminary proof of concept shows that our codec surpasses COIN and COIN++ in performance when using partial bits-back coding. Code is available at https://github.com/Xinjie-Q/GaussianImage.  
- **📝翻译**: 隐式神经表征（INR）最近在图像表现和压缩方面取得了巨大成功，提供高质量的视觉质量和1000帧/秒的高速渲染速度，前提是GPU资源充足。然而，这一要求常常阻碍其在内存有限的低端设备上的使用。为此，我们提出了一种开创性的二维高斯喷溅图像表示与压缩范式，名为GaussianImage。我们首先引入二维高斯图来表示图像，每个高斯图有8个参数，包括位置、协方差和颜色。随后，我们揭示了一种基于累积求和的新颖渲染算法。令人惊讶的是，我们的方法GPU内存占用至少降低3美元，配合时间快5倍倍，不仅在表现性能上可与INR（如WIRE、I-NGP）媲美，而且无论参数大小如何，渲染速度都快达1500-2000帧。此外，我们整合了现有的矢量量化技术，构建了图像编解码器。实验结果表明，我们的编解码器在速率失真方面的性能可与基于压缩的INR如COIN和COIN++相媲美，同时实现约2000 FPS的解码速度。此外，初步概念验证表明，当采用部分比特回溯编码时，我们的编解码器性能超过了COIN和COIN++。代码可在 https://github.com/Xinjie-Q/GaussianImage 获取。  

### [10] Gaussian Splatting in Style  
- **⏳发布**：2024-03-13（更新：2024-09-06）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Abhishek Saroha, Mariia Gladkova, Cecilia Curreli et al.  
- **📝说明**：GCPR 2024  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.08498v2)    
- **📝摘要**：3D scene stylization extends the work of neural style transfer to 3D. A vital challenge in this problem is to maintain the uniformity of the stylized appearance across multiple views. A vast majority of the previous works achieve this by training a 3D model for every stylized image and a set of multi-view images. In contrast, we propose a novel architecture trained on a collection of style images that, at test time, produces real time high-quality stylized novel views. We choose the underlying 3D scene representation for our model as 3D Gaussian splatting. We take the 3D Gaussians and process them using a multi-resolution hash grid and a tiny MLP to obtain stylized views. The MLP is conditioned on different style codes for generalization to different styles during test time. The explicit nature of 3D Gaussians gives us inherent advantages over NeRF-based methods, including geometric consistency and a fast training and rendering regime. This enables our method to be useful for various practical use cases, such as augmented or virtual reality. We demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance with superior visual quality on various indoor and outdoor real-world data.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D场景风格化将神经风格转移的工作扩展到了3D。这一问题的一个重要挑战是如何在多个视角下保持风格化外观的统一性。绝大多数之前的作品都通过为每张风格化图像训练3D模型和一组多视图图像来实现这一点。相比之下，我们提出了一种基于样式图像集合训练的新颖架构，在测试时能够实时生成高质量的风格化新颖视图。我们将模型底层的3D场景表示方式选择为3D高斯溅射。我们利用多分辨率哈希网格和一个微小的MLP处理三维高斯量，获得风格化视图。MLP在测试时会基于不同的样式代码进行泛化。3D高斯的显式特性为我们带来了相较于NeRF方法的固有优势，包括几何一致性以及快速的训练和渲染过程。这使得我们的方法适用于多种实际应用场景，如增强现实或虚拟现实。我们证明了我们的方法在各种室内外真实数据中实现了最先进的性能和卓越的视觉质量。  

### [11] 3DGStream: On-the-Fly Training of 3D Gaussians for Efficient Streaming of Photo-Realistic Free-Viewpoint Videos  
- **⏳发布**：2024-03-03（更新：2024-06-11）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Jiakai Sun, Han Jiao, Guangyuan Li et al.  
- **📝说明**：CVPR 2024 Accepted (Highlight). Project Page: https://sjojok.github.io/3dgstream  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.01444v4)    
- **📝摘要**：Constructing photo-realistic Free-Viewpoint Videos (FVVs) of dynamic scenes from multi-view videos remains a challenging endeavor. Despite the remarkable advancements achieved by current neural rendering techniques, these methods generally require complete video sequences for offline training and are not capable of real-time rendering. To address these constraints, we introduce 3DGStream, a method designed for efficient FVV streaming of real-world dynamic scenes. Our method achieves fast on-the-fly per-frame reconstruction within 12 seconds and real-time rendering at 200 FPS. Specifically, we utilize 3D Gaussians (3DGs) to represent the scene.
