Instead of the naïve approach of directly optimizing 3DGs per-frame, we employ a compact Neural Transformation Cache (NTC) to model the translations and rotations of 3DGs, markedly reducing the training time and storage required for each FVV frame. Furthermore, we propose an adaptive 3DG addition strategy to handle emerging objects in dynamic scenes. Experiments demonstrate that 3DGStream achieves competitive performance in terms of rendering speed, image quality, training time, and model storage when compared with state-of-the-art methods.  
- **📝翻译**: 构建多视角视频动态场景的写实自由视角视频（FVV）仍然是一项充满挑战的任务。尽管当前神经渲染技术取得了显著进步，这些方法通常需要完整的视频序列进行离线训练，且无法实现实时渲染。为解决这些限制，我们引入了3DGStream，这是一种旨在高效FVV流式真实动态场景的方法。我们的方法实现了每帧快速重建，且能在12秒内实现实时渲染，达到200帧/秒。具体来说，我们使用三维高斯分布图（3DGs）来表示场景。我们采用了紧凑的神经转换缓存（NTC）来模拟3DG的平移和旋转，显著减少了每个FVV帧所需的训练时间和存储空间。此外，我们提出了一种自适应的三维图加法策略，用于处理动态场景中出现的对象。实验表明，与最先进方法相比，3DGStream在渲染速度、图像质量、训练时间和模型存储方面均具竞争力。  


## February 2024

### [1] GaussianObject: High-Quality 3D Object Reconstruction from Four Views with Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2024-02-15（更新：2024-11-13）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Chen Yang, Sikuang Li, Jiemin Fang et al.  
- **📝说明**：ACM Transactions on Graphics (SIGGRAPH Asia 2024). Project page: https://gaussianobject.github.io/ Code: https://github.com/chensjtu/GaussianObject  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.10259v4)   · [Project](https://gaussianobject.github.io/,)  
- **📝摘要**：Reconstructing and rendering 3D objects from highly sparse views is of critical importance for promoting applications of 3D vision techniques and improving user experience. However, images from sparse views only contain very limited 3D information, leading to two significant challenges: 1) Difficulty in building multi-view consistency as images for matching are too few; 2) Partially omitted or highly compressed object information as view coverage is insufficient. To tackle these challenges, we propose GaussianObject, a framework to represent and render the 3D object with Gaussian splatting that achieves high rendering quality with only 4 input images. We first introduce techniques of visual hull and floater elimination, which explicitly inject structure priors into the initial optimization process to help build multi-view consistency, yielding a coarse 3D Gaussian representation. Then we construct a Gaussian repair model based on diffusion models to supplement the omitted object information, where Gaussians are further refined. We design a self-generating strategy to obtain image pairs for training the repair model. We further design a COLMAP-free variant, where pre-given accurate camera poses are not required, which achieves competitive quality and facilitates wider applications. GaussianObject is evaluated on several challenging datasets, including MipNeRF360, OmniObject3D, OpenIllumination, and our-collected unposed images, achieving superior performance from only four views and significantly outperforming previous SOTA methods. Our demo is available at https://gaussianobject.github.io/, and the code has been released at https://github.com/GaussianObject/GaussianObject.  
- **📝翻译**: 从高度稀疏的视角重建和渲染3D对象对于推动3D视觉技术的应用和提升用户体验至关重要。然而，稀疏视图中的图像仅包含非常有限的三维信息，导致两个重大挑战：1）由于匹配图像数量不足，难以建立多视图一致性;2）由于视野覆盖不足，物体信息被部分遗漏或高度压缩。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了GaussianObject框架，一种用高斯喷涂表示和渲染3D对象的框架，仅需4张输入图像即可实现高渲染质量。我们首先引入了视觉包体消除和浮动元素消除技术，这些技术明确地在初始优化过程中注入结构先验，帮助构建多视角一致性，从而得到粗略的三维高斯表示。然后我们基于扩散模型构建高斯修复模型，补充遗漏的对象信息，进一步细化高斯分析。我们设计了一种自生成策略，用于获取用于训练修复模型的图像对。我们还设计了一种无需COLMAP的变体，无需预先设定的精确相机姿态，实现了竞争力的质量并促进了更广泛的应用。GaussianObject在多个具有挑战性的数据集上进行评估，包括MipNeRF360、OmniObject3D、OpenIllumination以及我们收集的无摆拍图像，仅用四个视角就实现了更优的性能，并且显著优于以往的SOTA方法。我们的演示版已在 https://gaussianobject.github.io/ 年发布，代码已于 https://github.com/GaussianObject/GaussianObject 年发布。  


## January 2024

### [1] Learning Segmented 3D Gaussians via Efficient Feature Unprojection for Zero-shot Neural Scene Segmentation  
- **⏳发布**：2024-01-11（更新：2024-07-28）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Bin Dou, Tianyu Zhang, Zhaohui Wang, Yongjia Ma, Zejian Yuan  
- **📝说明**：16 pages, 9 figures, correct writing details  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.05925v4)   · [Project](https://David-Dou.github.io/CoSegGaussians.)  
- **📝摘要**：Zero-shot neural scene segmentation, which reconstructs 3D neural segmentation field without manual annotations, serves as an effective way for scene understanding. However, existing models, especially the efficient 3D Gaussian-based methods, struggle to produce compact segmentation results. This issue stems primarily from their redundant learnable attributes assigned on individual Gaussians, leading to a lack of robustness against the 3D-inconsistencies in zero-shot generated raw labels. To address this problem, our work, named Compact Segmented 3D Gaussians (CoSegGaussians), proposes the Feature Unprojection and Fusion module as the segmentation field, which utilizes a shallow decoder generalizable for all Gaussians based on high-level features. Specifically, leveraging the learned Gaussian geometric parameters, semantic-aware image-based features are introduced into the scene via our unprojection technique. The lifted features, together with spatial information, are fed into the multi-scale aggregation decoder to generate segmentation identities for all Gaussians. Furthermore, we design CoSeg Loss to boost model robustness against 3D-inconsistent noises. Experimental results show that our model surpasses baselines on zero-shot semantic segmentation task, improving by ~10% mIoU over the best baseline. Code and more results will be available at https://David-Dou.github.io/CoSegGaussians.  
- **📝翻译**: 零镜头神经场景分割，即在不需人工注释的情况下重建三维神经分割场，是理解场景的有效方法。然而，现有模型，尤其是高效的三维高斯方法，难以产生紧凑的分割结果。这一问题主要源于它们在单个高斯量上分配的冗余可学习属性，导致对零射中原始标签中3D不一致性缺乏稳健性。为解决这一问题，我们的研究名为紧凑分割三维高斯（CoSegGaussian），提出了特征解投影与融合模块作为分割场，该模块利用一个浅层解码器，基于高层特征可推广至所有高斯分布。具体来说，利用所学的高斯几何参数，通过我们的非投影技术引入了语义感知图像特征。提取的特征连同空间信息一起输入多尺度聚合解码器，生成所有高斯分布的分割身份。此外，我们设计CoSeg Loss以增强模型对3D不一致性噪声的鲁棒性。实验结果显示，我们的模型在零样本语义分割任务中超越基线，比最佳基线提升了约10%的mIoU。代码和更多结果将于 https://David-Dou.github.io/CoSegGaussians 公布。  


## December 2023

### [1] Spacetime Gaussian Feature Splatting for Real-Time Dynamic View Synthesis  
- **⏳发布**：2023-12-28（更新：2024-04-04）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Zhan Li, Zhang Chen, Zhong Li, Yi Xu  
- **📝说明**：Accepted to CVPR 2024. Project page: https://oppo-us-research.github.io/SpacetimeGaussians-website/  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.16812v2)    
- **📝摘要**：Novel view synthesis of dynamic scenes has been an intriguing yet challenging problem. Despite recent advancements, simultaneously achieving high-resolution photorealistic results, real-time rendering, and compact storage remains a formidable task. To address these challenges, we propose Spacetime Gaussian Feature Splatting as a novel dynamic scene representation, composed of three pivotal components. First, we formulate expressive Spacetime Gaussians by enhancing 3D Gaussians with temporal opacity and parametric motion/rotation. This enables Spacetime Gaussians to capture static, dynamic, as well as transient content within a scene. Second, we introduce splatted feature rendering, which replaces spherical harmonics with neural features. These features facilitate the modeling of view- and time-dependent appearance while maintaining small size. Third, we leverage the guidance of training error and coarse depth to sample new Gaussians in areas that are challenging to converge with existing pipelines. Experiments on several established real-world datasets demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art rendering quality and speed, while retaining compact storage. At 8K resolution, our lite-version model can render at 60 FPS on an Nvidia RTX 4090 GPU. Our code is available at https://github.com/oppo-us-research/SpacetimeGaussians.  
- **📝翻译**: 动态场景的新视角综合是一个既有趣又具有挑战性的问题。尽管近期已有进步，同时实现高分辨率的写真级真实效果、实时渲染和紧凑存储仍是一项艰巨任务。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了时空高斯特征喷溅（Spacetime Gaussian Feature Splatting）作为一种新颖的动态场景表示，由三个关键组成部分组成。首先，我们通过增强具有时间不透明度和参数运动/旋转的三维高斯分布，来构建表达性时空高斯分布。这使得时空高斯分布能够捕捉场景中的静态、动态和瞬态内容。其次，我们引入了分割特征渲染，用神经特征替代球面谐波。这些功能有助于建模视角和时间相关的外观，同时保持较小的尺寸。第三，我们利用训练误差和粗深度的指导，在难以与现有管道融合的领域采样新的高斯分布图。在多个已建立的真实数据集上的实验表明，我们的方法实现了最先进的渲染质量和速度，同时保持了紧凑的存储空间。在8K分辨率下，我们的轻量版可以在Nvidia RTX 4090显卡上实现60帧每秒渲染。我们的代码可在 https://github.com/oppo-us-research/SpacetimeGaussians 获取。  

### [2] Compact 3D Scene Representation via Self-Organizing Gaussian Grids  
- **⏳发布**：2023-12-19（更新：2024-05-02）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Wieland Morgenstern, Florian Barthel, Anna Hilsmann, Peter Eisert  
- **📝说明**：Added compression of spherical harmonics, updated compression method with improved results (all attributes compressed with JPEG XL now), added qualitative comparison of additional scenes, moved compression explanation and comparison to main paper, added comparison with "Making Gaussian Splats smaller"  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.13299v2)   · [Project](https://fraunhoferhhi.github.io/Self-Organizing-Gaussians/)  
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting has recently emerged as a highly promising technique for modeling of static 3D scenes. In contrast to Neural Radiance Fields, it utilizes efficient rasterization allowing for very fast rendering at high-quality. However, the storage size is significantly higher, which hinders practical deployment, e.g. on resource constrained devices. In this paper, we introduce a compact scene representation organizing the parameters of 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) into a 2D grid with local homogeneity, ensuring a drastic reduction in storage requirements without compromising visual quality during rendering. Central to our idea is the explicit exploitation of perceptual redundancies present in natural scenes. In essence, the inherent nature of a scene allows for numerous permutations of Gaussian parameters to equivalently represent it. To this end, we propose a novel highly parallel algorithm that regularly arranges the high-dimensional Gaussian parameters into a 2D grid while preserving their neighborhood structure. During training, we further enforce local smoothness between the sorted parameters in the grid. The uncompressed Gaussians use the same structure as 3DGS, ensuring a seamless integration with established renderers. Our method achieves a reduction factor of 17x to 42x in size for complex scenes with no increase in training time, marking a substantial leap forward in the domain of 3D scene distribution and consumption. Additional information can be found on our project page: https://fraunhoferhhi.github.io/Self-Organizing-Gaussians/  
- **📝翻译**: 三维高斯喷溅最近被广泛应用于静态三维场景建模的极具前景。与神经辐射场不同，它采用高效的光栅化技术，实现了极高质量的快速渲染。然而，存储容量显著增加，这不利于实际部署，例如在资源有限的设备上。本文介绍了一种紧凑的场景表示，将三维高斯喷溅（3DGS）参数组织成具有局部均匀性的二维网格，确保大幅减少存储需求，同时不影响渲染时的视觉质量。我们的核心理念是明确利用自然场景中存在的感知冗余。本质上，场景的固有性质允许多种高斯参数的排列等价地表示它。为此，我们提出了一种新颖的高度并行算法，能够定期将高维高斯参数排列成二维网格，同时保持其邻域结构。在训练过程中，我们进一步强化网格中排序参数之间的局部平滑性。未压缩高斯分布采用与3DGS相同的结构，确保与既有渲染器的无缝集成。我们的方法在复杂场景中实现了17倍到42倍的缩小因子，且训练时间未增加，标志着三维场景分布和消费领域的重大飞跃。更多信息可见我们的项目页面：https://fraunhoferhhi.github.io/Self-Organizing-Gaussians/  

### [3] EAGLES: Efficient Accelerated 3D Gaussians with Lightweight EncodingS  
- **⏳发布**：2023-12-07（更新：2024-09-26）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Sharath Girish, Kamal Gupta, Abhinav Shrivastava  
- **📝说明**：Website: https://efficientgaussian.github.io Code: https://github.com/Sharath-girish/efficientgaussian  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.04564v3)   · [Project](https://efficientgaussian.github.io)  
- **📝摘要**：Recently, 3D Gaussian splatting (3D-GS) has gained popularity in novel-view scene synthesis. It addresses the challenges of lengthy training times and slow rendering speeds associated with Neural Radiance Fields (NeRFs). Through rapid, differentiable rasterization of 3D Gaussians, 3D-GS achieves real-time rendering and accelerated training. They, however, demand substantial memory resources for both training and storage, as they require millions of Gaussians in their point cloud representation for each scene. We present a technique utilizing quantized embeddings to significantly reduce per-point memory storage requirements and a coarse-to-fine training strategy for a faster and more stable optimization of the Gaussian point clouds. Our approach develops a pruning stage which results in scene representations with fewer Gaussians, leading to faster training times and rendering speeds for real-time rendering of high resolution scenes. We reduce storage memory by more than an order of magnitude all while preserving the reconstruction quality. We validate the effectiveness of our approach on a variety of datasets and scenes preserving the visual quality while consuming 10-20x lesser memory and faster training/inference speed. Project page and code is available https://efficientgaussian.github.io  
- **📝翻译**: 近年来，3D高斯喷涂（3D-GS）在小说视角场景合成中越来越受欢迎。它解决了神经辐射场（NeRFs）相关的长训练时间和缓慢渲染速度带来的挑战。通过快速、可微分的3D高斯光栅化，3D-GS实现了实时渲染和加速训练。然而，它们在训练和存储过程中都需要大量内存资源，因为每个场景的点云表示都需要数百万高斯分布量。我们提出了一种利用量化嵌入的技术，显著降低每点存储需求，并采用粗细训练策略，以更快更稳定地优化高斯点云。我们的方法开发了一个剪枝阶段，使场景表示高斯数更少，从而加快训练时间和实时渲染速度，实现高分辨率场景的实时渲染。我们减少存储内存超过一个数量级，同时保持重建质量。我们在多种数据集和场景上验证了方法的有效性，既保持了视觉质量，又节省了10-20倍的内存，并提升了更快的训练/推理速度。项目页面和代码均可 https://efficientgaussian.github.io  

### [4] HiFi4G: High-Fidelity Human Performance Rendering via Compact Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2023-12-06（更新：2023-12-07）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Yuheng Jiang, Zhehao Shen, Penghao Wang et al.  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.03461v2)    
- **📝摘要**：We have recently seen tremendous progress in photo-real human modeling and rendering. Yet, efficiently rendering realistic human performance and integrating it into the rasterization pipeline remains challenging. In this paper, we present HiFi4G, an explicit and compact Gaussian-based approach for high-fidelity human performance rendering from dense footage. Our core intuition is to marry the 3D Gaussian representation with non-rigid tracking, achieving a compact and compression-friendly representation. We first propose a dual-graph mechanism to obtain motion priors, with a coarse deformation graph for effective initialization and a fine-grained Gaussian graph to enforce subsequent constraints. Then, we utilize a 4D Gaussian optimization scheme with adaptive spatial-temporal regularizers to effectively balance the non-rigid prior and Gaussian updating. We also present a companion compression scheme with residual compensation for immersive experiences on various platforms. It achieves a substantial compression rate of approximately 25 times, with less than 2MB of storage per frame. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, which significantly outperforms existing approaches in terms of optimization speed, rendering quality, and storage overhead.  
- **📝翻译**: 我们最近在照片真实人体建模和渲染方面取得了巨大进展。然而，高效地渲染真实的人类表现并将其集成到光栅化流程中仍然具有挑战性。本文介绍了HiFi4G，这是一种显式且紧凑的高斯算法方法，用于从密集素材中实现高保真人类表现渲染。我们的核心直觉是将三维高斯表示与非刚性跟踪结合，实现紧凑且易于压缩的表现。我们首先提出一种对偶图机制以获得运动先验，使用粗变形图进行有效初始化，并采用细粒度高斯图来强制后续约束。然后，我们利用带有自适应时空正则化器的四维高斯优化方案，有效平衡非刚性先验和高斯更新。我们还提供了伴随压缩方案，并对多平台的沉浸式体验提供剩余补偿。它实现了约25倍的压缩率，每帧存储空间不到2MB。大量实验证明了我们方法的有效性，在优化速度、渲染质量和存储开销方面显著优于现有方法。  

### [5] GauHuman: Articulated Gaussian Splatting from Monocular Human Videos  
- **⏳发布**：2023-12-05  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Shoukang Hu, Ziwei Liu  
- **📝说明**：project page: https://skhu101.github.io/GauHuman/; code: https://github.com/skhu101/GauHuman  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.02973v1)    
- **📝摘要**：We present, GauHuman, a 3D human model with Gaussian Splatting for both fast training (1 ~ 2 minutes) and real-time rendering (up to 189 FPS), compared with existing NeRF-based implicit representation modelling frameworks demanding hours of training and seconds of rendering per frame. Specifically, GauHuman encodes Gaussian Splatting in the canonical space and transforms 3D Gaussians from canonical space to posed space with linear blend skinning (LBS), in which effective pose and LBS refinement modules are designed to learn fine details of 3D humans under negligible computational cost. Moreover, to enable fast optimization of GauHuman, we initialize and prune 3D Gaussians with 3D human prior, while splitting/cloning via KL divergence guidance, along with a novel merge operation for further speeding up. Extensive experiments on ZJU_Mocap and MonoCap datasets demonstrate that GauHuman achieves state-of-the-art performance quantitatively and qualitatively with fast training and real-time rendering speed. Notably, without sacrificing rendering quality, GauHuman can fast model the 3D human performer with ~13k 3D Gaussians.  
- **📝翻译**: 我们提出了GauHuman，这是一个采用高斯喷射的3D人类模型，支持快速训练（1~2分钟）和实时渲染（最高189帧/秒），相比现有基于NeRF的隐式表示建模框架，后者每帧需数小时训练和数秒渲染。具体来说，GauHuman 在正则空间中编码高斯喷溅，并通过线性混合蒙皮（LBS）将三维高斯从正则空间转换为有姿态空间，LBS 设计了有效姿态和 LBS 细化模块，以极低的计算成本学习三维人类的细节。此外，为了实现GauHuman的快速优化，我们用3D人类先验初始化和修剪3D高斯分布，同时通过KL散度引导进行拆分/克隆，并采用了新颖的合并操作以进一步加快速度。在ZJU_Mocap和MonoCap数据集上的广泛实验表明，GauHuman在快速训练和实时渲染速度下，在定量和定性上都实现了最先进的性能。值得注意的是，在不牺牲渲染质量的前提下，GauHuman 可以用 ~13k 3D 高斯快速建模 3D 人类表演者。  

### [6] SC-GS: Sparse-Controlled Gaussian Splatting for Editable Dynamic Scenes  
- **⏳发布**：2023-12-04（更新：2024-03-31）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Yi-Hua Huang, Yang-Tian Sun, Ziyi Yang et al.  
- **📝说明**：Code link: https://github.com/yihua7/SC-GS  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.14937v3)   · [Project](https://yihua7.github.io/SC-GS-web/)  
- **📝摘要**：Novel view synthesis for dynamic scenes is still a challenging problem in computer vision and graphics. Recently, Gaussian splatting has emerged as a robust technique to represent static scenes and enable high-quality and real-time novel view synthesis. Building upon this technique, we propose a new representation that explicitly decomposes the motion and appearance of dynamic scenes into sparse control points and dense Gaussians, respectively. Our key idea is to use sparse control points, significantly fewer in number than the Gaussians, to learn compact 6 DoF transformation bases, which can be locally interpolated through learned interpolation weights to yield the motion field of 3D Gaussians. We employ a deformation MLP to predict time-varying 6 DoF transformations for each control point, which reduces learning complexities, enhances learning abilities, and facilitates obtaining temporal and spatial coherent motion patterns. Then, we jointly learn the 3D Gaussians, the canonical space locations of control points, and the deformation MLP to reconstruct the appearance, geometry, and dynamics of 3D scenes. During learning, the location and number of control points are adaptively adjusted to accommodate varying motion complexities in different regions, and an ARAP loss following the principle of as rigid as possible is developed to enforce spatial continuity and local rigidity of learned motions. Finally, thanks to the explicit sparse motion representation and its decomposition from appearance, our method can enable user-controlled motion editing while retaining high-fidelity appearances. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach outperforms existing approaches on novel view synthesis with a high rendering speed and enables novel appearance-preserved motion editing applications. Project page: https://yihua7.github.io/SC-GS-web/  
- **📝翻译**: 动态场景的新颖视图合成仍然是计算机视觉和图形学中的一个挑战。近年来，高斯喷涂已成为一种稳健的技术，用于表示静态场景并实现高质量且实时的新颖视图合成。基于该技术，我们提出了一种新表示法，明确将动态场景的运动和外观分解为稀疏控制点和密集高斯分布。我们的核心思想是使用稀疏控制点，数量远少于高斯分布，来学习紧凑的6 DoF变换基底，这些基底可以通过学习的插值权重局部插值，得到三维高斯的运动场。我们采用变形MLP预测每个控制点时变的6 DoF变换，降低学习复杂度，增强学习能力，并促进获得时间和空间相干运动模式。随后，我们共同学习三维高斯分布、控制点的典范空间位置以及变形MLP，以重建三维场景的外观、几何和动力学。在学习过程中，控制点的位置和数量会被自适应调整，以适应不同区域不同的运动复杂性，并开发出遵循尽可能刚性原则的ARAP损失，以强制学习动作的空间连续性和局部刚性。最后，得益于显式的稀疏运动表现及其与外观的分解，我们的方法能够实现用户控制的动作编辑，同时保持高保真外观。大量实验表明，我们的方法在新颖视图合成方面表现优于现有方法，且具备高速渲染速度，并支持新颖的外观保留运动编辑应用。项目页面：https://yihua7.github.io/SC-GS-web/  


## November 2023

### [1] SparseGS: Sparse View Synthesis using 3D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2023-11-30（更新：2026-06-23）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Haolin Xiong, Sairisheek Muttukuru, Hanyuan Xiao et al.  
- **📝说明**：Version accepted to 3DV 2025. Project page: https://github.com/ForMyCat/SparseGS  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.00206v4)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has recently enabled real-time rendering of unbounded 3D scenes for novel view synthesis. However, this technique requires dense training views to accurately reconstruct 3D geometry. A limited number of input views will significantly degrade reconstruction quality, resulting in artifacts such as "floaters" and "background collapse" at unseen viewpoints. In this work, we introduce SparseGS, an efficient training pipeline designed to address the limitations of 3DGS in scenarios with sparse training views. SparseGS incorporates depth priors, novel depth rendering techniques, and a pruning heuristic to mitigate floater artifacts, alongside an Unseen Viewpoint Regularization module to alleviate background collapses. Our extensive evaluations on the Mip-NeRF360, LLFF, and DTU datasets demonstrate that SparseGS achieves high-quality reconstruction in both unbounded and forward-facing scenarios, with as few as 12 and 3 input images, respectively, while maintaining fast training and real-time rendering capabilities.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）最近实现了无界3D场景的实时渲染，实现了新颖视角合成。然而，该技术需要密集的训练视图来准确重建三维几何。有限的输入视图会显著降低重建质量，导致“漂浮物”和“背景坍塌”等伪影出现在看不见的视点。在本研究中，我们介绍了SparseGS，一条高效的训练流水线，旨在解决3DGS在稀疏训练视图场景中的局限性。SparseGS结合了深度先验、新颖的深度渲染技术和修剪启发式方法以减少浮动伪影，同时还配备了Unseen Viewpoint Regularization模块以缓解背景崩溃。我们对Mip-NeRF360、LLFF和DTU数据集的广泛评估表明，SparseGS在无界和前向场景下都能实现高质量重建，分别仅需12张和3张输入图像，同时保持快速训练和实时渲染能力。  

### [2] DynMF: Neural Motion Factorization for Real-time Dynamic View Synthesis with 3D Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2023-11-30（更新：2024-12-05）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Agelos Kratimenos, Jiahui Lei, Kostas Daniilidis  
- **📝说明**：Project page: https://agelosk.github.io/dynmf/  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.00112v2)    
- **📝摘要**：Accurately and efficiently modeling dynamic scenes and motions is considered so challenging a task due to temporal dynamics and motion complexity. To address these challenges, we propose DynMF, a compact and efficient representation that decomposes a dynamic scene into a few neural trajectories. We argue that the per-point motions of a dynamic scene can be decomposed into a small set of explicit or learned trajectories. Our carefully designed neural framework consisting of a tiny set of learned basis queried only in time allows for rendering speed similar to 3D Gaussian Splatting, surpassing 120 FPS, while at the same time, requiring only double the storage compared to static scenes. Our neural representation adequately constrains the inherently underconstrained motion field of a dynamic scene leading to effective and fast optimization. This is done by biding each point to motion coefficients that enforce the per-point sharing of basis trajectories. By carefully applying a sparsity loss to the motion coefficients, we are able to disentangle the motions that comprise the scene, independently control them, and generate novel motion combinations that have never been seen before. We can reach state-of-the-art render quality within just 5 minutes of training and in less than half an hour, we can synthesize novel views of dynamic scenes with superior photorealistic quality. Our representation is interpretable, efficient, and expressive enough to offer real-time view synthesis of complex dynamic scene motions, in monocular and multi-view scenarios.  
- **📝翻译**: 由于时间动力学和运动复杂性，准确高效地建模动态场景和运动被认为是极具挑战性的任务。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了DynMF，一种紧凑高效的表示方式，能够将动态场景分解为几个神经轨迹。我们认为动态场景的每点运动可以分解为一组显式或习得轨迹。我们精心设计的神经框架由一小组仅按时间查询的学习基底组成，渲染速度接近3D高斯喷射，超过120帧每秒，同时存储空间量仅为静态场景的两倍。我们的神经表征充分限制了动态场景中固有的不足约束运动场，从而实现高效且快速的优化。这通过对每个点的运动系数进行指令，从而强制基底轨迹的每点共享。通过对运动系数施加稀疏度损失，我们能够解开构成场景的运动，独立控制它们，并生成前所未有的运动组合。我们只需5分钟训练，就能达到最先进的渲染质量，半小时内就能合成出具有卓越写实品质的动态场景新颖视图。我们的表现方式足够可解释、高效且富有表现力，能够在单眼和多视角场景场景下实时合成复杂动态场景运动。  

### [3] Scaffold-GS: Structured 3D Gaussians for View-Adaptive Rendering  
- **⏳发布**：2023-11-30  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Tao Lu, Mulin Yu, Linning Xu et al.  
- **📝说明**：Project page: https://city-super.github.io/scaffold-gs/  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.00109v1)    
- **📝摘要**：Neural rendering methods have significantly advanced photo-realistic 3D scene rendering in various academic and industrial applications. The recent 3D Gaussian Splatting method has achieved the state-of-the-art rendering quality and speed combining the benefits of both primitive-based representations and volumetric representations. However, it often leads to heavily redundant Gaussians that try to fit every training view, neglecting the underlying scene geometry. Consequently, the resulting model becomes less robust to significant view changes, texture-less area and lighting effects. We introduce Scaffold-GS, which uses anchor points to distribute local 3D Gaussians, and predicts their attributes on-the-fly based on viewing direction and distance within the view frustum. Anchor growing and pruning strategies are developed based on the importance of neural Gaussians to reliably improve the scene coverage. We show that our method effectively reduces redundant Gaussians while delivering high-quality rendering. We also demonstrates an enhanced capability to accommodate scenes with varying levels-of-detail and view-dependent observations, without sacrificing the rendering speed.  
- **📝翻译**: 神经渲染方法在各种学术和工业应用中显著推动了逼真的3D场景渲染。最近的3D高斯喷溅方法实现了最先进的渲染质量和速度，结合了基于原始的表示和体积表示的优势。然而，这常常导致大量重复的高斯图，试图适应所有训练视图，忽视了底层的场景几何。因此，最终模型对显著视角变化、无纹理区域和光照效果的韧性降低。我们介绍了Scaffold-GS，它利用锚点分布局部三维高斯，并根据视锥内的视野方向和距离实时预测其属性。锚点生长和修剪策略基于神经高斯分布对可靠提升场景覆盖的重要性而开发。我们证明了该方法有效减少了冗余高斯分布，同时实现了高质量的渲染。我们还展示了增强的能力，能够适应不同细节层级和视角依赖观测的场景，同时不牺牲渲染速度。  

### [4] CompGS: Smaller and Faster Gaussian Splatting with Vector Quantization  
- **⏳发布**：2023-11-30（更新：2024-09-26）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：KL Navaneet, Kossar Pourahmadi Meibodi, Soroush Abbasi Koohpayegani, Hamed Pirsiavash  
- **📝说明**：Code is available at https://github.com/UCDvision/compact3d  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.18159v3)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) is a new method for modeling and rendering 3D radiance fields that achieves much faster learning and rendering time compared to SOTA NeRF methods. However, it comes with a drawback in the much larger storage demand compared to NeRF methods since it needs to store the parameters for several 3D Gaussians. We notice that many Gaussians may share similar parameters, so we introduce a simple vector quantization method based on K-means to quantize the Gaussian parameters while optimizing them. Then, we store the small codebook along with the index of the code for each Gaussian. We compress the indices further by sorting them and using a method similar to run-length encoding. Moreover, we use a simple regularizer to encourage zero opacity (invisible Gaussians) to reduce the storage and rendering time by a large factor through reducing the number of Gaussians. We do extensive experiments on standard benchmarks as well as an existing 3D dataset that is an order of magnitude larger than the standard benchmarks used in this field. We show that our simple yet effective method can reduce the storage cost for 3DGS by 40 to 50x and rendering time by 2 to 3x with a very small drop in the quality of rendered images.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷射（3DGS）是一种用于建模和渲染3D辐射场的新方法，相比SOTA NeRF方法，其学习和渲染时间要快得多。然而，它存在一个缺点，即相比NeRF方法，存储需求要大得多，因为需要存储多个三维高斯的参数。我们注意到许多高斯分布可能共享相似参数，因此引入基于K均值的简单向量量子化方法，在优化高斯参数的同时对它们进行量子化。然后，我们存储小码本以及每个高斯码的索引。我们通过排序索引并使用类似连续编码的方法进一步压缩索引。此外，我们使用简单正则化器来促进零不透明度（可见高斯），通过减少高斯数，大幅减少存储和渲染时间。我们对标准基准测试以及一个比该领域标准基准大一个数量级的现有三维数据集进行了大量实验。我们展示了，我们简单而有效的方法可以将3DGS的存储成本降低40到50倍，渲染时间减少2到3倍，同时渲染图像质量的下降幅度非常小。  

### [5] LightGaussian: Unbounded 3D Gaussian Compression with 15x Reduction and 200+ FPS  
- **⏳发布**：2023-11-28（更新：2024-11-12）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Zhiwen Fan, Kevin Wang, Kairun Wen et al.  
- **📝说明**：NeurIPS 2024, Project page: https://lightgaussian.github.io/  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.17245v6)    
- **📝摘要**：Recent advances in real-time neural rendering using point-based techniques have enabled broader adoption of 3D representations. However, foundational approaches like 3D Gaussian Splatting impose substantial storage overhead, as Structure-from-Motion (SfM) points can grow to millions, often requiring gigabyte-level disk space for a single unbounded scene. This growth presents scalability challenges and hinders splatting efficiency. To address this, we introduce LightGaussian, a method for transforming 3D Gaussians into a more compact format. Inspired by Network Pruning, LightGaussian identifies Gaussians with minimal global significance on scene reconstruction, and applies a pruning and recovery process to reduce redundancy while preserving visual quality. Knowledge distillation and pseudo-view augmentation then transfer spherical harmonic coefficients to a lower degree, yielding compact representations. Gaussian Vector Quantization, based on each Gaussian's global significance, further lowers bitwidth with minimal accuracy loss. LightGaussian achieves an average 15x compression rate while boosting FPS from 144 to 237 within the 3D-GS framework, enabling efficient complex scene representation on the Mip-NeRF 360 and Tank & Temple datasets. The proposed Gaussian pruning approach is also adaptable to other 3D representations (e.g., Scaffold-GS), demonstrating strong generalization capabilities.  
- **📝翻译**: 利用基于点的实时神经渲染技术的最新进展，推动了三维表示的更广泛应用。然而，像3D高斯喷溅这样的基础方法会带来巨大的存储开销，因为运动结构（SfM）点可以增长到数百万个点，单个无界场景通常需要GB级磁盘空间。这种增长带来了可扩展性的挑战，并阻碍了 Splat 的效率。为此，我们引入了光高斯，一种将三维高斯映射为更紧凑格式的方法。受网络剪枝启发，LightGaussian识别场景重建中具有极低全局意义的高斯分布，并采用剪枝和恢复工艺以减少冗余，同时保持视觉质量。知识蒸纯和伪视图增强随后将球面谐系数转移到较低程度，从而得到紧凑的表示。基于每个高斯分布的全局显著性的高斯矢量子化，进一步降低比特宽度，精度损失最小。LightGaussian在3D-GS框架内实现平均15倍压缩率，同时将帧率从144提升至237，从而在Mip-NeRF 360和Tank & Temple数据集上实现高效的复杂场景表示。所提出的高斯剪枝方法也适用于其他三维表示（如Scaffold-GS），展现出强大的泛化能力。  

### [6] HumanGaussian: Text-Driven 3D Human Generation with Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2023-11-28（更新：2024-03-14）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Xian Liu, Xiaohang Zhan, Jiaxiang Tang et al.  
- **📝说明**：Accepted by CVPR 2024, camera-ready version. Project Page: https://alvinliu0.github.io/projects/HumanGaussian  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.17061v2)   · [Project](https://alvinliu0.github.io/projects/HumanGaussian)  
- **📝摘要**：Realistic 3D human generation from text prompts is a desirable yet challenging task. Existing methods optimize 3D representations like mesh or neural fields via score distillation sampling (SDS), which suffers from inadequate fine details or excessive training time. In this paper, we propose an efficient yet effective framework, HumanGaussian, that generates high-quality 3D humans with fine-grained geometry and realistic appearance. Our key insight is that 3D Gaussian Splatting is an efficient renderer with periodic Gaussian shrinkage or growing, where such adaptive density control can be naturally guided by intrinsic human structures. Specifically, 1) we first propose a Structure-Aware SDS that simultaneously optimizes human appearance and geometry. The multi-modal score function from both RGB and depth space is leveraged to distill the Gaussian densification and pruning process. 2) Moreover, we devise an Annealed Negative Prompt Guidance by decomposing SDS into a noisier generative score and a cleaner classifier score, which well addresses the over-saturation issue. The floating artifacts are further eliminated based on Gaussian size in a prune-only phase to enhance generation smoothness. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superior efficiency and competitive quality of our framework, rendering vivid 3D humans under diverse scenarios. Project Page: https://alvinliu0.github.io/projects/HumanGaussian  
- **📝翻译**: 通过文本提示生成逼真的3D人类是一项既理想又具有挑战性的任务。现有方法通过分数蒸馏采样（SDS）优化网格或神经场等三维表示，但这在细节不足或训练时间过长方面存在问题。本文提出了一个高效且有效的框架——HumanGaussian，能够生成具有细粒度几何和真实外观的高质量三维人类。我们的关键见解是，3D高斯喷溅是一种高效的渲染器，具有周期性的高斯收缩或增长，这种自适应密度控制可以自然地由人类内在结构引导。具体来说，1）我们首先提出一种结构感知SDS，能够同时优化人体外观和几何形状。利用RGB和深度空间的多模评分函数，提取高斯密度化和修剪过程。2）此外，我们通过将SDS分解为噪声更大的生成评分和更干净的分类器评分，设计了退火负面提示指导，有效解决了过度饱和的问题。在仅修剪阶段，根据高斯尺寸进一步消除浮动伪影，以提升生成的平滑性。大量实验证明了我们框架的卓越效率和竞争力，能够在不同场景下呈现生动的三维人类。项目页面：https://alvinliu0.github.io/projects/HumanGaussian  

### [7] GS-IR: 3D Gaussian Splatting for Inverse Rendering  
- **⏳发布**：2023-11-26（更新：2024-03-28）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Zhihao Liang, Qi Zhang, Ying Feng, Ying Shan, Kui Jia  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.16473v3)    
- **📝摘要**：We propose GS-IR, a novel inverse rendering approach based on 3D Gaussian Splatting (GS) that leverages forward mapping volume rendering to achieve photorealistic novel view synthesis and relighting results. Unlike previous works that use implicit neural representations and volume rendering (e.g. NeRF), which suffer from low expressive power and high computational complexity, we extend GS, a top-performance representation for novel view synthesis, to estimate scene geometry, surface material, and environment illumination from multi-view images captured under unknown lighting conditions. There are two main problems when introducing GS to inverse rendering: 1) GS does not support producing plausible normal natively; 2) forward mapping (e.g. rasterization and splatting) cannot trace the occlusion like backward mapping (e.g. ray tracing). To address these challenges, our GS-IR proposes an efficient optimization scheme that incorporates a depth-derivation-based regularization for normal estimation and a baking-based occlusion to model indirect lighting. The flexible and expressive GS representation allows us to achieve fast and compact geometry reconstruction, photorealistic novel view synthesis, and effective physically-based rendering. We demonstrate the superiority of our method over baseline methods through qualitative and quantitative evaluations on various challenging scenes.  
- **📝翻译**: 我们提出了GS-IR，这是一种基于3D高斯喷溅（GS）的新型逆渲染方法，利用前向映射体积渲染实现逼真的新颖视角合成和重新光照效果。与以往使用隐式神经表征和体积渲染（如NeRF）且表现力低且计算复杂度较高的工作不同，我们扩展了GS这一用于新型视图合成的顶级表现，用于从未知光照条件下拍摄的多视图图像中估算场景几何、表面材质和环境光照。在将GS引入逆渲染时有两个主要问题：1）GS不支持原生生成合理的法线;2）前向映射（如光栅化和喷涂）无法像向后映射（如光线追踪）那样追踪遮挡。为应对这些挑战，我们的GS-IR提出了一种高效的优化方案，结合基于深度导数的正则化进行正常估计，并采用基于烘焙的遮挡来建模间接光照。灵活且富有表现力的GS表示使我们能够实现快速紧凑的几何重建、逼真的新颖视图合成以及有效的基于物理的渲染。我们通过对各种挑战性场景的定性和定量评估，证明了我们方法相对于基线方法的优越性。  

### [8] Compact 3D Gaussian Representation for Radiance Field  
- **⏳发布**：2023-11-22（更新：2024-02-15）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Joo Chan Lee, Daniel Rho, Xiangyu Sun, Jong Hwan Ko, Eunbyung Park  
- **📝说明**：Project page: http://maincold2.github.io/c3dgs/  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.13681v2)   · [Project](https://maincold2.github.io/c3dgs/.)  
- **📝摘要**：Neural Radiance Fields (NeRFs) have demonstrated remarkable potential in capturing complex 3D scenes with high fidelity. However, one persistent challenge that hinders the widespread adoption of NeRFs is the computational bottleneck due to the volumetric rendering. On the other hand, 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) has recently emerged as an alternative representation that leverages a 3D Gaussisan-based representation and adopts the rasterization pipeline to render the images rather than volumetric rendering, achieving very fast rendering speed and promising image quality. However, a significant drawback arises as 3DGS entails a substantial number of 3D Gaussians to maintain the high fidelity of the rendered images, which requires a large amount of memory and storage. To address this critical issue, we place a specific emphasis on two key objectives: reducing the number of Gaussian points without sacrificing performance and compressing the Gaussian attributes, such as view-dependent color and covariance. To this end, we propose a learnable mask strategy that significantly reduces the number of Gaussians while preserving high performance. In addition, we propose a compact but effective representation of view-dependent color by employing a grid-based neural field rather than relying on spherical harmonics. Finally, we learn codebooks to compactly represent the geometric attributes of Gaussian by vector quantization. With model compression techniques such as quantization and entropy coding, we consistently show over 25$\times$ reduced storage and enhanced rendering speed, while maintaining the quality of the scene representation, compared to 3DGS. Our work provides a comprehensive framework for 3D scene representation, achieving high performance, fast training, compactness, and real-time rendering. Our project page is available at https://maincold2.github.io/c3dgs/.  
- **📝翻译**: 神经辐射场（NeRFs）在高保真度捕捉复杂三维场景方面展现出显著潜力。然而，阻碍NeRF广泛采用的一个持续挑战是体积渲染带来的计算瓶颈。另一方面，3D高斯喷涂（3DGS）作为一种替代表示方式出现，利用基于3D高斯的表示，采用光栅化流水线来渲染图像，而非体积渲染，实现了极快的渲染速度和令人期待的画质。然而，3DGS需要大量3D高斯图以维持渲染图像的高保真度，这需要大量内存和存储空间。为解决这一关键问题，我们特别强调两个关键目标：在不牺牲性能的前提下减少高斯点数，以及压缩高斯属性，如视角依赖的颜色和协方差。为此，我们提出了一种可学习的掩膜策略，显著减少高斯分布数量，同时保持高性能。此外，我们提出了一种紧凑但有效的视角依赖色彩表示方法，采用基于网格的神经场，而非依赖球面谐波。最后，我们学习码本，通过向量量化紧凑地表示高斯的几何属性。通过量化和熵编码等模型压缩技术，我们持续显示出超过25美元/倍数的存储减少和提升渲染速度，同时保持场景表现质量，相较于3DGS。我们的工作为3D场景表示提供了全面的框架，实现高性能、快速训练、紧凑性和实时渲染。我们的项目页面可在 https://maincold2.github.io/c3dgs/ 访问。  

### [9] A Compact Dynamic 3D Gaussian Representation for Real-Time Dynamic View Synthesis  
- **⏳发布**：2023-11-21（更新：2024-07-04）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Kai Katsumata, Duc Minh Vo, Hideki Nakayama  
- **📝说明**：17 pages, 11 figures, ECCV 2024  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.12897v2)    
- **📝摘要**：3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has shown remarkable success in synthesizing novel views given multiple views of a static scene. Yet, 3DGS faces challenges when applied to dynamic scenes because 3D Gaussian parameters need to be updated per timestep, requiring a large amount of memory and at least a dozen observations per timestep. To address these limitations, we present a compact dynamic 3D Gaussian representation that models positions and rotations as functions of time with a few parameter approximations while keeping other properties of 3DGS including scale, color and opacity invariant. Our method can dramatically reduce memory usage and relax a strict multi-view assumption. In our experiments on monocular and multi-view scenarios, we show that our method not only matches state-of-the-art methods, often linked with slower rendering speeds, in terms of high rendering quality but also significantly surpasses them by achieving a rendering speed of $118$ frames per second (FPS) at a resolution of 1,352$\times$1,014 on a single GPU.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D高斯喷溅（3DGS）在综合静态场景多视图时展现出显著成功。然而，3DGS在应用于动态场景时面临挑战，因为3D高斯参数需在每个时间步更新，这需要大量内存和至少十几条观测值。为解决这些局限性，我们提出了一个紧凑的动态三维高斯表示，将位置和旋转建模为时间的函数，并采用少量参数近似，同时保持三维空间结构的其他属性，如缩放、颜色和不透明度不变。我们的方法可以显著减少内存使用，并放宽严格的多视图假设。在单目和多视角场景的实验中，我们证明了我们的方法不仅在高渲染质量上与最先进的方法相匹配，且在单一GPU上实现了每秒118美元（FPS）渲染速度，分辨率为1352美元/倍1014美元。  

### [10] Compressed 3D Gaussian Splatting for Accelerated Novel View Synthesis  
- **⏳发布**：2023-11-17（更新：2024-01-22）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Simon Niedermayr, Josef Stumpfegger, Rüdiger Westermann  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.02436v2)    
- **📝摘要**：Recently, high-fidelity scene reconstruction with an optimized 3D Gaussian splat representation has been introduced for novel view synthesis from sparse image sets. Making such representations suitable for applications like network streaming and rendering on low-power devices requires significantly reduced memory consumption as well as improved rendering efficiency. We propose a compressed 3D Gaussian splat representation that utilizes sensitivity-aware vector clustering with quantization-aware training to compress directional colors and Gaussian parameters. The learned codebooks have low bitrates and achieve a compression rate of up to $31\times$ on real-world scenes with only minimal degradation of visual quality. We demonstrate that the compressed splat representation can be efficiently rendered with hardware rasterization on lightweight GPUs at up to $4\times$ higher framerates than reported via an optimized GPU compute pipeline. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets demonstrate the robustness and rendering speed of the proposed approach.  
- **📝翻译**: 最近，采用优化的3D高斯簇状表示实现高精度场景重建技术被引入，用于从稀疏图像集进行新颖视图合成。使此类表示适用于网络流媒体和低功耗设备渲染等应用，需要显著减少内存消耗并提高渲染效率。我们提出了一种压缩三维高斯分布表示，利用敏感度感知向量聚类和量化感知训练来压缩方向颜色和高斯参数。学习的码本码率低，在真实场景中压缩率最高可达31美元，且视觉质量下降极小。我们证明，压缩后的 splat 表示可以通过硬件光栅化高效渲染，在轻量级 GPU 上以高达 4 美元/倍倍的帧率，比通过优化的 GPU 计算流水线报告的还要高。跨多个数据集的广泛实验展示了该方法的鲁棒性和渲染速度。  

### [11] Drivable 3D Gaussian Avatars  
- **⏳发布**：2023-11-14（更新：2025-02-10）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Wojciech Zielonka, Timur Bagautdinov, Shunsuke Saito et al.  
- **📝说明**：Accepted to 3DV25 Website: https://zielon.github.io/d3ga/  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.08581v2)    
- **📝摘要**：We present Drivable 3D Gaussian Avatars (D3GA), a multi-layered 3D controllable model for human bodies that utilizes 3D Gaussian primitives embedded into tetrahedral cages. The advantage of using cages compared to commonly employed linear blend skinning (LBS) is that primitives like 3D Gaussians are naturally re-oriented and their kernels are stretched via the deformation gradients of the encapsulating tetrahedron. Additional offsets are modeled for the tetrahedron vertices, effectively decoupling the low-dimensional driving poses from the extensive set of primitives to be rendered. This separation is achieved through the localized influence of each tetrahedron on 3D Gaussians, resulting in improved optimization. Using the cage-based deformation model, we introduce a compositional pipeline that decomposes an avatar into layers, such as garments, hands, or faces, improving the modeling of phenomena like garment sliding. These parts can be conditioned on different driving signals, such as keypoints for facial expressions or joint-angle vectors for garments and the body. Our experiments on two multi-view datasets with varied body shapes, clothes, and motions show higher-quality results. They surpass PSNR and SSIM metrics of other SOTA methods using the same data while offering greater flexibility and compactness.  
- **📝翻译**: 我们介绍可驱动的3D高斯化身（D3GA），这是一个多层次、可控的人体3D模型，利用嵌入四面体笼中的3D高斯原。使用笼状结构相较于常用的线性混合蒙皮（LBS）的优点在于，像三维高斯图这样的原件自然被重新定向，其核通过封装四面体的变形梯度被拉伸。为四面体顶点建模了额外的偏移，有效地将低维驱动姿态与待渲染的广义原件集解耦。这种分离通过每个四面体对三维高斯分布的局部影响实现，从而提升优化效果。利用基于笼子的变形模型，我们引入了一个合成流水线，将虚拟形象分解为多层，如服装、手或面部，从而提升了服装滑动等现象的建模。这些部件可以根据不同的驾驶信号进行条件，比如面部表情的关键点或服装和身体的关节角度矢量。我们在两个多视图数据集上进行的实验显示，这些数据集具有不同的体型、服装和动作，结果质量更高。它们在使用相同数据时，超越了其他SOTA方法的PSNR和SSIM指标，同时提供了更高的灵活性和紧凑性。  


## September 2023

### [1] DreamGaussian: Generative Gaussian Splatting for Efficient 3D Content Creation  
- **⏳发布**：2023-09-28（更新：2024-03-29）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Jiaxiang Tang, Jiawei Ren, Hang Zhou, Ziwei Liu, Gang Zeng  
- **📝说明**：Camera-ready version. Project page: https://dreamgaussian.github.io/  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2309.16653v2)    
- **📝摘要**：Recent advances in 3D content creation mostly leverage optimization-based 3D generation via score distillation sampling (SDS). Though promising results have been exhibited, these methods often suffer from slow per-sample optimization, limiting their practical usage. In this paper, we propose DreamGaussian, a novel 3D content generation framework that achieves both efficiency and quality simultaneously. Our key insight is to design a generative 3D Gaussian Splatting model with companioned mesh extraction and texture refinement in UV space. In contrast to the occupancy pruning used in Neural Radiance Fields, we demonstrate that the progressive densification of 3D Gaussians converges significantly faster for 3D generative tasks. To further enhance the texture quality and facilitate downstream applications, we introduce an efficient algorithm to convert 3D Gaussians into textured meshes and apply a fine-tuning stage to refine the details. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superior efficiency and competitive generation quality of our proposed approach. Notably, DreamGaussian produces high-quality textured meshes in just 2 minutes from a single-view image, achieving approximately 10 times acceleration compared to existing methods.  
- **📝翻译**: 3D内容创作的最新进展主要利用基于优化的3D生成，通过分数提取抽样（SDS）进行。尽管已有令人满意的结果，但这些方法常常存在每样本优化缓慢的问题，限制了其实际应用。本文提出了DreamGaussian，一种新颖的三维内容生成框架，能够同时实现效率和质量。我们的核心见解是设计一个生成式3D高斯喷溅模型，配合网格提取和UV空间中的纹理细化。与神经辐射场中使用的占用剪枝不同，我们证明了三维高斯分布的渐进密度化在三维生成任务中收敛速度显著加快。为了进一步提升纹理质量并便于后续应用，我们引入了一种高效的算法，将三维高斯量转换为纹理网格，并进行微调阶段以细化细节。大量实验证明了我们提出方法的卓越效率和竞争性产生质量。值得注意的是，DreamGaussian仅需2分钟即可从单视图生成高质量纹理网格，加速速度约为现有方法的10倍。  

### [2] Text-to-3D using Gaussian Splatting  
- **⏳发布**：2023-09-28（更新：2024-04-02）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Zilong Chen, Feng Wang, Yikai Wang, Huaping Liu  
- **📝说明**：To appear at CVPR 2024. Project page: https://gsgen3d.github.io. Code: https://github.com/gsgen3d/gsgen  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2309.16585v4)    
- **📝摘要**：Automatic text-to-3D generation that combines Score Distillation Sampling (SDS) with the optimization of volume rendering has achieved remarkable progress in synthesizing realistic 3D objects. Yet most existing text-to-3D methods by SDS and volume rendering suffer from inaccurate geometry, e.g., the Janus issue, since it is hard to explicitly integrate 3D priors into implicit 3D representations. Besides, it is usually time-consuming for them to generate elaborate 3D models with rich colors. In response, this paper proposes GSGEN, a novel method that adopts Gaussian Splatting, a recent state-of-the-art representation, to text-to-3D generation. GSGEN aims at generating high-quality 3D objects and addressing existing shortcomings by exploiting the explicit nature of Gaussian Splatting that enables the incorporation of 3D prior. Specifically, our method adopts a progressive optimization strategy, which includes a geometry optimization stage and an appearance refinement stage. In geometry optimization, a coarse representation is established under 3D point cloud diffusion prior along with the ordinary 2D SDS optimization, ensuring a sensible and 3D-consistent rough shape. Subsequently, the obtained Gaussians undergo an iterative appearance refinement to enrich texture details. In this stage, we increase the number of Gaussians by compactness-based densification to enhance continuity and improve fidelity. With these designs, our approach can generate 3D assets with delicate details and accurate geometry. Extensive evaluations demonstrate the effectiveness of our method, especially for capturing high-frequency components. Our code is available at https://github.com/gsgen3d/gsgen  
- **📝翻译**: 结合分数蒸馏采样（SDS）与体积渲染优化的自动文本转三维生成技术，在合成逼真三维对象方面取得了显著进展。然而，大多数现有的SDS和体积渲染的文本转三维方法存在几何不准确的问题，例如Janus问题，因为很难显式地将3D先验集成到隐式的3D表示中。此外，他们通常需要大量时间去生成色彩丰富的复杂3D模型。作为回应，本文提出了GSGEN方法，这是一种新颖的方法，采用了最新最先进的高斯喷溅表示法，用于文本到三维生成。GSGEN旨在通过利用高斯先验的显式特性，生成高质量的三维对象并解决现有不足。具体来说，我们的方法采用渐进式优化策略，包括几何优化阶段和外观细化阶段。在几何优化中，结合普通二维SDS优化，在三维点云扩散事先下建立粗略表示，确保其大致形状合理且三维一致性。随后，获得的高斯图会经过迭代外观细化以丰富纹理细节。在此阶段，我们通过基于紧致密度的密度增加高斯分布的数量，以增强连续性和忠实度。通过这些设计，我们的方法能够生成细节精致且几何准确的三维资产。广泛的评估证明了我们方法的有效性，尤其是在捕捉高频成分方面。我们的代码可在 https://github.com/gsgen3d/gsgen 获取  


## July 2022

### [1] 3DG-STFM: 3D Geometric Guided Student-Teacher Feature Matching  
- **⏳发布**：2022-07-06（更新：2022-07-19）  
- **🧑‍🔬作者**：Runyu Mao, Chen Bai, Yatong An, Fengqing Zhu, Cheng Lu  
- **📝说明**：  
- **🔗链接**：[arXiv Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2207.02375v2)    
- **📝摘要**：We tackle the essential task of finding dense visual correspondences between a pair of images. This is a challenging problem due to various factors such as poor texture, repetitive patterns, illumination variation, and motion blur in practical scenarios. In contrast to methods that use dense correspondence ground-truths as direct supervision for local feature matching training, we train 3DG-STFM: a multi-modal matching model (Teacher) to enforce the depth consistency under 3D dense correspondence supervision and transfer the knowledge to 2D unimodal matching model (Student). Both teacher and student models consist of two transformer-based matching modules that obtain dense correspondences in a coarse-to-fine manner. The teacher model guides the student model to learn RGB-induced depth information for the matching purpose on both coarse and fine branches. We also evaluate 3DG-STFM on a model compression task. To the best of our knowledge, 3DG-STFM is the first student-teacher learning method for the local feature matching task. The experiments show that our method outperforms state-of-the-art methods on indoor and outdoor camera pose estimations, and homography estimation problems. Code is available at: https://github.com/Ryan-prime/3DG-STFM.  
- **📝翻译**: 我们解决了寻找两张图像之间密集视觉对应关系的关键任务。这是一个具有挑战性的问题，主要由于各种因素，比如纹理不佳、重复图案、光照变化以及实际场景中的运动模糊。与使用密集对应基准作为局部特征匹配训练直接监督的方法不同，我们训练3DG-STFM：一个多模态匹配模型（Teacher），在三维密集对应监督下强制执行深度一致性，并将知识转移到二维单模匹配模型（Student）。教师和学生模型均由两个基于变换器的匹配模块组成，这些模块以粗到细的方式获得密集对应关系。教师模型引导学生模型学习RGB诱导的深度信息，以匹配粗枝和细枝条。我们还评估了3DG-STFM在模型压缩任务中的应用。据我们所知，3DG-STFM是首个用于局部特征匹配任务的师生学习方法。实验表明，我们的方法在室内外相机姿态估计以及同调估计问题上优于最先进的方法。代码可在：https://github.com/Ryan-prime/3DG-STFM 获取。
